SB 10.15

(10.15)

atha pañcadaśo’dhyāyaḥ

gocāraṇaṁ dhenukāsura-vadhaḥ kāliya-viṣa-dūṣitāmbu-pānān mṛtānāṁ gavāṁ gopānāṁ ca punar ujjīvanam |

|| 10.15.1 ||

śrī-śuka uvāca—

tataś ca paugaṇḍa-vayaḥ-śritau vraje

babhūvatus tau paśu-pāla-sammatau |

gāś cārayantau sakhibhiḥ samaṁ padair

vṛndāvanaṁ puṇyam atīva cakratuḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) :

tataḥ pañcadaśe dhenu-pālanaṁ dhenukārdanam |

kāliya-kṣveḍato gopa-rakṣaṇaṁ ca nirūpyate ||*||

ahi-vaktra-praveśena vṛthā khinnān sakhīn ataḥ |

kṛṣṇaḥ prāveśayat pakva-phalaṁ tālāli-kānanam ||**||

paśu-pāla-sammatau paśu-pālane sammatau, paśu-pālānāṁ vā sammatau | īṣad-vayo-balātirekam anukṛtavantāv ity arthaḥ | atīva cakratuḥ sarvataḥ prasarpaṇena ||1||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tato brahma-stuter anantaram | kṣveḍas tu garalaṁ viṣam ity amaraḥ ||*|| yato vṛthā khinnās tataḥ kāraṇāt | tālānām āliḥ paṅktir yatra kānane, tat tathā | tṛṇarājāhvayas tālaḥ ity amaraḥ ||**||

paugaṇḍaṁ daśamāvadhir ity ukteḥ | pañcama-varṣato daśamābdāntaṁ paugaṇḍam | paśu-pālane saṁmatasyāpi pālakāntarā-prītyā teṣu praveśo durghaṭa iti matvā samāsāntaram | ity artha iti— vayo-balābhāve paśūnāṁ nivartana-sthāpanādi na sambhavatīti bhāvaḥ | go-cāraṇasya prathama-dine kārttika-śuklāṣṭamīti pādme | tathā hi—

śuklāṣṭamī kārttike tu smṛtā gopāṣṭamī budhaiḥ |

tad-dinād vāsudevo’bhūd gopaḥ pūrvaṁ tu vatsapaḥ || iti ||1||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tataś ca iti kathā-rītyā vākyālaṅkāre | tad vā, tato vatsa-pālana-krīḍānantaram eveti madhye krīḍāntarādinā vilambo nirastaḥ | tv-arthe ca-kāro bhinnopakramāt | paugaṇḍa-vayasā pūrvato’dhika-rasa-prakaṭana-yogya-ṣaṣṭhābdārambha-kāle śritau sevitau santau | vraja iti paugaṇḍa-līlāyā anyatrābhāvaṁ bodhayan vraja-sambandhena tasyā mādhuryaṁ tatratyānāṁ ca bhāgya-bharaṁ sūcayati | evam agre’nyatrāpi jñeyam |

paśu-pālane sammatāv iti śrī-vṛndāvana-vihārārthaṁ sāgrajasya śrī-bhagavato go-pālanecchā ciraṁ jātāste, sā ca bālya-dṛṣṭyā śrī-nandādīnāṁ sneha-bhareṇa sammatā na syāt, adhunā ca yathā-kālaṁ kiñcid-vayo-balātireka-prakaṭanena sammatābhūd ity arthaḥ | yad vā, paśu-pālanāṁ sarveṣāṁ gopa-jātīnām | kiṁ vā, paśūnāṁ pālānāṁ ca sammatau santau gāś cārayantau babhūvatuḥ | śrī-bhagavatā pālitānāṁ mukta-stanyatvena mātṛ-saṅge militānām api taṁ tyaktum aśaknuvatāṁ vatsānāṁ tan-māṭṝṇāṁ ca tad-anugatena vṛṣādīnām api, sāhacaryeṇa nirudhyamānānām api sarveṣāṁ tasyāntike samāgamanāt | tena vinā vane paśūnām agamanāc ca | tatra ca dvitvaṁ sneha-bhareṇa śrī-baladevena sahābhedāt sahacaratvāc ca | gāś cārayantāv iti tayor gavaṁ eva pālane ukte’pi paśu-pāla-sammatāv iti tat-saṅge mahiṣy-ādīnām api sarva-paśūnāṁ pālanāpekṣayā |

sakhibhiḥ samam iti vatsapānām eva sarveṣāṁ tat-sahacarāṇāṁ go-pālane pravṛtteḥ | evaṁ tataḥ prabhṛti pūrva-go-pālana-kartāro go-pālanān nivṛttā iti jñeyam | śrī-bhagavato go-pālane pravṛttiḥ kārtika-śuklāṣṭamyām | tathā ca kārtika-māhātmye—

śuklāṣṭamī kārtike tu smṛtā gopāṣṭamī budhaiḥ |

tad-dinād vāsudevo’bhūd gopaḥ pūrvaṁ tu vatsapaḥ || iti ||

padaiḥ svāvatārārtha-rodanādi-prayatna-kṛc-chrī-dharaṇy-anugrahāya pādukādy-agrahaṇāt sākṣād-uditair vajrāṅkuśādy-asādhāraṇa-lakṣaṇa-yuktaiḥ śrī-pādābja-cihnaiḥ puṇyaṁ sākṣāt puṇya-rūpaṁ sundaraṁ vā | atīva iti svabhāvataḥ puṇyam api vṛndāvanam adhunā pūrvāpekṣayā sarvataḥ prasarpaṇenātyantaṁ cakratur ity arthaḥ | yad vā, śrī-vaikuṇṭhād ādhikyābhiprāyeṇa ||1||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tataḥ pañcama-varṣa-kīḍānantaraṁ tv-arthe ca-kāro bhinnopakramāt vraja iti pūrvavat evam agre'pi jñeyam | paśu-pāla-sammatāv iti samantataḥ śrī-vṛndāvana-vihārārtha sāgrajasya śrī-bhagavato go-pālanecchā ciraṁ jātāsti sā ca bālya-dṛṣṭayā śrī-nandādīnāṁ snehabhareṇa sammatā na syāt adhunā ca yathā-kālaṁ kiñcid-vayo-balātireka prakaṭane sammatābhūd ity arthaḥ | paśu-pālānāṁ vā sammatau iti vyākhyā tu tayoḥ paśu-pālana-prāvīṇya-sūcikā paṇḍita-sammata iti vat | kiṁ vā paśūnāṁ pālānāṁ ca sammatau santau śrī-bhagavatā pālitānāṁ mukta-stanyatvena mātṛ-saṅgemelitānām api taṁ tyaktam aśaknuvatāṁ vatsānāṁ tan mātṛṇāṁ ca, tad anugatvena vṛṣādīnām api sāhacaryeṇa nirudhyamānānām api sarveṣāṁ tasyāntike samāgamanāt, tena vinā vane paśūnām agamanāc ca tatra tāv iti | yugalatvena nirdeśāt snehabharadyotanā krīḍā sauṣṭhavārthā sakhibhiḥ samam iti tataḥ prabhṛti-pūrve go-pālanānivṛttā iti jñeyam |

ye khalu tataḥ pravayaso gopāḥ ity-ādau varṇitā iti jñeyam ayam-bhāvaḥ | pūrva sva-dharma-rūpeṇa go-cāraṇe tasmin putra-rūpasya pratinidher ayogyatvāt śrī-vrajeśvareṇa svayam eva go-cāraṇaṁ tatas tat-saṅgānurodhena tat-savayaskair eva sva-sva-go-cāraṇam adhunā tu śrī-kṛṣṇena tad ārambheṇa tat-saṅga-yogyas tat-sa-vayaskair eva tad iti etac ca kārttika-śuklāṣṭamyāṁ tathā ca pādme kārttika-māhātmye—

śuklāṣṭamī kārtike tu smṛtā gopāṣṭamī budhaiḥ |

tad-dinād vāsudevo’bhūd gopaḥ pūrvaṁ tu vatsapaḥ || iti |

padaiḥ tādṛśa-go-sevāyāḥ gopa-jāti-sva-dharmatvena pādukādy-agrahaṇāt sākṣād uditaiḥ śrī-pādābja-cihnaḥ puṇyaṁ puṇya-janaka sundaraṁ vā atīveti pūrvāpekṣayā [vaikuṇṭhādy apekṣayā] vā sarvataḥ prasarpaṇena ||1||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) :

dhenūnāṁ rakṣaṇe jyeṣṭha-stutiḥ svaiḥ saha khelanam |

dhenukasya vadho rakṣā viṣāt pañcadaśe gavām ||

tataḥ pañcama-varṣa-krīḍānantaraṁ paśūnāṁ pālane sammatau gopaiḥ sammatī-bhūtau | tad-dinaṁ tu pādme kārttika-māhātmye dṛṣṭam—

śuklāṣṭamī kārtike tu smṛtā gopāṣṭamī budhaiḥ |

tad dinād-vāsudevo'bhūd-gopaḥ pūrvaṁ tu vatsapaḥ || iti |

padaiḥ pada-cihnair dhvajādibhiḥ puṇyaṁ cāru atīva iti pūrvam ūnaviṁśati-cihnānāṁ caraṇayor laghutvād rekhāṇām atisūkṣmatvena spaṣṭībhāvāt ||1||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) :

dhenūnāṁ pālanaṁ pañca-daśe saṅkarṣaṇastuti |

svaiḥ krīḍādhenuka-vadhaṁ sastrāṇaṁ ca viṣato’bhavat ||

tataḥ pañcamābda-krīḍāntaraṁ paugaṇḍaṁ vayaḥ ṣāṣṭhyābdikīm avasthām āśṛitau vyañjayantyau tau śrī-bala-kṛṣṇau paśupāla-sammatau babhūvatuḥ | tad-dinaṁ tu gopāṣṭamīty uktam—

śuklāṣṭamī kārtike tu smṛtā gopāṣṭamī budhauḥ |

tad-dinād vāsudevo’bhūd jñeyaḥ pūrvaṁ tu vatsapaḥ || iti pādma-kārtika-māhātmye |

padair aṅghri-cihnair dhvaja-vajrādibhir vṛndāvanam atīva pūnyaṁ manojñaṁ cakratuḥ, idānīm aṅghri-cihnānāṁ pravyaktyā tatra śobhādhikyāt |

nanu, kṛṣṇasya gopasyāpi rāja-putratvāt go-cāraṇaṁ na yuktam iti cet tasya pareśatvena tad-aucityāt, sa hi yajña-puruṣaḥ mantrair haviṣā ca gavyena yajña-siddhir iti gopālanasya yajña-puruṣatā-sampādakatvāt ||1||

...

|| 10.15.2 ||

tan mādhavo veṇum udīrayan vṛto

gopair gṛṇadbhiḥ sva-yaśo balānvitaḥ |

paśūn puraskṛtya paśavyam āviśad

vihartu-kāmaḥ kusumākaraṁ vanam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : sva-yaśo gṛṇadbhir gopair vṛtas tad vanaṁ prāviśat ||2||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : balānvitaḥ sa-rāmaḥ | kusumākaraṁ puṣpa-khani-rūpam | tad-vanaṁ vṛndāvanam | śleṣeṇa mādhava iti vasantavat tad-ullāsaka ity arthaḥ ||2||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ sāmānyena dvayor api go-cāraṇādikam uddiśyādhunā viśeṣato vicitra-madhura-madhura-krīḍāṁ vakṣyan, tatra ca śrī-bhagavataḥ prādhānyaṁ dyotayan prathama-dina-krīḍām anyatrātideśārtham āha—tad ity-ādinā | tat śrī-vṛndāvanākhyam | kiṁ vā, suprasiddham anirvacanīya-māhātmyam vā | yad va, śrī-bādarāyaṇeḥ prema-bhareṇa smaraṇa-viśeṣāt sarvathārthaḥ sa eva |

mādhavo lakṣmī-kānta iti vṛndāvanasya sarva-sampad-vistāraṇābhiprāyeṇa | yad vā, nijāśeṣa-bhagavattā-prakaṭanārthaṁ madhu-vaṁśe’vatīrṇa iti tasya tad ucitam eveti bhāvaḥ | śleṣeṇa mādhavākhya-vasantasya praveśena yathā svata eva sarvaṁ vanaṁ puṣpādibhiḥ samṛddhaṁ syāt, tathā tasya praveśena tad iti | kiṁ vā, madhu madhura-rasaḥ, tena krīḍatīti mādhavaḥ, śrī-vṛndāvanasya madhura-rasopabhogārtham ity arthaḥ |

uccair īrayan, tad iti śrīmad adhara-pallava-mādhuryeṇaiva vādayan—tac ca śrī-vṛnḍavana-vartināṁ nija-praveśa-vijñāpanena praharṣaṇārtham | kiṁ vā, tad-antaḥ-praveśena svasyaiva harṣa-bharodayāt svasya | kiṁ vā, svaṁ nija-dhana-rūpaṁ yaśo gṛṇadbhiḥ kīrtayadbhr iti vihārārthaṁ tasya vihārecchayā nija-praharṣāt | paśūn iti saṅgināṁ mahiṣy-ādy-apekṣayā, āviveśa prītyāntaḥ praviveśa | śleṣeṇa paśu-pakṣi-vṛkṣādayas tatratyāḥ sarve śrī-kṛṣṇāviṣṭā babhūvur iti | kusumānām ākaram iti śarat-kāle bahula-puṣpa-sampatteḥ | kiṁ vā, śrī-vṛndāvanasya svabhāvata eva sadā sarva-puṣpa-samṛddheḥ, anena tathā paśavyam iti svata eva paśūnāṁ sukha-siddhyā tat-pālana-prayāsābhāvena ca, tathā gopair vṛta iti balānvita ity etābhyāṁ sukha-vihāra-sāmagrī darśitā ||2||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ sāmānyena dvayor api go-cāraṇādikam uddiśyādhunā viśeṣato vicitra-madhura-madhura-krīḍāṁ vakṣyan, tatra ca śrī-bhagavataḥ prādhānyaṁ dyotayan prathama-dina-krīḍām anyatrātideśārtham āha—tad ity-ādinā | tat śrī-vṛndāvanākhyam | kiṁ vā, suprasiddham anirvacanīya-māhātmyaṁ vā | yad vā, śrī-bādarāyaṇeḥ prema-bhareṇa smaraṇa-viśeṣāt sarvathārthaḥ sa eva |

mādhavo lakṣmī-kānta iti vṛndāvanasya sarva-sampad-vistāraṇābhiprāyeṇa | śleṣeṇa vasanta iva tad ullāsakaḥ uccaiḥ īrayan vādayan tac ca tad antaḥ-praveśena svasyaiva harṣodayāt | śrī-vṛndāvana-varttināṁ nija-praveśa-jñāpanena praharṣeṇotsukyāc ca svasya yaśo-gṛṇadbhir iti vihārārambhe teṣāṁ tat-premamaya-harṣabharodayaḥ sūcitaḥ | āviśat āviveśa protyāntaḥ praviveśa śleṣeṇa paśu-pakṣi-vṛkṣādayaḥ tatratyāḥ sarve śrī-kṛṣṇāviṣṭā vabhūvur ity arthaḥ | kūsumānām ākaram iti svabhāvata eva sadā sarva-puṣpa-samṛddheḥ anena tathā paśavyam iti svata eva paśūnāṁ sukha-siddhyā tat-pālana-prayāsābhāvena ca tathā gopair-vṛta iti balānvita ity etābhyāṁ sukha-vihāra-sāmagrī darśitā ata eva vihartu-kāma ity evoktam || 2||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : tad vanaṁ paśavyaṁ paśubhyo hitam ā samantād viveśa | mādhava iti śleṣeṇa vasanta iva tad-ullāsakaḥ ||2||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : tad vanaṁ mādhavaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ, śleṣeṇa vasanta iva tad-ullāsa-kṛt ||2||

...

|| 10.15.3 ||

tan mañju-ghoṣāli-mṛga-dvijākulaṁ

mahan-manaḥ-prakhya-payaḥ-sarasvatā |

vātena juṣṭaṁ śata-patra-gandhinā

nirīkṣya rantuṁ bhagavān mano dadhe ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tad vanaṁ nirīkṣya rantuṁ mano dadhe | kathaṁ-bhūtam ? mañju-ghoṣā ye’li-mṛga-dvijā bhramara-mṛga-pakṣiṇaḥ, tair ākulaṁ vyāptaṁ mahan-manaḥ-prakhya-payaḥ-sarasvatā mahatāṁ manasā prakhyaṁ tulyaṁ svacchaṁ payo yasmiṁs tat sara āśrayatvenāsti yasya, tena vātena iti śaityam uktam | śata-patra-gandhinā iti parimalavattvam | juṣṭaṁ vanam iti māndyaṁ sūcitam ||3||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : mañju-ghoṣā manohara-śabdāḥ | manojñaṁ mañju mañjulam ity amaraḥ | mahatāṁ hari-parāṇāṁ manasā prakhyaṁ tulyaṁ svacchaṁ payo jalaṁ, vimardotthe parimalo gandho jana-manohare ity amaraḥ | śatapatraṁ kuśeśayam ity amaraḥ | vanasya saghanatvād vāyor vegas tatra mandībhavatīti bhāvaḥ ||3||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : anyam api tāṁ varṇayan śrī-bhagavato vihārārambham āha—tad iti | mahānto bhagavad-bhaktās tan-manaḥ-prakhyatvenātyanta-svacchatvaṁ śrī-bhagavad vihāra-yogyatvaṁ coktam | juṣṭaṁ sevitam iti śleṣeṇa vāyor api sevakatayā vanasya māhātmyaṁ ca nirīkṣya sarvataḥ prasanna-dṛṣṭi-prasāraṇādinā dṛṣṭvā mano dadhe, prītyā mano’bhiniviṣṭaṁ cakre, prārabhatety arthaḥ | bhagavān iti nija-bhagavattāṁ darśayitum iti bhāvaḥ ||3||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : anyām api tāṁ varṇayan śrī-bhagavato vihārārambham āha—tad iti | mahānto bhagavad-bhaktās tan-manaḥ-prakhyatvenātyanta-svacchatvaṁ śrī-bhagavad-vihāra-yogyatvaṁ coktam | juṣṭaṁ sevitam iti, śleṣeṇa vāyor api sevakatayā vanasya māhātmyaṁ ca nirīkṣya sarvataḥ prasanna-dṛṣṭi-prasāraṇādinā dṛṣṭvā mano dadhe, prītyā mano'bhiniviṣṭaṁ cakre, prārabhatety arthaḥ | bhagavān iti nija-bhagavattāṁ darśayitum iti bhāvaḥ ||3||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : tat pañcendriyāhlādakaṁ vanaṁ nirīkṣya mañju-ghoṣā alayo mṛgā dvijāḥ pakṣiṇaś ca tair vyāptam iti vividhena sausvaryeṇa | śrotrasya vātena juṣṭaṁ sevitam iti vyañjitena māndyena | mahatāṁ manaḥ-prakhyaṁ manaḥ-sadṛśaṁ śītala-madhura-svacchaṁ payo yatra tat-sara āśrayatvenāsti yasya teneti | śaityena ca tvag-indriyasya mādhuryeṇa rasanāyāḥ | śatapatra-gandhinā iti saurabhyeṇa nāsāyāḥ śatapatrastha-saundaryeṇa netrasyāpy āhlādakam ||3||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : tad vanaṁ vīkṣya bhagavān tatra rantuṁ mano dadhe | kīdṛk ? mañju-ghoṣā alino mṛgā dvijāḥ pakṣiṇaś ca tair ākulaṁ vyāptam iti vividha-sukha-ravatvāt śrotrendriyasya vātena juṣṭaṁ sevitaṁ | kīdṛśena ? mahan-manaḥ-prakhyaṁ sādhu-citta-mayaṁ śītatva-madhura-svacchaṁ payo yatra tat saro yasyāśrayatvenāstīti, tena māndya-śaitya-dhāritvāt tvag-indriyasya, mādhuryeṇa rasanendriyasya, śata-patra-gandhinā iti saugandhyavattvāt ghrāṇendriyasya ca, śatapatra-saundarya-dhāritvāt dṛg-indriyasya ca, āhlādakam itīndriya-pañcakāhladādi vanaṁ sūcyate ||3||

...

|| 10.15.4 ||

sa tatra tatrāruṇa-pallava-śriyā

phala-prasūnoru-bhareṇa pādayoḥ |

spṛśac-chikhān vīkṣya vanaspatīn mudā

smayann ivāhāgra-jam ādi-puruṣaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : aruṇa-pallava-śriyā saha | spṛśantyaḥ śikhāḥ śākhāgrāṇi yeṣāṁ tān ||4||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : ādi-puruṣa ity anujatve’pi vastutas tad-ādir evety āha—smayann iva vismita iva | vanaspatīn atra bhakti-bhara-bhagna-grīvā narā yathā puṣpādinā hari-caraṇāravindam arcayanti, tathaiveme vṛkṣā iti dhvanitam ||4|| [avaśiṣṭaṁ viśvanāthasyānurūpam]

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : smayann ivāhāgrajam ity-ādi | ādi-puruṣatvena agraja iti mahān upapattiḥ, ata eva agrajaṁ smayann evāgrajam upalakṣī-kṛtya ||4||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tatra tatra sthāne sthāne sarvatraivety arthaḥ | śrī sampat | bharaḥ bhāraḥ | aruṇa-iti tair vyākhyātam | yad vā, tayā tena ca hetunā kṛtvā vā | yad vā, pādayor nija-aruṇa-pallavād api śriyā śobhayā hetun eveti parājayena parama-saundaryeṇa ca, spṛśac-chikhān iti bhaktyā sparśanaṁ bodhitam | tataś ca patrāṇām anuktiḥ phala-puṣpavad upādeyatvābhāvāt | evaṁ teṣāṁ teṣāṁ śraiṣṭhyena vanānāṁ patīn vanaspatīn mahā-vṛkṣān ity uktam | smayann iva iti svata eva sadā śrī-mukha-prasakteḥ [prasādāt] | yad vā, agrajam ivāha, vastutaḥ svatas tasminn eva tat-tad-varṇanasya paryavasānāt | ādi-puruṣa iti tasyāgrajatvāsambhave’pi krīḍā-viśeṣārtham agrajatayā prādurbhūtaṁ taṁ prati tathā vaktuṁ yujyata eveti bhāvaḥ | etad apy agre ādya-rati-viśeṣu cittollāsārthaṁ prathamam ekaṁ krīḍanam eva [pūrva-śloke] rantuṁ bhagavān mano dadhe [10.15.3] ity uktatvāt | yad vā, ādi-puruṣaḥ puruṣottama iti loka-śikṣaṇārtham agraja-sammānanaṁ tat-tad-vinoda-mādhuryādikaṁ ca sarvaṁ tasya yuktam eveti bhāvaḥ |

atredaṁ tattvam—vanaspatīnāṁ praṇati-viśeṣeṇa santuṣṭaḥ san tebhyas tad dātu-kāmo’pi sad-guṇa-gaṇa-vinamro lajjayā tan svasminn api teṣāṁ sevā-paratāṁ cānirdiśann agrajasya sammāna-miṣeṇa taṁ praty eva tāṁ varṇayan teṣāṁ tat-tad-guṇa-varṇana-dvārā prathamaṁ śrī-vṛndāvanāntaḥ-praveśe tatratyān sarvān eva prati prasāda-viśeṣam akarod iti ||4||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tatra tatra sthāne sthāne sarvatravety arthaḥ | śrīḥ sampat bharo bhāraḥ aruṇeti tair vyākhyātam | yad vā, tathā tena ca hetunā karaṇena vā spṛśac-chikhān eva śreṣṭhayena vanānāṁ patīn vanaspatīn mahā-vṛkṣān ity uktaṁ | yadyapi vānaspatyaḥ phaleḥ puṣpāt tair apuṣpād vanaspatiḥ ity amaroktyā vanaspati śabdena vṛkṣa-sāmānyaṁ nocyate tathāpi liṅga-samavāya-nyāyāt, tac-chabdena vānassatyā api gṛhyante | smayan iti narmadyotakaṁ kurvanti gopya ivety-ādau tat-prākaṭyāt tat-tad-iti-cāñcalya-krīḍopodvalakatvena evam ity-ādinā | vakṣyamāṇāc ca tac-caikātmyena sa-vayaskatayā saha sarvadā kroḍāparatvena bālye sakhyāṁśa-prābalyāt tathaivāgrajasya tadānīṁ gauṇatvam ālambyāha—agrajam iveti, agrajam api smayann iveti ca tathaivābhiprāyaḥ, darśayiṣyate ca tad-bhāva-dvaye kasyāpi kadācid udbhūtatva kvacit krīḍā-pariśrāntam ity-ādibhyāṁ ato'graja-bhāvāṁśa-sadbhāvena narma cedaṁ stutir īty aiva kṛtam |

nanv evañ cet śreṣṭhatvād agrajenaiva kathaṁ na tasmai narma nirmitaṁ tatrāha—ādir guṇādinā śreṣṭhaś cāsau puruṣaś ceti evaṁ kavinā tu sa-vinodaṁ tat-tan-narma-saṅgītamayī-stutir api tasminn eva paryāvasāyitā etad api kartavyeṣu ramaṇa-viśeṣeṣu cittollāsena prathamam ekaṁ krīḍanam eva rantuṁ mano dadhe ity uktatvāt | evaṁ sa-narma-vacanam api bhagavan-nirmitatvāt sarvaṁ yathāvad eva jñavam ||4||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : smayann iti narma-dyotakaṁ kurvanti gopya ivety-ādau tat-prākaṭyāt tat tad iti cāpalya-krīḍopodbalakatvena evam ity-ādinā vakṣyamāṇāc ca tac ca sa-vayaskatayā saha sarvadā krīḍā-paratvenaikātmyena sakhyāṁśa-bhāvāt tatraivāha—agrajam iva iti | atasmat tad-bhāvena narma cedaṁ stuti-rītyaiva kṛtam | evaṁ sa-narma-vacanam api bhāgavatatvāt sarvaṁ yathāvad eva jñeyam ||4||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : aruṇa-pallavānāṁ śrīḥ śobhā adho-mukhatvena pāda-sparśanāt phalānāṁ prasūnānāṁ ca uru-bhareṇa pādayoḥ spṛśantyaḥ śikhā yeṣāṁ, tān vanaspatīn vṛkṣān vilokya smayan smayamāna iti vivakṣitasya vanaspatīnām utkarṣasya paryavasānam svotkarṣa eva syāt, svotkarṣasya ca svayam ukty-anaucityāt | mudā ity ānanda-janitena gāmbhīryābhāvenoktyā vinā sthātum aśakteś ca | rāme sakhya-bhāvotthena smitenānena sva-mahotkarṣāropas tatraiva vyañjitaḥ | ata evāgrima-śloke ādi-puruṣa iti sva-nāmnāpi tasya sambodhanam kariṣyate iti mad-abhiprāyam imaṁ mad-agrajo mā budhyatām iti smita-nihnavavān, na tu smayann ity arthaḥ | tathā hi—

śrī-vṛndāvana-tad-vāsi-mādhuryolbaṇa-cetasā |

tat-stave hariṇā labdhe nijotkarṣāvasāyinam ||

tam ālocya tato rāmam apadiśya vyadhāyi saḥ |

ato’tra naiva tātparyaṁ rāmotkarṣānuvarṇane |

sakhya-bhāvāt tadā rāme narmaṇedam udīritam ||[la.bhā. 1.5.345-346] iti

bhāgavatāmṛtīyā-sārdha-kārikāḥ adi puruṣa iti tad anujatve’pi svayaṁ bhagavattvād ādiḥ ||4||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : tatra tatra paryaṭan sa nanda-sūnur ādi-puruṣo vanaspatīn vīkṣya smayann iva agrajam āha ity anvayaḥ | kīdṛśān ? aruṇānāṁ pallavānām adho-mukhānāṁ śriyā śobhayā saha phalādīnām uru-bhareṇa hetunā pādayoḥ spṛśantyaḥ śikhā yeṣāṁ, tān atra vanaspaty-utkarṣasya svotkarṣe paryavasānāt tasyotkarṣasya ca svasminn abhidhātum ayuktavād agrajaṁ vyapadiśyābhidhānaṁ na doṣāvahaṁ mudā smayann ivety atigāmbhīryam uktiṁ vinā sthātum aśaktiś ca vyajyate ||4||

...

|| 10.15.5 ||

śrī-bhagavān uvāca—

aho amī deva-varāmarārcitaṁ

pādāmbujaṁ te sumanaḥ-phalārhaṇam |

namanty upādāya śikhābhir ātmanas

tamo-'pahatyai taru-janma yat kṛtam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : taru-janma yena tamasā kṛtaṁ, tasya tamasaḥ pāpasya apahatyai nāśāya | athavā yena tvayā īśvareṇa sarvopakārakaṁ taru-janma kṛtaṁ taṁ tvāṁ namanti | evaṁ ślāghye’pi janmani yad ajñāna-rūpaṁ tamo’sti tasya apahatyai ||5||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tad evāha—yena pāpena | na hi pāpino bhagavad-dṛṣṭi-gocarī-bhavanti ity arucyārthāntaram āha—athavā iti | śrī-vṛndāvana-tarūṇām upāsanā-prāpta-druma-rūpāṇāṁ, vṛndāvane kim api gulma-latauṣadhīnām [bhā.pu. 10.14.34] ity-ādy-ukta-brahmādibhir iti prārthyamāna-janmanāṁ tamo’bhāvāt, loke sajātīya-tarūṇāṁ yat tamo-janyaṁ janma tat tamo'pahatyai iti vā ||5||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : adas-śabda-prayogeṇāha—aho amī ity-ādi | tamo'pahatyai tamo-nāśāya | yat yasmāt taru-janma vṛndāvaṇe kṛtam aṅgī-kṛtam | ātmana iti tṛtīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī | athavā, ātmanas taru-janma | yad vā, tarūṇām anyeṣāṁ janma yat kṛtaṁ, tasya tamaso'pahatyai ātma-sājātyād anyeṣāṁ tarūṇām api tamo-nāśakā vṛndāvana-tarava ity arthaḥ | kutaḥ ? ātmana ātma-jātīya-hetoḥ, na tu te tamo-janmānaḥ, teṣāṁ man-mayatvāt | tathā coktaṁ vaikuṇṭha-varṇane kaivalyam iva mūrtimat [bhā.pu. 3.15.16] ity evam uttara-śloke'pi ||5||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : aho iti praharṣe | sādara-sambodhane vāścarye vā | amī ime sthāvara-yonayo’pi | deva-vara ! he pūjya-śreṣṭha ! sarva-devottameti vā | ata eva sumanaḥ puṣpaṁ phalaṁ ca, tad eva arhaṇam arhyate’neneti pūjopakaraṇam | śikhābhir agra-bhāgaiḥ kṛtvā upa samīpe upaḍhokanatvena vā ādāya | sumana iti śleṣeṇa bhāva-rūpatā tasya dhvanitā | tamaso ’jñānasya apahatyai | amaraiḥ sattva-pradhānair arcitam ity anvayaḥ |

yad vā, aho bhrātaḥ ! deva-varaiḥ śrī-brahmādibhir amaraiś ca mṛtyur ahitair apy arccitam, tatra muktānām ajñānaṁ mokṣa-svīkārāt | yad vā, ātmanaḥ śikhābhir loke hi bhaktyaupahṭokana-dravyaṁ nija-śirasi kṛtvavān īyeta | sāmānyena taru-janma, sarva-sthāvara-jātiḥ, mahārytā sarva-sthāvaratvam eva prārthanāt, tasya kāraṇaṁ yat-tamaḥ duṣprārabdhaṁ tasyāpahatyai iti sāmānya-dṛṣṭyoktam—sthāvaratvena paśu-pakṣyādivat tvat-saṅge gamanāśaktar iti bhāvaḥ |

yad vā, ātmanas tamaḥ śokas tad apahatyai | nanu tat kutaḥ ? tatrāha—yad yasmāt tvayā taru-janma kṛtam | anyat samānam | ata ete sadā tvat-saṅgino bhavantv iti varo jñeyaḥ ||5||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : aho iti praharṣe āścarya vā, amī ime sthāvara-yonayo'pi he deva-vara ! sarva-devottama ! sumanasaḥ puṣpañ ca phalaṁ ca tad evārhaṇaṁ pūjopakaraṇam ātmanaḥ śikhābhir agra-bhāgaiḥ śleṣeṇa śirobhiḥ upādāya upādeyatvena gṛhītvā te tava pādāmbujaṁ namanti namas kurvantaḥ śikhābhir eva tava pādāmbuje samarpayantīty arthaḥ | kīdṛśaṁ pādāmbujam ? amarā brahmādi-devā muktāś ca tair apy arcitam |

nanu, kathaṁ sthāvarāṇām īdṛśaṁ jñānaṁ ? tatrāha—tama iti | yeṣām īdṛśo bhāvas teṣām ajñānaṁ nāsty eva pratyuta tamo'pahatyai paśyatāṁ śṛṇvatāṁ ca tamo-nāśāya yat yaḥ śrī-vṛndāvana-sambandhi taru-janma kṛtam aṅgī-kṛtam | yad vā, tamo'pahatyai paśu-pakṣyādivat tvat-saṅgamāsakti-duḥkha-nāśāya namanti ke te'mī yat yais taru-janma kṛtaṁ prārthitam ity arthaḥ | evaṁ nitya-siddhān prati-vivakṣitaṁ sādhana-siddhān prati tu ātmanas tamo'pahatyai tavāprāpti-duḥkha-nāśāya yad ity-ādi | yathā brahmaṇā prārthitam iti bhāvaḥ | evaṁ sarvatra pracāreṇa muhuḥ sarveṣām eva sukhaṁ kāryam iti svaira-vihārecchyā proktam || 5||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (kṛṣṇa-sandarbhaḥ 172) : ātmanaḥ sumanaḥ-phala-rūpam arhaṇam upādāya ātmana eva śikhābhir namanti | yad yaiḥ | śṛṇvatāṁ paśyatāṁ ca saṁsāriṇāṁ tamo’pahatyai taru-janmai-tat-kṛtam iti | yat-kṛtam iti tṛtīyā-tat-puruṣo vā ||5||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : aho iti ||5||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : atha vṛndāvanasya tarv-āder alaukikatāṁ sva-sevā-parāyaṇatāṁ ca darśayituṁ sva-mahima-kathane svasyānaucityam iti baladeva-vyapadeśenaiva tasya vṛndāvana-tarv-āder ātma-viṣayāṁ ratiṁ bhagavān prapañcayati—aho ity-ādi | smayann ivāhāgrajam ādi-puruṣaḥ [bhā.pu. 10.15.4] iti pūrvokta-śloka-paṭhitena ādi-puruṣa-śabdena svasyāgrajavattvābhāvāt agrajavattvaṁ svayam eva svīkṛtam ity āyātam | ataḥ smayann iva prahāsa-pūrvam iveti svāśrayasyārādhyatvasya tatrāropo’vagantavyaḥ | evam eva dhīrodātta-nāyaka-lakṣaṇāṁ yad ātma-ślāghā-parāṅmukhatā, tenātra asmac-chabda-prayoge kartavye yuṣmac-chabda-prayogaḥ |

tathā hi—aho āścarye | smayann ivāhāgrajam ādi-puruṣa iti yad āha, tasya vivṛtim | aho āścaryam | he devavara ! te tava pādāmbujam amī taravaḥ sumanaḥ-phalārhaṇam ādāya śikhābhir namanti | na phala-bhareṇa amī namrāḥ, api tu praṇāma-cikīrṣayaiva namra-śirasa ity arthaḥ | amī iti | ke te? ity āśaṅkyāha—yat yais tarubhis tamo’pahatyai draṣṭṝṇāṁ tamo-nāśāya ātmanas taru-janma kṛtaṁ gṛhītam | kādācitkaṁ taru-janma ? amarārcitam, amarair brahmādibhir apy arcitaṁ spṛhaṇīyam, kim apy aṭavyām [bhā.pu. 10.14.34] ity ukteḥ | etena vṛndāvana-tarūṇāṁ parama-bhāgavatatvam āyātam ||5||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : svīya-puṣpa-phalādibhiḥ sva-prabhoḥ śrī-kṛṣṇasya caraṇāv arcayāma iti mano'nulāpaṁ yuṣmākam ahaṁ jānāmīti sva-vijñatvaṁ parama-bhaktān śrī-vṛndāvanīya-vṛkṣān kaṭākṣeṇa jñāpayann agrajam āha—aho iti | śikhābhiḥ sva-sva-śirobhir upāyanaṁ tat-tad-upāya-padāmbujaṁ namanti iti bhaktyā śirobhir eva caraṇayos tat tad arpayantīty arthaḥ | kim-artham ? ātmanas tamaso’parādhasya apahatyai, yena aparādhena kṛtam utpāditaṁ taru-janma | "hanta asmābhir aparādha eva kaścit kṛtaḥ, yat kṛṣṇa-sannidhi-gamanāsamartham asmākaṁ taru-janma vidhātrā kṛtam" iti teṣām anurāgotthaṁ vacanam evānvavocad bhagavān | vastutas tu brahmādibhir api prārthyamānatvād vṛndāvanīya-taru-janma nāparādha-phalam iti jñeyam ||5||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : aho iti | he devavara ! amī taravaḥ sumanaḥ-phalārhaṇam ātmanaḥ śikhābhiḥ śirobhir upādāya tad-rūpam upāyanaṁ gṛhītvā amarair viriñcādibhir arcitaṁ te pādāmbujaṁ namanti | kim-artham ? asman-namaskāraṁ śṛṇvatāṁ tamaso’jñānasya apahatyai vināśāya yad yaḥ puṣpādibhs tat-pādārhaṇāya taru-janma kṛtaṁ svīkṛtaḥ | yad vā, tamasaḥ paśu-pakṣy-ādivat tvat-saṅgamāśakti-janya-duḥkhasyāpahatyai namanti yad yena te padāmbujena taru-janma kṛtam, na caiṣām ajñānaṁ śakyaṁ vaktuṁ tādṛg-bhāvavattvāt brahmādi-prārthitatvāc ca taj-janmanaḥ ||5||

...

|| 10.15.6 ||

ete’linas tava yaśo’khila-loka-tīrthaṁ

gāyanta ādi-puruṣānupadaṁ bhajante |

prāyo amī muni-gaṇā bhavadīya-mukhyā

gūḍhaṁ vane’pi na jahaty anaghātma-daivam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : he anagha ! vane gūḍham api tvāṁ na tyajanti | tvayi manuṣya-veṣeṇa nigūḍhe sati munayo’py ali-veṣeṇa nigūḍhās tvāṁ bhajantīty arthaḥ ||6||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : anagheti | gopa-veṣatve’py aparimitaiśvaryatvaṁ tava iti sambuddhy-abhiprāyaḥ | alino bhramarāḥ, bhramarāṇāṁ pakṣa-mūlam alam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ity ukteḥ | alam astu yeṣām ity alinaḥ | ity artha iti prakāśe prakāśaṁ gupte guptam iti nyāyena guptasya gupta-veṣeṇaiva bhajanam ucitam iti bhāvaḥ | vettā sarvasya vedyasya muniḥ sadbhir udāhṛtaḥ ity utpalaḥ | muni-gaṇā rahasya-līlā-gamanārthaṁ bhramarī-bhavanti | tena “bho bhramarāḥ ! mad-atirahasya-kuñjam api praviśyāsmat-saurabhyam āsvādayata | mā saṅkucata !” iti tān prati prasādo dhvanitaḥ |

akhila-lokānāṁ mukta-mumukṣv-ādīnāṁ tīrthaṁ sevyaṁ śodhakaṁ vā | yad vā, akhilo’nalpaḥ loko jñānaṁ yeṣāṁ, teṣāṁ sarvajñānām api sevyam | anaghānāṁ nirduḥkhānāṁ muktānām ātma-daivaṁ prāpyatvena niścitam | muktānāṁ paramā gatiḥ ity ukteḥ | duḥkhāno-vyasaneṣv agham ity amaraḥ | ādi-puruṣa-sarva-kāraṇa-kāraṇābhajane pātityaṁ mā bhūd ity arthaḥ | ātma-prabhavam īśvaram na bhajanty avajānanti sthānād bhraṣṭā patanty adhaḥ [bhā.pu. 11.5.33] ity ukteḥ | pūrvokta-muni-gaṇa-pakṣe’pi yojanāsyeti ||6||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : ete'lino munīnāṁ manana-śilānāṁ gaṇā bhavadīya-mukhyā pārṣada-pravarās tava yaśo gāyantī mama yaśo gāyantīty arthaḥ | loke ca parama-gabhīro dhīrodātta īśvaro bhrātaraṁ sakhāyam amātyaṁ vā lakṣī-kṛtyātmano mahimānaṁ prakaṭayati | ādi-puruṣasya mamānupathaṁ bhajante ity anenaivādi-puruṣa-śabdenaitad vyajyate | alināṁ tiryak-jātīnām api munitve pārṣadatve aprākṛtatvam | evaṁ tarūṇām api kutas tamaḥ ? ||6||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (dig-darṣinī) : [bṛ.bhā. 2.7.113] idānīṁ sthāvarebhyaḥ śreṣṭhānāṁ jaṅgamānāṁ madhye prathamaṁ kīṭānāṁ māhātmyaṁ pūrvavad bhagavān balarāma āha—ete alinaḥ [bhā.pu. 10.15.6] iti | bhramarā ime anupathaṁ pathi pathi bhajante anusaranti tvām | kiṁ kurvantaḥ ? tava yaśo gāyantaḥ | yad vā, anupathaṁ gāyanto bhajanta iti yojyam | sarvatraiva yaśo-gāna-lakṣaṇaṁ bhajanaṁ kurvantīty arthaḥ |

kīdṛśam ? akhilaṁ sampūrṇaṁ lokānāṁ bhuvanānāṁ janānāṁ vā jīvānāṁ tīrthaṁ sarva-loka-pāvitryāpyāyana-karāśeṣa-tīrtha-svarūpam ity arthaḥ | yad vā, adhikārānapekṣayā sarveṣām api lokānāṁ tīrthaṁ saṁsāra-tārakaṁ bhagavad-bhakti-mahima-jñāna-pradāyaka-guru-rūpaṁ vā | avivakṣitatvāt sandhy-abhāvaḥ |

amī muni-gaṇā eva | tatra ca nātmārāmāḥ, kintu bhavadīyā bhavad-bhaktāḥ | teṣv api mukhyā yato vane vanya-veśena gūḍham api nijeṣṭa-devaṁ tvāṁ na tyajanti | svayam apy ali-veśena nigūḍhāḥ santo gīta-priyaṁ tvāṁ gīta-gānena bhajantīty arthaḥ |

he ādi-puruṣeti tavādi-puruṣatvād ete'pi ādi-sevakāḥ | ata eva tval-līlānusāreṇa sarvadā tvāṁ bhajanti | tathā he anagha ! sarva-doṣa-rahita ! sarvāparādhāgrāhaka ! sarva-duḥkha-vimocaka ! iti vety anena tyāgānarhatayā anavasare bhajane cāparādha-bhayābhāvenāśeṣa-duḥkha-vimocakatvena ca kadācid api tvāṁ na tyajantīti yuktam eveti bhāvaḥ | prāya iti bāhulye | muni-gaṇaiḥ samaṁ gūḍham ity anena vā, na jahāti ity anena vā yojyam | tena ca kadācit kasyacit prema-viśeṣodayena gānādi-virāmād bhakta-vara-muni-gaṇebhyo'pi śraiṣṭhyam | kadācit pāramaiśvarya-prakaṭanaṁ vā rātry-ādau vicchedo vā sūcyate | yad vā, prāya iti vitarke |

tataś caitad uktaṁ bhavati—yathā parama-vaiṣṇavā munayo veda-vane nigūḍham api durbodha-līlatva-durlabhatvādināpi hetunā tvāṁ na tyajanti, kintu karma-jñānādi-mārgeṣu vartamānā api tatra tatra sāra-bhūtaṁ tvad-yaśa eva parama-sevyatvena gāyanto'ntaryāmi-rūpeṇa sarva-jīva-pūjyatama-parameśvara-rūpeṇa ca prakāśamānaṁ tvāṁ bhajante, sākṣād iva paśyanti | kiṁ vā, sarva-parityāgena kevalaṁ premṇā sevante tathaivaite alino'pi | ato nūnaṁ tatra reme ||113||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : eta iti śrīmad-aṅgulyā darśayati | akhilānām adhikārāna pekṣayā sarveṣām eva lokānāṁ jīvānāṁ tīrthaṁ saṁsāra-tārakaṁ bhagavad-bhakti-māhātmya-dyotaka-guru-rūpaṁ vā | anupathaṁ pathi pathi bhajante’nuvartante tvām | yad vā, anupathaṁ gāyanto yaśa eva santa ādi-puruṣasya śrī-brahmaṇo’nupathaṁ bhajante, svabhāvaṁ labhanta ity arthaḥ | sadā yaśo-gānād bhavadīyāś ca te | ato mukhyāś ca muni-gaṇā iti maunādi-lakṣaṇam ātmārāmatvam apāsya bhagavad-bhajane pravṛtteṣu mukhyā ity arthaḥ | evaṁ ślāghāmiṣeṇa varo'yam eva, prāpa iti keṣāñcit svataḥ prathamata eva bhaktatvāt | yad vā, prāyo gūḍham ity anvayaḥ | kadācid aiśvarya-prakaṭanāt prāyaḥ-śabdaḥ | na vidyate’gham aparādho bhaktānāṁ yasmin, teṣāṁ sarvāparādha-marṣaṇāt tat-sambodhanam | yad vā, anaghaṁ ca tam ātma-daivaṁ ceti | ayam atyāge hetuḥ | prāya iti vitarke vā | tataś cedam uktaṁ bhavati—yathā parama-vaiṣṇavā munayo veda-vacane nigūḍham api durbodha-līlatva-sulabhatvādināpi hetunā na tvāṁ tyajanti, kintu karma-jñānādi-mārgeṣu vartamānā api tatra tatra sāra-bhūtaṁ tvad-yaśa eva parama-śreyo-buddhyānukṣaṇaṁ gāyantaḥ sarva-parityāgena tvāṁ premṇā sevante, tathaivaite’lino’pi, ato nūnaṁ ta eveme iti |

yad vā, nanu, śrī-brahmaṇo’py atra kiñcin-mātra-janma-prārthanayā tasmād apy atratyānāṁ māhātmyenāmī muni-gaṇā iti na saṅgaccheta ? ata āha—bhavadīyeṣu mukhyā iti śrī-nāradādaya ity arthaḥ | ato munayo’py anugā yeṣāṁ te munīśvarā ity arthaḥ | anyat samam ||6||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : eta iti | śrīmad-agulyā darśayati | aviśeṣeṇākhila-lokānāṁ tīrthaṁ saṁsāramala-pāvanaṁ tvad bhakt-imāhātmyad yotaka-guru-rūpaṁ vā, anupathaṁ pathi pathi bhajante anuvartante tvām anupadam iti pāṭhe'pi tathaiva tac ca yuktam evety āha—he ādi-puruṣati | sadā svataḥ sarveṣāṁ tvat-sevakatvād iti bhāvaḥ | atrānu-mimīta iva prāya iti bhavadīyā bhavato nānā rūpasyopāsakā ye teṣv api pūrṇasya mad agraja-rūpasya bhavata upāsakatvān mukhyā ye munayaḥ parama-manana-niścitai-tad-rūpatvad bhajanena tata evānyatra mauna-śīlatvena cānanyā ity artha | teṣāṁ gaṇāḥ ata eva śleṣeṇa munayo'pi gaṇā anugā yeṣāṁ te munīśvarā ity arthaḥ |

śrī-brahmaṇāpi durlabhasya lābhāt te vane śrī-vṛndāvane gaḍham anya-rūpopāsakarair jñātam api atreva kvacit krīḍā-viśeṣāya nilīya-sthitam api ca na jahati tatra hetuḥ--ātma-daivam iti | bhavadīya-mukhyā iti ca anayoś ca mitho hetutvaṁ he anagha ! na vidyate bhaktānām aghaṁ yasmin saḥ | he aparādhāgrāhin ! parama-kāruṇiketi yāvat anaghātma-devam ity ekaṁ vā padaṁ tad evam eṣām abhīṣṭa-siddhiḥ kāryeti bhāvaḥ | prāya iti vitarke śrī-nāradādivat yaśo-gāna-parama-rahasya-tad anveṣaṇānugatyādi-sāmyāt ||6||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : bhavadīyā bhavato nānā-rūpasyopāsakā ye teṣv api pūrṇasya mad-agraja-rūpasya bhavata upāsakatvāt mukhyā ye munayas teṣāṁ gaṇā vane śrī-vṛndāvane gūḍham anya-rūpopāsakair avajñātam iti na jahati | he anagha ! aparādhāgrāhin ||6.7||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na kevalaṁ tarava evopāsate, bhramarāścety āha—ene’linas tava yaśa ity-ādi | taveti pūrvavad asmac chabda-prayoge yuṣmac chabdaḥ | ato’mī muni-gaṇā manana-śīlānāṁ bhāgavatānāṁ gaṇā manana-dharmatvād yaśo-gānasyāspaṣṭatā samucitaiva | bhavadīyānāṁ bhāgavatānāṁ mukhyā parama-bhāgavatā ity arthaḥ | he anagha ! vane gūḍham api ātma-daivaṁ na jahati, sahaiva yaśo gāyanto bhramantīty arthaḥ ||6||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : tatratyān jaṅgamān stauti dvābhyām | ete’lino bhramarāḥ anupathaṁ tvad-aṅga-saurabhānusāritvāt | vane kvacid rahasya-līlārthaṁ gūḍhaṁ sahacarādy-agamyam api tvāṁ na jahati na tyajanti | he anagha ! iti tatra gamane’py eṣāṁ tv aghaṁ tvaṁ na gṛhṇāsi | tasmād ete bhavadīya-mukhyā eva muni-gaṇā rahasya-līlā-manana-śīlā bhramarī-bhavanti, tena bho bhramarāḥ ! mad-atirahasya-kuñjam api praviśyāsmat-saurabhyam āsvādayata mā saṅkucateti tān prati prasādo dhvanitaḥ ||6||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : atha vṛndāvana-sthān jaṅgamān stauti—eta iti dvābhyām | he ādi-puruṣa ! ete alinaḥ tava yaśo gāyanto’nupatha tvad-aṅga-saurabhānusāreṇa tvāṁ bhajante | kvacid rahasya-līlāyai gūḍhaṁ sakhibhir agamyam api tvām ātma-daivaṁ na jahati tasmād amī bhavadīyeṣu mukhyā muni-gaṇās tvad-rahasya-manana-śīlāḥ śukādaya evaṁ bhaveyuḥ | he anagha ! tatra gamane’py eṣām aghaṁ na gṛhṇāsīti tatrānumatiś ca vyajyate ||6||

...

|| 10.15.7 ||

nṛtyanty amī śikhina īḍya mudā hariṇyaḥ

kurvanti gopya iva te priyam īkṣaṇena |

sūktaiś ca kokila-gaṇā gṛham āgatāya

dhanyā vanaukasa iyān hi satāṁ nisargaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : iyān hi satāṁ nisarga iti | yad asti svasmiṁs tad gṛham āgatāya mahate mahā-puruṣāya samarpayantīti ||7||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : satāṁ nisarga iti | nṛtya-saharṣāvaloka-priya-vacanair gṛhāgatasya sādhoḥ saṁmānanam iti satāṁ viduṣāṁ svābhāviko dharma ity arthaḥ | śikhino mayūrāḥ | yad vā, keśa-śikhā-dhāriṇaḥ | hariṇyo mṛgyaḥ | pakṣe, hariṁ nayanti prāpnuvanti iti hariṇyaḥ hari-bhaktāḥ | sūktair karṇa-priyair vacobhiḥ | pakṣe, ko brahmā, o viṣṇuḥ, u śivaḥ | teṣāṁ kilo vārtā yeṣāṁ te kokilā indrādayaḥ | tān gaṇayati pūjanārtham iti kokila-gaṇā yajñādi-karaṇa-śīlāḥ | vārtā-sambhāvyayoḥ kilaḥ iti śāśvataḥ | iyān etāvān | satāṁ sāstra-śraddhāvatām | nisargaḥ svabhāvaḥ | yad gṛhāgatāya atithaye sudṛṣṭi-suvaco-namanādinā pūjanam iti | anyathā,

atithir yasya bhagnāśo gṛhād yāti hy apūjitaḥ |

tasya pūrva-kṛtaṁ puṇyaṁ nāśam āyāti sarvataḥ || ity-ādi-smṛtir vyākupyeteti ||7||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na kevalam alinaḥ, amī śikhino'pi ity āha—nṛtyanty amī ity-adi | na kevalaṁ śikhino nṛtyanti, hariṇyaś ca īkṣaṇena priyaṁ kurvanti | kā iva ? gopikā iva | na kevalaṁ hariṇyaḥ, kokila-gaṇā api sūktair upaniṣat-prayaiḥ | ete hi veda-mūrtayaḥ ||7||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : īḍya ! he stuti-yogya ! iti lajjayā vimukhī-bhavantam agrajam abhimukhī-karoti | yad vā, īḍyāyāḥ ślāghyāyāḥ, mudā parama-harṣeṇety arthaḥ | asya sarvair apy anuṣaṅgaḥ | prema-nṛtya-kāriṇo bhavantv iti varo jñeyaḥ | īkṣaṇena prasanna-netra-dṛṣṭyā priyaṁ prīti-bhāvaṁ vā te tava gṛham āgatāya abhyāgatasya prītyai taṁ prati nija-nijottama-dravya-samarpaṇopapatteḥ |

yad vā, te tava gṛhaṁ praty āgatāya—bhāve niṣṭhā, sva-sva-gṛhe tavāgamanārtham ity arthaḥ | ata etā gopyo bhavantv iti varo jñeyaḥ, svabhāva-nirīkṣaṇāt, strītvāc ca |

yad vā, gopya iveti sarvatraiva dṛṣṭāntaḥ | evaṁ tāsāṁ tat-tad-akhila-sampattyā sarvebhyas tebhyo vaiśiṣṭyam, mayūrādīnāṁ ca paraṁ nṛtyādiṣv eva sauṣṭhavaṁ sūcitam | tataś ca sadā tvat-priya-rahasya-gīta-viśeṣa-gāyakā bhavantv iti varaḥ | dhanyāḥ parama-bhāgyavantaḥ | eṣām etad upayuktam evety āha—iyān iti | hi yataḥ | svabhāvata evaiṣāṁ parama-sādhutvād iti bhāvaḥ |

yad vā, dhanyāḥ parama-prema-dhana-yuktā bhavantv iti vṛndāvanīyān sarvān eva praty ayaṁ varaḥ | hi yasmāt, satāṁ prema-bhaktānām eveyān svabhāvo bhavatīti ||7||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : īḍya ! he stuti-yogyeti lajjayā smitvā vimukhī-bhavantam ivāgrajam abhimukhī-karoti mudety asya sarvair apy anuṣaṅgaḥ īkṣaṇena priyaṁ prīti bhāvaṁ te tubhyaṁ janayanti hacyarthānāṁ prīyamāṇaḥ [bhā.pu. 1.4.33] iti saṁpradānatvaṁ gopya iveti vīkṣaṇasya suṣṭhutayā premṇā ca sāmyāt dairgdya-cāñcalya-sa-prematvādinā tat smaraṇāc ca ata eva śrī-rāma-preyasyo'py anyā jñeyāḥ | itthaṁ paugaṇḍam ārabhya tāsu tasya bhāvodayaḥ sūcitaḥ | parama-tejasvitvena paugaṇḍa eva kaiśorāṁśāvirbhāvāt tāsām api tādṛśatvāt sūktaḥ śrotra-sukhada-śabdaḥ tattat kutaḥ ? gṛham āgatāya abhyāgatāyety arthaḥ | tac ca vāk caturthī ca sūnṛtā iti nyāyena yuktam evety āha—iyān iti ||7||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : gopya iva iti netra-guṇena tat-smaraṇāt ||7||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na kevalam alina eva eteṣīty āha—nṛtyanty amīty-ādi | amī śikhino mudā nṛtyanti | etena te'pi parama-bhāgavatāḥ unmādavan nṛtyati lāka-bāhyaḥ [bhāā.pu. 11.2.40] iti tal-lakṣaṇokteḥ iti | he īdya ! hariṇyo’pi īkṣaṇena te priyaṁ kurvanti | te iti pūrvavat | kā iva ? gopya iva | athavā, gopye nirjane sthale | iva evārthaḥ | etenaitā api parama-bhāgavāḥ | yat sarvatra tad-īkṣaṇam [bhā.pu. 7.5.55] ity ukteḥ | na kevalam etā eva | kokilā apīty āha—sūktair apīty ādi | kokila-gaṇā api sūktaiḥ priyaṁ kurvanti | etenaite’pi parama-bhāgavatāḥ—nirantara-stavanāt | gṛham āgatāya atithaya iveti | iva-śabda ubhayatra yojyaḥ | tad-arthe atithi-nimittam | vāk caturthī ca sūnṛtā ity ukteḥ | ato vanaukaso vṛndāvanaukaso dhanyā eva | tatra hetuḥ—iyān hi satāṁ vaiṣṇavānāṁ nisargaḥ svabhāvaḥ | etenāmī vaiṣṇavā evety arthaḥ ||7||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : te gṛham āgatāya tvāṁ gṛham āgataṁ sammānayituṁ sūktaiḥ priyaṁ kurvantīti pūrveṇaivānvayaḥ | iyān satāṁ nisarga iti nṛtya-saharṣāvalokana-priya-vacanair gṛhāgatasya sādhoḥ sammānam iti satāṁ svābhāviko dharma ity arthaḥ ||7||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : gṛham āgatāya te tubhyaṁ tvāṁ sat kartum ity arthaḥ | sūktaiḥ priyaṁ kurvantīti pūrveṇaiva sambandhaḥ | satāṁ nisargaḥ iyān sva-gṛhāgatasya sādhor nṛtya-saharṣa-vīkṣaṇa-priya-vacanaiḥ sammānanaṁ satāṁ svābhāviko dharma ity arthaḥ ||7||

...

|| 10.15.8 ||

dhanyeyam adya dharaṇī tṛṇa-vīrudhas tvat-

pāda-spṛśo druma-latāḥ karajābhimṛṣṭāḥ |

nadyo’drayaḥ khaga-mṛgāḥ sadayāvalokair

gopyo’ntareṇa bhujayor api yat-spṛhā śrīḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tṛṇa-vīrudhaś ca tava pādau spṛśantīti tathā | karajābhimṛṣṭā nakhaiḥ spṛṣṭāḥ | sadayair avalokanaiḥ | śrīr api yasmai spṛhayati kevalaṁ, tena bhujayor antareṇa vakṣasā gopyo dhanyā iti ||8||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : vakṣasā tat-sambandhena lakṣita-lakṣaṇayā bhujāntareṇa vakṣaḥ-saṁyogo lakṣyate | dhanyeyaṁ bhūmir dhanyāś cātratyāḥ padārthāḥ iti bhāvaḥ || [viśvanātha: yad vā, sann ayaḥ ity-āditaḥ] ||8||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na kevalam ete dhanyāḥ, dharaṇī cety āha—dhanyeyam adyeti | adya dharaṇī dhanyaiva, yatas tvat-pāda-spṛśa ity-ādi pūrvavat | druma-latāś ca karajābhimṛṣṭāḥ, ato dhanyāḥ | nadyo'drayaś cābhimṛṣṭā ity eka-deśenānuvartanīyam | khaga-mṛgāś ca sadayāvalokair dhanyāḥ | gopyo bhujayor antareṇa vakṣasā dhanyāḥ, svātmānaṁ [prati] sarveya-muktiḥ | tat-prakāśaḥ—yat spṛhā-śrīr iti vākyād yāni yāni spṛhayatīti yat-spṛhā, śrīs tu śrī-kṛṣṇa eva spṛhāvatī, tena tasya sevopakaraṇeṣv api tasyāḥ spṛhā ||8||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (dig-darṣinī) : [bṛ.bhā. 2.7.107] evaṁ sāmānyena vraja-bhūmes tad-vāsināṁ ca māhātmyaṁ saṅkīrtya, adhunā śrī-bhagavad-abhīṣṭottamataram eva balarāmaṁ matvā, śrī-parīkṣid ekenaiva ślokena sarveṣām eva teṣāṁ nāmādi-viśeṣeṇa saṁkṣepataḥ saubhāgyātiśayam agāyat | tathā hi—paugaṇḍe gopālana-līlayā vṛndāvane sarvataḥ sañcaran śrī-bhagavān tatratya-sad-guṇātirekeṇa parama-hṛṣṭo'tha tad-varṇanotsuka-sva-mahima-ślāghā-doṣa-dṛṣṭyā lajjayā cātmānaṁ parihṛtya, loke jyeṣṭha-saṁmānana-dharmaṁ pravartayann iva, nijāgrajaṁ śrī-balarāmaṁ lakṣīkṛtyāha—dhanyeyam [bhā.pu. 10.15.8] iti |

iyam anādi-kālato'nuvartamānā tadīya-vicitra-vibhūty-avatāra-gaṇa-vibhūṣitāpi dharaṇī pūrvaṁ tvayā varāha-rūpeṇoddhṛtā ramitā ca, sadā śeṣa-rūpeṇa śirasi dhāryamāṇāpi adyaiva dhanyā parama-saubhāgyavatī jātā | yad vā, bhakti-lakṣaṇa-dharmo dhanaṁ bhaktiś ca prema-lakṣaṇā | dharma iṣṭaṁ dhanaṁ puṁsām [bhā.pu. 11.19.39] iti | dharmo mad-bhaktikṛt proktaḥ [bhā.pu. 11.19.27] iti śrī-bhagavad-ukteḥ | tataś ca tucchīkṛta-catur-vargaka-prema-sampad-yutā babhūvety arthaḥ |

tad eva prapañcya darśayati—asyās tṛṇāni vīrudhaś ca gulmāni dhanyāḥ, na tu svargādi-sthitāḥ, yataḥ śrī-mathurāyām avatīrṇasya nanda-vraje gopālakasya satas tava pādau spṛśantīti tathā | etac ca dharaṇyās tṛṇa-vīrudhām api dhanyatve kāraṇam | adya ity asya sarvatraivānuṣaṅgaḥ | tataś ca śrī-raghunāthādi-rūpeṇa daṇḍakāraṇyādi-vihāre pāda-sparśaṁ prāptā api dharaṇy-ādayo'dyaiva dhanyā ity arthaḥ | yadi ca iyam iti padaṁ liṅga-vibhakti-vyatyayena dhanyetivad yathā-yogyaṁ sarvatrānuvartyeta, tadā imā iti sākṣād vartamānā aṅguli-nirdeśādinā darśyamānā vṛndāvana-gatās tṛṇādayo jñeyāḥ, etā eva dhanyā nānyatratyā ity arthaḥ |

tataś cādye'tipaugaṇḍa-vayo'tirekābhivyañjanena vicitra-gopāla-līlayā sarvataḥ sañcāreṇa kiñcid rasa-viśeṣāviṣkaraṇena ca pūrva-kālāpekṣayādhunā dhanyatvaṁ sampannam iti | drumā latāś ca phala-puṣpādi-grahaṇena bhūṣaṇādy-artha-patra-pallavādi-cchedanena ca tava karajair nakhair abhimṛṣṭāḥ saṁspṛṣṭāḥ santo dhanyāḥ | nadyaḥ śrī-yamunādyāḥ, adrayaḥ śrī-govardhanādayaḥ | khagāḥ mayūrādayaḥ, mṛgāś ca kṛṣṇasārādyāḥ | tava sadayair avalokair dhanyāḥ |

yadyapi dharaṇy-ādiṣu sarveṣv api parama-dayālos tasya sa-dayāvalokāḥ santy eva, tathāpi nady-adriṣu viśeṣatas tad uktir jala-pānāvagāhana-śikharārohaṇa-guhāśayanādi-vicitra-vihāra-sampatty-abhiprāyeṇa sambhavaty eva | yad vā, nadī-śreṣṭhāyāṁ priyatamāyāṁ śrī-yamunāyām, adri-rāje ca haridāsa-varye śrī-govardhane, tat-tat-sāhacaryeṇānya-nada-nadī-parvatādiṣv apy anugraha-viśeṣato ghaṭata eva | khaga-mṛgeṣu ca tat-taj-jāti-svabhāvena sadā bhagavac-caraṇāravinda-sannikarṣābhāvāt kevalaṁ kṛpā-dṛṣṭīnām eva prādhānyāt tathoktiḥ |

athavā, dayā-sahitair avalokair iti dayā-śabdena śikhaṇḍādi-grahaṇa-komala-kara-sparśa-madhurāhvānādi kāruṇya-ceṣṭitam api khaga-mṛgeṣūhyam | tathā yadyapi pāda-sparśo'pi druma-latā-nady-adriṣu sambhavati, tathāpi sundara-sal-lakṣaṇa-yuktāni pāda-cihnāni bhū-pradeśa eva samyag udayanta ity ato dharaṇyā eva viśeṣataḥ pāda-sparśa-saubhāgyaṁ sampadyate, na tathā anyad iti | prādhānyāt tāsu tasyaiva viśeṣoktiḥ | evam anyatrāpy ūhyam |

atha vicitra-madhura-līlayā pālyamānānāṁ gavādīnāṁ sahacarāṇāṁ ca gopānāṁ saubhāgya-varṇanaṁ krama-prāptam api sphuṭataratvād varṇayitvā dhanyatā-varṇanena hṛdayākrānta-parama-mahā-dhanya-gopī-gaṇa-premākṛṣṭa-cetāḥ sarvato'dhikatayā tadīya-saubhāgya-sampattīḥ saṅkīrtayati—gopya iti | iyam ity asyānuvṛtteḥ pūrvokta-rītyā imā gopya iti sadā hṛd-gatatvād aparokṣatā | tathoktiś ca māthura-vraja-varti-śrī-gopī-gaṇa-vyatirikta-gopālī-vyavacchedārthā | śrīr api yat-spṛhā yasmai spṛhayati kevalam ity arthaḥ |

tena bhujayor antareṇa śrī-vakṣasā gopyo dhanyāḥ dharaṇy-ādīnāṁ gopy-antānāṁ jāty-ādi-tāratamyena pāda-sparśādi-saubhāgya-lābhe pūrva-pūrvata uttarottareṣām ādhikyaṁ bodhavyam | ataḥ sarvebhyo gopīṣv evādhika-dhanyatā paryavasyati | yad vā, gopyo'pīti antareṇāpīti vā api-śabdenānvayaḥ | tad-balāc ca pāda-sparśana-karajābhimarśana-sadayāvalokair api viśeṣato vakṣasāliṅganādinā ca tā eva parama-mahā-dhanyā iti jñeyam | evaṁ sarvathā sarva-śraiṣṭhyam etāsāṁ svataḥ-siddham iti ||8||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ prathama-dṛṣṭāṇāṁ hṛdyatamānāṁ viśeṣato māhātmyam abhivarṇya punar harṣa-bhareṇa sāmānyataḥ sarveṣām api varṇayati | yad vā, kiṁ viśeṣa-varṇanena ? sarva evātratyā dhanyā ity āha—dhanyā iti | iyam ādito vartamānā vicitrāvatāra-bhūṣitā śrī-varāheṇoddhṛtya ramitā, śeṣa-rūpeṇa tvayā śirasi dhāryamāṇāpy adya śrī-mathurāyām avatīrṇe tvayi |

kiṁ vā, śrī-nanda-vraje paugaṇḍa-līlayā gopāle saty eva dhanyā parama-bhāgyavatī sarvottamābhūt | adyeiti sarvatrāgre’py anuvartyam | dhanyeyam ity api vacanādi-vyatyayena, tathā tvad iti samāsāntaḥ praviṣṭam api yathā-sthānam ākarṣaṇīyam | imāni tṛṇāni imā vīrudhaś ca gulminyo dhanyāḥ | na ca svargādi-vartinyo daṇḍakādi-sthitāś ca | tatra hetuḥ—tvat-pāda-spṛśaḥ | evam agre'pi vyākhyeyam |

dharaṇyā dhanyatve'py ayam eva vā hetur draṣṭavyaḥ—asādhāraṇa-sundara-padair goṣṭhādy akṣuṇṇa-pradeśeṣūditair alaṅkāra-viśeṣa-sampatteḥ | drumā latāś ca phala-puṣpādi-grahaṇena, bhūṣaṇādy-arthaṁ patrādi-cchedanena ca nakhaiḥ spṛṣṭāḥ, nadyaḥ śrī-yamunādyāḥ, adrayaḥ śrī-govardhanādayaḥ, khagā mayūrādayo, mṛgāḥ kṛṣṇasārādayaś ca, dūra-sthitāḥ sadayair avalokair dhanyāḥ | nady-adrīṇāṁ pādābja-sparśa-dhanyatā-sadbhāve'pi tathoktiḥ, prāyo dūra-vartitvāt, tathā dharaṇy-ādiṣu sarveṣu sadayāvaloka-sambhave'pi, tathā drumādiṣv api yathāyathaṁ pāda-sparśādi-sambhave'pi teṣu teṣu tat-tat-pradhānyāpekṣayā tathoktam | evam agre'pi | imāḥ śrī-nanda-vraja-vartinyaḥ | kiṁ vā, sadā hṛd-vartitvenāparokṣāḥ | bhujayor antareṇa āliṅganenety arthaḥ | anyat tair vyākhyātam |

yad vā, api-śabdaḥ samuccaye, bhujayor antareṇa sadayāvalokādibhiś ca tāsu dhanyatā-kāraṇānaṁ pāda-sparśanādīnāṁ sarveṣām api prādhānyena sad-bhāvād iti tāsāṁ sarvato'dhikatara-dhanyatoktā | śrī-vaikuṇṭheśasya vakṣasi nityam eva vasantī lakṣmīs tvad-vakṣasa eva spṛhayatīty asaṅgatam eva | tenāyam arthaḥ—kṛṣṇasyāsamordhvaṁ mādhurya-bharam avekṣya tasmai spṛhayantī śrīs tapaś cakāra | tac ca—yad-vāñchayā śrīr lalanācarat tapaḥ [bhā.pu. 10.16.36] iti nāga-patnībhir vakṣyate | ataḥ śriyo'py adhika-māhātmyaṁ vraja-sundarīṇāṁ suvyaktam eva ||8||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ tat-katṛka-sevayā tān stutvā śrī-rāma-katṛka-prasādenāpi dharaṇyādi-sahitān eva tān stauti—dhanyeti | iyam ādito vartamānā vicitrāvatāra-sparśa-saubhāgyavatī viśeṣataḥ śrī-varāha-śeṣa. prasādātiśaya-labdha-māhātmyāpi adya tvad-avatāra eva dhanyā para-praśaṁsanīyābhūt āstāṁ tāvad asyā dhanyātvaṁ tat saṁbhavānāṁ madhye ladhiṣṭhā imāḥ śrī-vṛndāva-vartinyaḥ tṛṇa-vīrudhas tṛṇa-rūpā latā dūrvādyā api dhanyāḥ yatas tvat-pāda-spṛśaḥ evam uttaratra ca dhanyeyam iti vacanaṁ liṅga-vyatyayenānuvartyaṁ tvad iti | chāndaso ḍaso luk | tato yathā-sthānam ākarṣaṇīyaṁ tathā drumā latāś ca karajair aṅgulibhiḥ kisalayādīnāṁ saukumārya-sparśāya bhūṣaṇādy-arthac-chedanāya vā spṛṣṭāḥ santaḥ mālaty adarśi vaḥ kaccit [bhā.pu. 10.30.8] ity-ādivat karajāḥ nakhā ity arthe | tu tair abhimarṣoṁ nāma nāvaratā-sūcakaḥ kisalayādau leno jñeyaḥ |

sa ca śrī-gopīnām uddīpanārthaḥ paśyatemā latāḥ ity-ādivat tathā etāḥ nadyaḥ ete'drayo'pi tvat-pāda-spṛśaḥ santa iti gamyaṁ yojyaṁ vā teṣu tasyaiva prādhānyāt nadyas tad ety-ādau gṛhṇanti | pādayugalam iti hantāyam adrir ity-ādau yad-rāma-kṛṣṇa-caraṇa-sparśa-pramoda iti vakṣyamāṇāc ca atha gopī-paryāyāṁ śyāma-sārivāṁ tahi kañcit-tad-vakṣo-lagnāṁ darśayan śleṣaṇāha, gopya iti | mat-pitṛvyād avatīrṇasya punar-mat-pitur dharmatāṁ prāptasya gopa-kanyā-pariṇayanam eva bhaviṣyatīti sūcayantya iti bhāvaḥ tad evam bhāvī | yas tasya priyātvaṁ prāpsyantībhiḥ kābhiś cid-gopībhiḥ saha vihāras tasya sūcanā kṛtā yat-spṛheti śrī-vaikuṇṭha-nātha-vakṣasthitā lakṣmīr api yat-spṛhety arthaḥ | na kevalaṁ spṛhā-mātraṁ kintu vakṣyate cānyann āgapatnībhiḥ yad-vāñchyā śrīr lalanācarat-ta paḥ iti evam apy atra śrī-gokule tad aprātiḥ śrī-gopīnām iva tad ananyatvād-bhāvāt tāsu tad adhikāriṇīṣv ananugatatvāc ceti bhāvaḥ | atra sarveṣāṁ sarveṣu satsv api tasya tasya prasādasya parama-kāṣṭhā-prātatvāt viśeṣoktir iti jñeyam ||8||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : gopya iveti netra-guṇena tat-smaraṇāt | atha gopī-paryāyāṁ śyāma-sārivāṁ tarhi kathañcid vakṣo-lagnāṁ darśayan—śleṣeṇa gopya iti | netra-guṇena tat-smaraṇāt rāma-priyābhiḥ kācid rāmasya bhāvi-vilāsa-sūcaneyam ||8||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : kiṁ ca, eteṣāṁ dhanyatā tu nitya-siddhaiva, aprākṛtatvāt | dharaṇy apy adya idānīṁ dhanyety āha—dhanyeyam ity-ādi | adya idānīm iyaṁ dharaṇī dhanyā | tṛṇa-vīrudhaś ca etās tṛṇa-vīrudho dharaṇī-sambandhinya eva | pūrvaṁ tu, aho amī deva-varāmarārcitam [bhā.pu. 10.15.5] ity-ādinā ya uktās taravas te’prākṛtāḥ | adyeti kālasya pārthakya-nirdeśāt |

tatrāyaṁ vicāraḥ—vaikuṇṭhaṁ tu trividhaṁ bhavati | bhaumam abhaumaṁ bhaumābhaumaṁ ceti | bhaumaṁ bhauma-svargavat puruṣottama-kṣetram, vapur bhūtaṁ mahātmanaḥ ity-ukteḥ | abhaumaṁ sva-yoga-śakty-ādhāram | bhaumābhaumaṁ vraja-vṛndāvanādi | sānnidhyaṁ nityadā hareḥ [bhā.pu. 10.1.28], yatra sannihito hariḥ ity-ādinā vihita-mahima-khyāpanāyāntarbhūtatvāt | bhaumatvaṁ,

tataḥ prabhṛti nandasya vrajaḥ sarva-samṛddhimān |

harer nivāsātma-guṇai ramākrīḍam abhūn nṛpa ||[bhā.pu. 10.5.18]

ity-ādinā harer nivāsasya parama-vaikuṇṭhasya bhāva-guṇaiḥ saha ramākrīḍam abhūt | sarva-samṛddhimān—aṣṭa-siddhi-nava-nidhibhiś ca samṛddha ity anenābhaumam | tenādyeti kāla-niyamaḥ | adya mad-avatāre yadyapy aṁśena sarvadaiva mama sānnidhyam eva, tathāpi paripūrṇa-bhāvenādyāvatīrṇo’smīti pūrṇa-bhāvena mal-loko’py avatīrṇa iti bhaumābhaumatva-siddhiḥ |

dhanyatve hetuḥ—tvat-pāda-spṛśaḥ | pūrvavad yuṣmac-chabda-prayogaḥ | druma-latā iti dharaṇī-sambandhitvāt punar upādānam | na kevalaṁ dharaṇy-ādaya eva, nady-ādayaś ca | nadyo yamunādyāḥ | adrayo govardhanādayaḥ | śrīr api yad bhāvāya spṛhayati | ataḥ śrī-śabdopādānād uktasya sarvasya svāśrayatvaṁ prakāśita-śriyaḥ saṅkarṣaṇa-paratve’prasiddhatvāt ||8||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : evaṁ tat-tat-kartṛka-sevayā tān stutvā śrī-rāma-kartṛka-prasādenāpi tān evānuraktair anyaiś ca sahitān stauti—dhanyeyaṁ dharaṇī adya ity avatāra-gata-sthūla-kālam ālambya ukti-vācakena padena tvat-svarūpa-varāha-śeṣa-sparśād api tvat-sparśo’syā atisukhada iti dyotitam | kuto dhanyeyam ? iti cet, dharaṇī-sthānāṁ tṛṇādīnām api tvat-samparkād evety āha—tṛṇāni ca vīrudhaś ca tās tvat-pādābhyāṁ spṛk sparśo yāsāṁ tathā-bhūtāḥ, yataḥ drumā latāś ca karajaiḥ puṣpa-troṭanārthaṁ nakharair abhimṛṣṭā spṛṣṭāḥ, yataḥ nady-ādayaś ca sa-kṛpāvalokaiḥ |

yad vā, san ayaḥ śubhāvaho vidhir yebhyas tathā-bhūtair avalokaiḥ sahitā yataḥ kiñcit sugandha-śītalāṁ gopī-paryāyāṁ śārivāṁ vallīṁ vakṣasi kautukena dhriyamāṇāṁ vilokyāha—gopyaḥ śyāma-vallyo’pi bhujayor antaraṁ vakṣas tena sahitā yataḥ śrīḥ śobhāpi yasmai spṛhayati, tathā hi bhāgavatāmṛtīyāḥ kārikāḥ—

sadā vakṣaḥ-sthalasthāpi vaikuṇṭheśitur indirā |

kṛṣṇoraḥ-spṛhayāsyaiva rūpaṁ vivṛṇute’dhikam ||

paurāṇikam upākhyānam atra saṅkṣipya likhyate ||

śrīḥ prekṣya kṛṣṇa-saundaryaṁ tatra lubdhācarat tapaḥ |

kurvatīṁ prāha tāṁ kṛṣṇaḥ kiṁ te tapasi kāraṇam ||

vijihīrṣe tvayā goṣṭhe gopī-rūpeti sābravīt |

tad durlabham iti proktā lakṣmīs taṁ punar abravīt ||

svarṇa-rekheva te nātha vastum icchāmi vakṣasi |

evam astv iti sā tasya tad-rūpā vakṣasi sthitā ||[la.bhā. 1.5.349-351] iti ||8||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : atha vṛndāvana-bhuvaṁ tatratyān anyāṁś ca balabhadra-prasāda-pātratvena stauti—dhanyeyam iti | iyaṁ dharaṇī vṛndāvana-bhūmir dhanyā ślāghyā adya iti, tvad-avatāra-varāha-sparśād api tvat-sparśo’syā atiharṣa-kṛd iti sūcyate | tad-bhavās tṛṇa-vīrudhas tṛṇa-rūpā latā-dūrvādyāś ca dhanyāḥ, yatas tvat-pāda-spṛśaḥ druma-latāś ca dhanyāḥ, yatas tvat-karajaiḥ puṣpa-troṭanārtham api tair abhimṛṣṭāḥ spṛṣṭāḥ nady-ādayaś ca san, ayaṁ śubhāvaho vidhiḥ yebhyas taiḥ sa-kṛpair vāvalokair dhanyāḥ marutā calantīṁ śyāmalatāṁ tad-vakṣo-lagnāṁ vīkṣyāha gopyaḥ śyāma-latāḥ śleṣeṇa vraja-kiśoryas tava bhujayor antareṇa vakṣasā dhanyāḥ | tad-anantaraṁ kīdṛśaṁ ? śrīr yat-spṛhā iti lakṣmīr yat spṛhayatīty arthaḥ | vakṣyate caivaṁ nāga-patnībhiḥ, yad-vāñchayā śrīr lalanācarat tapaḥ [bhā.pu. 10.16.36] ity-ādinā vraja-kiśorī-viṣayakaḥ pūrva-rāgo’tra padye nanda-sūnoḥ sūcitaḥ ||8||

...

|| 10.15.9 ||

śrī-śuka uvāca—

evaṁ vṛndāvanaṁ śrīmat kṛṣṇaḥ prīta-manāḥ paśūn |

reme sañcārayann adreḥ sarid-rodhaḥsu sānugaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : evaṁ śrīmad vṛndāvanaṁ prati prītaḥ sann adreḥ samīpa-varti-sarit-taṭeṣu paśūn sañcārayan sānugo gopaiḥ saha vartamānaḥ prīta-manā reme ||9||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : evam evaṁ-prakāram | adrer govardhanasya | yad vā, ittham agrajaṁ paritoṣya gopyo’ntreṇa bhujayor iti nijoktyaivoddīpta-kandarpas tat-saṅga eva gāḥ sakhīś ca niyujya, “bhoḥ śrīmad īkṣaṇam atra subalena sārdhaṁ govardhana-kandarā-rodhasi viśramyāgantāsmi tvam agre kālindī-rodhassu tāvad vihara” ity uktvā, tato viyujya paugaṇḍe’pi kaiśorāvirbhāvād rahasi vraja-bālābhiḥ sārdhaṁ reme ty āha—evam agrajaṁ stutvā tad-dvāraiva paśūn vṛndāvanaṁ sañcārayann adreḥ sarito mānasa-gaṅgāyā rodhassu reme ity anvayaḥ | śrīmatī vraja-yoṣin-mukhyā asyaiva prītā premavatā yasmin saḥ | kulāla-kartṛko ghaṭa itivat prītety asya viśeṣyatva-vivakṣayā para-nipātaḥ | ata eva prīta-manāḥ anugābhiḥ sakhibhiḥ sahitaḥ | vyākhyānasyāsya rahasyatvād etasyāvarakaṁ ratnasya kanaka-sampuṭam iva vyākhyāntaram avatārikāṁ vinaivāsti | tad yathā śrīmatī baladevādyāḥ prītā yasmin | sānugo’nugaiḥ sahitaḥ | anyat samānam ||9||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evam īdṛśaṁ varṇayann ity arthaḥ | yad vā, anena prakāreṇa, tat prati prītaḥ santuṣṭaḥ, kṛta-prāsāda ity arthaḥ ||9||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ sanarma-varṇanādi-prakāreṇa vṛndāvanaṁ vyāpya prītaḥ san kālādhva-bhāva-deśānām ity-ādinā karmatvam adreḥ śrī-govaddhanasya prītatva-prītamanas tvayoḥ sāmānya-viśeṣābhyāṁ bhedaḥ ||9||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : evaṁ sa-narma-varṇanādi-prakāreṇa vṛndāvanaṁ vyāpya prītaḥ san ||9-10||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : evam iti spaṣṭam | yad vā, ittham agrajaṁ paritoṣya gopyo’ntareṇa bhujayor iti nijoktyaivoddīpta-kandarpas tat-saṅga eva gāḥ sakhīṁś ca niyujya, “bhoḥ śrīmad-ārya ! kṣaṇam aham atra subalena sārdhaṁ govardhana-kandarā-rodhasi viśramyāgantāsmi | tvam agre kālindī-rodhaḥsu tāvad vihara” ity uktvā, tato viyujya paugaṇḍe’pi kaiśorāvirbhāvād rahasi vraja-bālābhiḥ sārdhaṁ reme ity āha—evam agrajaṁ stutvā, tad-dvāraiva paśūn vṛndāvanaṁ sañcārayan adreḥ sarito mānasa-gaṅgāyā rodhaḥsu reme ity anvayaḥ | śrīmatī vraja-yoṣin-mukhyā saiva prītā premavatī yasmin sa kulāla-kartṛko ghaṭa itivat prītety asya viśeṣyatva-vivakṣayā pūrve nipātaḥ | ata eva prīta-manāḥ anugābhiḥ sakhībhiḥ sahitaḥ vyākhyānasyāsya rahasyatvād etasyāvarakaṁ ratnasya kanaka-sampuṭam iva vyākhyāntaram avatārikāṁ vinaivāsti | tad yathā śrīmanto baladevādyāḥ prītā yasmin sa sānugaḥ anugaiḥ sahitaḥ | anyat samānam ||9-10||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : evam agraja-stuti-vidhayā vṛndāvanaṁ reme | akarmaka-dhātunā yogāt deśasya karmatvam | śrīmanto balabhadrādayaḥ prītāḥ yasmin saḥ anugair bhṛtyaiḥ sahitaḥ adrer giri-rājasya sarito mānasa-gaṅgāyā rodhaḥsu paśūn sañcārayan | rahasyārthaś cāyam—evam ukta-vidhayāgrajaṁ stutvā gopyāntareṇeti svokti-samuddīpita-smaro dhenur-mitrāṇi ca tasmin niyojya sa-subalo’ham adri-śobhāṁ vilokya āgacchāmīti nivedya tad-dvārā paśūn vṛndāvanaṁ sañcārayan | śrīmatī prītā premavatī yatra tādṛśaḥ | tata eva prīta-manāḥ sānugas tad-anugābhiḥ sahitaś cādreḥ sarid-rodhaḥsu reme iti ||9-10||

...

|| 10.15.10 ||

kvacid gāyati gāyatsu madāndhāliṣv anuvrataiḥ |

upagīyamāna-caritaḥ pathi saṅkarṣaṇānvitaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tām eva ratim āha—kvacid ity-ādi-daśabhiḥ ||10||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : yā vṛndāvane ratiḥ kṛtā, tām ity arthaḥ | anuvrataiḥ sakhibhiḥ | sragvī vanamālā-dharaḥ ||10||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : prīta-manaso ratim eva darśayati—kvacid ity-ādinā | pūrvaṁ kecid veṇūn vādayantaḥ [bhā.pu 10.12.7] ity-ādinā bālakānām eva prādhānyena tat-tat-krīḍoktā, idānīṁ tu prīta-manastvena sākṣac-chrī-kṛṣṇasyaiveti viśeṣaḥ | kvacit kasmiṁścit pathi | "sragvī" iti pāṭhe kasmiṁścit pradeśe kadācid iti vā | evam agre’pi |

madena śrī-vṛndāvana-puṣpa-rasasya pānajena śrī-bhagavat-sānnidhya-saubhāgyajena vā andheṣu sukha-viśeṣeṇa nimīlitākṣeṣu mahāmatteṣu vā, tādṛśeṣv aliṣv iti gāna-mādhuryam abhipretam | saṅkarṣaṇa-śabdaḥ śrī-bhagavatā saha tasyāpṛthaktayā gānābhiprāyeṇa, tathā coktam—yadūnām apṛthag-bhāvāt saṅkarṣaṇam uśanty api [bhā.pu 10.8.12] ity anuvratais tad eka-prīti-parair gopair upa samīpe, sarvoparitanatvena vā, gīyamānāni caritāni yasya | yad vā, upagīyamānam ali-gānād api uttamatayā saṁślāghyamānaṁ caritam ali-gānānukāra-lakṣaṇaṁ yasya saḥ | evam idaṁ viśeṣaṇam agre’pi padya-pañcakaṁ yāvad anuvartyam ||10||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : prīta-manaso ratiṁ darśayan vartamāna-prayogeṇa sādhāraṇa-dina-gatām evāha—kvacid ity-ādinā | pūrva kecid veṇūnvādayantaḥ ityā-dinā vālakānām eva prādhānyena tat-tat-krīḍoktā, idānīṁ tu prīta-manastvena sākṣāt śrī-kṛṣṇasyaiveti viśeṣaḥ, kvacit kasmiścit [pathi saṅkarṣaṇānvita iti vā pāṭhaḥ] pathi stragvīti pāṭhe kasmiṁścit pradeśe kadācid iti vā, evam agre'pi | madena śrī-vṛndāvana-puṣpa-rasa-pānajena śrī-bhagavat-sānnidhya-saubhāgyajena vā, andheṣu mahā-matteṣu tādṛśeṣv aliṣv iti gāna-mādhuryam abhipretaṁ saṅkarṣaṇa-śabdaḥ śrī-bhagavatā saha tasyāpṛthaktayā gānābhi-prāyeṇa anuvrataiḥ tad eka-prīti-paropair gopaiḥ atra bhramarāṇāṁ sva-jātīyasya svara-mātrasya gānaṁ śrī-bhagavatas tad anusāri-svarasya tad ucita-rāgasya ca anuvratānāṁ tu tayor gīta-baddha-tac-caritasya ceti mitho gānamelanaṁ jñeyam ||10||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : evaṁ prathama-gocāraṇādi-prasaṅge sādhāraṇa-dina-gatāṁ līlām āha—kvacid ity-ādinā ||10||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ, viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī, baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ vaiṣṇavānandinī) : pūrva ślokasya vyākhyā draṣṭavyā.

...

|| 10.15.11 ||

anujalpati jalpantaṁ kala-vākyaiḥ śukaṁ kvacit |

kvacit sa-valgu kūjantam anukūjati kokilam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā), vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ), śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī), jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : kala-vākyair iti śukād api uttama-jalpanaṁ bodhayati | evaṁ valgv iti ca śarac-cheṣe'pi kokila-kūjanaṁ sarvatrāśraya-śrī-vṛndāvana-svabhāvāt ||11||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ, viśvanātha-cakravartī sārārtha-darśiṇī, baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ vaiṣṇavānandinī) : na vyākhyātam.

...

|| 10.15.12 ||

kvacic ca kala-haṁsānām anukūjati kūjitam |

abhinṛtyati nṛtyantaṁ barhiṇaṁ hāsayan kvacit ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : barhiṇaṁ nṛtyantam anu abhinṛtyati | barhiṇam abhimukho nṛtyatīti vā | sakhīn hāsayan ||12||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : barhiṇaṁ mayūram abhi nṛtyati tat-tulyaṁ nṛtyati sarvato vā | mayūra-tulyatvaṁ pakṣair vināsambhavaṁ matvārthāntaram āha—abhimukha iti | hāsayan hāsaṁ janayan ||12||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : ca-śabdo valgv iti samuccinoti, arthas tathaiva barhiṇam abhi lakṣīkṛtya nṛtyati, tad-abhimukhaḥ san nṛtyati ity arthaḥ | ha sphuṭam | āsayan tam evopaveśayan | nirjitya nṛtyād uparamayann ity arthaḥ | yad va, "nāyaṁ sādhu nṛtyati" iti barhiṇam eva prati sakhīn hāsayan ||12||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : paśūn iti śleṣeṇa śrī-kṛṣṇa-pārśvato dūraṁ gatvā nirbuddhitvaṁ uktaṁ | yena pūrvavad-ātma-paścād eva tat sphuraṇāt dūra-gatva-jñānam api na jātam iti bhāvaḥ | megheti tad-garjitaṁ lakṣyate tadvad-gambhīrayeti mahā-puruṣa-svabhāvata eva ||12||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : barhiṇam abhi lakṣyīkṛtya nṛtyati paśūnāṁ dūra-gātma-paścād eva śrī-kṛṣṇa-gamana-sphūrteḥ sattvānāṁ madhye yo vyāghra-siṁhayoḥ bhītas tadvac cānurauti sma, teṣām īṣad-bhaya-darśana-kautukārtham iti bhāvaḥ ||10||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : barhiṇam abhi lakṣyīkṛtya nṛtyati | sakhīn hāsayan, barhiṇām eva rasollāsayan ||12||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : nṛtyantaṁ barhiṇam abhi nṛtyati, sakhīn hāsayan ||12||

...

|| 10.15.13 ||

megha-gambhīrayā vācā nāmabhir dūragān paśūn |

kvacid āhvayati prītyā go-gopāla-manojñayā ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : nāmabhiḥ prasūti-vāra-prayuktādi-lakṣaṇaiḥ—"he sūrye, he some !" yad vā, "he aśvini, he yamune, he pīte !" ity evaṁ-rūpaiḥ | gavāṁ gopālānāṁ manasaḥ priyayā ||14||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : paśūn go-vṛṣa-vatsataryādīn | yad va, ātmano gavādīn sarvān eva catuṣpado dūragān sataḥ paśūn iti | śleṣeṇa śrī-kṛṣṇa-pārśvato dūraṁ gatatvān nirbuddhitvam uktam | nāmabhir gaṅgā-yamunā-haṁsī-dhavalī ity-ādi-tat-tat-saṁjñābhiḥ prītyā āhvayati | ata eva gavāṁ teṣu prīti-viśeṣeṇa | kiṁ vā, upalakṣaṇam etat | sarveṣām eva paśūnāṁ ca manojñayā cittākarṣiṇyā vācā vacanenaiva, na tu vaṁśyā | katham-bhūtayā ? meghasya garjitavad gambhīrayā, sā ca mahā-purūṣa-svabhāvata eva | kiṁ vā, dūragāhvāne’tra tad-viśeṣāpekṣayā ||13||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : paśūn go-vṛṣa-vatsataryādīn | yad vā, ātmano gavādīn sarvān eva catuṣpado dūragān sataḥ paśūn iti śleṣeṇa śrī-kṛṣṇa-pārśvato dūraṁ gatatvān nirbuddhitvam uktam | nāmabhir gaṅgā-yamunā-haṁsī-dhavalī ity-ādi tat-tat-saṁjñābhiḥ prītyā āhvayati | ata eva gavām teṣu prīti-viśeṣeṇa | kiṁ vā, upalakṣaṇam etat | sarveṣām eva paśūnāṁ ca manojñayā cittākarṣiṇyā vācā vacaneneva, na tu vaśyā katham-bhūtayā ? meghasya garjitavad gambhīrayā, sā ca mahā-puruṣa-svabhāvata eva | kiṁ vā, dūragāhvāne'tra tad-viśeṣāpekṣayā ||13||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ, viśvanātha-cakravartī sārārtha-darśiṇī, baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ vaiṣṇavānandinī) : na vyākhyātam.

...

|| 10.15.14 ||

cakora-krauñca-cakrāhva- bhāradvājāṁś ca barhiṇaḥ |

anurauti sma sattvānāṁ bhītavad vyāghra-siṁhayoḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : cakorādīn anukṛtya rauti | kadācic ca sattvānāṁ madhye vyāghra-siṁhayos tābhyāṁ bhītavad bhavati | sattveṣu palāyamāneṣu palāyata ity arthaḥ | tayoḥ sattvānāṁ balodrekāṇāṁ bhītavad bhavatīti vā ||14||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : cakoro jīvaṁ-jīvaś candra-priyaḥ pakṣīḥ krauñco jala-caraḥ pakṣī, cakrāhvaḥ kokaḥ, bhāradvāja urddhavāścaṁcuś citritāṅgaḥ pakṣī | sattvānāṁ jīvānāṁ sattvaṁ jīve bale guṇe iti kośāt | vyāghra-siṁhayor iti pañcamy arthe ṣaṣṭhī | ity artha iti | sattveṣu mṛgādiṣu dhāvatsu svayam api tathaiva dhāvati na tu bhīta iti bhāvaḥ | anye'pi sadiyo bhaya-hetavo bahavaḥ saṁti dvayor evopādānaṁ katham ity asvarasād arthāntaram āha—tayor yāghra-siṁhayoḥ | sattvānāṁ sakāśād-bhītavad iti | yadyapy anye bhaya-hetavaḥ santi, na teṣv etayos tulyaṁ balam iti bhāvaḥ ||14|| atha viśvanāthaḥ.

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : kvacid ity anuvartata eva | cakoraś candrikāpāyī, krauñcaś ciñcoṭa-bhakṣī, cakrāhvaś cakravāko, bharadvāja eva bhāradvājaḥ, svarthe’ṇ, vyāghrāṭākhyaḥ pakṣī | cakorādīnāṁ kañcit kvacid anukṛtya rauti, sarva-śaktimattvād ity aiśvaryaṁ tatrāpi pūrvavad bodhavyam | bhītavad iti krīḍā-kautukena vati-pratyayas tayor hiṁsratvābhāvenāpi vastutas tābhyāṁ bhayābhāvāt | yad vā, yathānyaḥ kaścid bālako bhīto bhavet, tathaiva bhītaḥ syād ity arthaḥ, bālya-līlā-svabhāvāt | sma iti prasiddham evedam, nātra saṁśayaḥ kārya ity arthaḥ ||14||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : kvacid ity anuvartata eva | cakoraś candrikā pāyī, krauñcaś ciñcoṭabhakṣī, cakrāhvaś cakravāko bharadvāja eva bhāradvājaḥ—svārthe'ṇ, vyāghrāṭākhyaḥ pakṣī cakorādīnāṁ kañcit kvacid anukṛtya rauti, sarvān eva yugapad anukṛtya rauti sarva-śaktimattvāt ity aiśvarya tatrāpi pūrvavad bodhyaṁ bhītavad iti | krīḍā-kautukena vati-pratyayas tayor hinatvābhāvenāpi vastutas tābhyāṁ bhayābhāvāt smeti prasiddham evedaṁ nātra saṁśayaḥ kārya ity arthaḥ | atra teṣām uttara-pakṣe vyāghrādi-balātiśayānāṁ sambandhe bhītāyata ity arthaḥ | yad vā, sattvānāṁ madhye yo vyāghra-siṁhayoḥ sambandhe bhītaḥ tadvac cānurauti teṣām īṣad-bhaya-darśana-kautukārtham iti bhāvaḥ ||14||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : kintu sattvānāṁ prāṇināṁ madhye vyāghra-siṁhayoḥ śabdena bhītavad bhavati, sakhīṣu palāyamāneṣu svayam api palāyate | vastutas tu svasya svābhāvika-śauryeṇa bhayābhāvo vati-pratyayenoktaḥ ||14||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : cakorādīn anukaroti | katicic ca sattvānāṁ prāṇināṁ madhye vyāghra-siṁhayoḥ śabdena bhītavad bhavati | sattveṣu vidravatsu svayam api vidravati | vatinā bhayābhāvo darśitaḥ, svayam atiśūratvāt | tac ca śūrāṇāṁ sakhīnāṁ vidravaṇena hāso’yam iti bodhyam ||14||

...

|| 10.15.15 ||

kvacit krīḍā-pariśrāntaṁ gopotsaṅgopabarhaṇam |

svayaṁ viśramayaty āryaṁ pāda-saṁvāhanādibhiḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : āryam agrajam | viśramayati vigata-śramaṁ karoti ||15||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : gopasyotsaṅga upaviṣṭasya uru-jaṅghā-yoga upabarhaṇam upadhānaṁ śiraḥ-pīṭhaṁ yasya tam ||15||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : ādi-śabdād vījanādīni ||15||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : ādi-śabdād vījanādīni ||15||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : upabarhaṇaṁ śīrṣopadhānam ||15||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : upabarhaṇaṁ śīrṣopadhānaṁ viśramayaty āśramaṁ karoti ||15||

...

|| 10.15.16 ||

nṛtyato gāyataḥ kvāpi yudhyato valgato mithaḥ |

gṛhīta-hastau gopālān hasantau praśaśaṁsatuḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : mitho nṛtyādīn kurvato gopān praśaśaṁsatuḥ ||16||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : nṛtyata ity-ādīnāṁ caturṇāṁ dvitīyāntānāṁ gopālān ity anena sambandhaḥ | valgataḥ plavataḥ valga-plavane dhātuḥ | skhalanādi-viṣaye hasantau | praśaṁsatuḥ tuṣṭuvatuḥ ||16||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : kṛṣṇa-līlā-prasaṅga eva dvayor api yugapad evāha—nṛtyata iti | kvāpi praśaśaṁsatur valgato gati-viśeṣaṁ kurvato mitho’nyo’nyam āsaktyety arthaḥ | yad vā, asya yudhyata ity anenaiva pareṇa vānvayaḥ | anyat tair vyākhyātam |

yad vā, tau kvāpi nṛtyataḥ, kvāpi gāyata ity evaṁ kvāpīti sarvair api yojyam | kiṁ ca, kvāpi mitho gṛhīta-hastau kvāpi hasantau bhavataḥ | yad vā, pada-dvayam idaṁ viśeṣaṇatvena sarvatraiva yojyam | kvāpi gopālān praśaśaṁsatuḥ—"parama-vidagdhāḥ ! śūrāḥ ! susnigdhāḥ !” ity-ādi-ślāghāṁ cakratuḥ | kiṁ vā, nṛtyādinā praharṣodayena hasantau praśaśaṁsatuḥ | kiṁ vā, gopālān hasantau—"aho ! ime gānena gandharva-gaṇa-tiraskāriṇaḥ, nṛtyena vidyādhara-viḍambakāḥ, yuddhena trilokī-jitvarāḥ" ity-ādi-parihāsaṁ kurvantau praśaśaṁsatur eva, tattvato māhātmya-viśeṣa-khyāpanāt ||16||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : upasaṁhariṣyan viśrāma-krīḍāṁ vadan gopānāṁ saubhāgya-bharaṁ varṇayati, kvacid iti tribhiḥ | pallavety upalakṣaṇaṁ komala-nava-dala-koraka-puṣpāṇāṁ talpeṣu bahutvaṁ pṛthak pṛthak pañcaṣair militvā nirmitatvena bāhulyāt tataś ca bahutareṣv api teṣu teṣāṁ prītyai tat tad alakṣitas tat tat premodbodhitena nija-śakti-viśeṣaṇa bahu-rūpatayaiva śeta iti vijñāpayati—evam īśa-ceṣṭita iti vakṣyamāṇamaiśvaryam atrāpi saṅgataṁ syāt | niyuddhaṁ tair eva saha bāhu-yuddhaṁ tena śramaḥ śrī-gaṇḍādi-viṣayaka-mauktika-sundara-prasvedaka-ṇikodayādikaraḥ tena karśito durbala iva anena sakhīnām api tādṛśaṁ balavattva sūcitam, tathā cāgame gopaiḥ samāna-guṇaśīla-vayovilāsa-veṣaiḥ iti tad evam api teṣāṁ svayam asura-māraṇādau yad apravṛttis tatredaṁ paśyāma sarvasya vajasya tenāpi gaṇena śrī-kṛṣṇaika-sakhatārtha-līlā-śaktir eva teṣām udyamaṁ stabhayatīti gopeti te kiñcid ajyeṣṭhā jñeyāḥ | tatropabarhaṇa-racanaṁ ca tat-sukha-lābhāyaiva tena tat-kṛtaṁ kiṁ vā racitasyāpi tenaiva tad arthaṁ tyāgo jñeyaḥ ||16||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : hasantau kṛṣṇa-rāmau nṛtyādīn kurvato gopālān praśaṁsatuḥ ||16||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : hasantau rāma-keśavau mitho nṛtyādīn kurvato gopān praśaṁsatuḥ ||16||

...

|| 10.15.17 ||

kvacit pallava-talpeṣu niyuddha-śrama-karśitaḥ |

vṛkṣa-mūlāśrayaḥ śete gopotsaṅgopabarhaṇaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : niyuddhaṁ bāhu-grahaṇādinā dvandva-yuddham ||17||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (dig-darṣinī) : [bṛ.bhā. 2.7.123] atha gopa-vargāṇāṁ śrī-gopāla-deve prema-bhakti-māhātmyaṁ paugaṇḍa-līlāyāṁ go-pālana-samaye śrī-vṛndāvane vayasyair gopālaiḥ saha sarvataḥ gāś cārayataḥ sthāne sthāne vicitra-krīḍāṁ kurvato bhagavato viśrāma-līlāṁ śrī-śukadeva-pādā varṇayāmāsuḥ—kvacit pallava- [bhā.pu. 10.15.16] śloka-trayeṇa |

kvacit kadācit kasmiṁścit śītala-vāta-sevita-yamunā-tīrādi-sthāne niyuddhena malla-līlayā yaḥ śramaḥ, tena karṣito vyāpta iva vṛkṣa-mūlaṁ sahaja-niviḍa-susnigdha-cchāya-kadamba-pādapa-talam āśrayo viśrāma-sthalaṁ yasya tathā-bhūtaḥ san | kvacit pūrvam eva śrī-vṛndā-devyā viracayya sthāpiteṣu | kiṁ vā, tat-kālam eva priya-sakha-gaṇair viraciteṣu pallavety upalakṣaṇaṁ pallava-patra-puṣpāṇām abhinava-komalānāṁ talpeṣu | bahubhiḥ racitatvād gauraveṇa bahu-vacanam | yad vā, paraspara-prīti-spardhayā sarvair ekaikaśyena viraciteṣu bahuleṣu teṣu sarveṣāṁ teṣāṁ prītyai viśva-rūpeṇa tad-alakṣitaḥ śete ity arthaḥ | gopasya kasyacit pradhānatvāt śrī-rādhā-bhrātuḥ śrīdāmna utsaṅgaḥ kroḍa eva upabarhaṇam upadhānaṁ yasya saḥ ||17||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : upasaṁhariṣyan viśrāma-krīḍāṁ vadan gopānāṁ saubhāgya-bharaṁ varṇayati—kvacid iti tribhiḥ | pallava- ity upalakṣaṇaṁ komala-nava-koraka-puṣpāṇāṁ talpeṣu | kiṁ vā, avilambārthaṁ kevalaṁ pallavair eva raciteṣu talpeṣu | tatra ca tadānīm eva prema-sambhrameṇa tvarayā bahubhir vayasyair viracitatvād gauraveṇa bahutvam | kiṁ vā, pratyekaṁ prema-dakṣaiḥ sarvair eva tair ekaikaśo nirmitatvena bāhulyāt | tataś ca bahutareṣv api teṣu teṣāṁ prītyai tad-alakṣito nija-śakti-viśeṣeṇa bahu-rūpatayaiva śeta iti jñeyam | evam īśa-ceṣṭitaḥ [bhā.pu. 10.15.20] iti vakṣyamāṇam aiśvaryam atrāpi saṅgataṁ syāt |

niyuddhaṁ tair eva saha bāhu-yuddhākhya-malla-līlā, tena śramo mauktika-sundara-śrī-gaṇḍādi-viṣayaka-prasveda-kaṇikodayādi-tal-lakṣaṇa-svīkārāt, tena karśitaḥ khinna iva | gopaḥ śrī-rādhā-devyāḥ bhrātā śrīdāma-nāmā priya-sakhaḥ, tasya utsaṅgaḥ krīḍam eva upabarhaṇam upadhānaṁ yasya saḥ | evaṁ śrīdāmno’pi tadvat tat tatra bahutvaṁ jñeyam | yad vā, gopānām utsaṅgopabarhaṇa iti kutrāpi kasyacid ity eva bodhyam | tac ca talpāṅgatvena yasya kasyāpy upabarhaṇa-sthas tair nijotsaṅgopadhānatā-sukhārtham avacitatvāt | kiṁ vā, racitasyāpi tenaiva tad-arthaṁ tyāgāt ||17||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : upasaṁhariṣyan viśrāma-krīḍāṁ vadan gopānāṁ saubhāgya-bharaṁ varṇayati—kvacid iti tribhiḥ | pallava-ity upalakṣaṇaṁ komala-nava-dala-koraka-puṣpāṇāṁ talpeṣu | bahutvaṁ pṛthak pṛthak pañcaṣair militvā nirmitatvena bāhulyāt | tataś ca bahutareṣv api teṣu teṣāṁ prītyai tat-tad-alakṣitas tat-tat-premodbodhitena nija-śakti-viśeṣeṇa bahu-rūpatayaiva śeta iti vijñāpayati—evam īśa-ceṣṭitaḥ [bhā.pu. 10.15.20] iti vakṣyamāṇam aiśvaryam atrāpi saṅgataṁ syāt | niyuddhaṁ tair eva saha bāhu-yuddhaṁ tena śramaḥ śrī-gaṇḍādi-viṣayaka-mauktika-sundara-prasveda-kaṇikodayādi-karaḥ, tena karśito durbala iva anena sakhīnām api tādṛśaṁ balavattvaṁ sūcitam | tathā cāgame—gopaiḥ samāna-guṇa-śīla-vayo-vilāsa-veṣaiḥ [kra.dī. 3.21] iti | tad evam api teṣāṁ svayam asura-māraṇādau yad apravṛttis tatredaṁ paśyāmaḥ sarvasya vrajasya tenāpi guṇena śrī-kṛṣṇaika-sukhatārtha-līlā-śaktir eva teṣām udyamaṁ stambhayatīti | gopa-iti te kiñcid-ajyeṣṭhā jñeyāḥ | tatra upabarhaṇa-racanaṁ ca tat-sukha-lābhāyaiva tena tat-kṛtaṁ | kiṁ vā, racitasyāpi tenaiva tad-arthaṁ tyāgo jñeyaḥ ||17||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : talpeṣv iti | bahutvaṁ tatra tatrānekena prakāreṇeti dyotakaṁ mahātmanaḥ mahātmānaḥ parama-bhāgyavantaḥ supāṁ supo bhavanti ity upasaṅkhyānena ||17||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ), viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : niyuddhaṁ sakhibhiḥ saha bhuja-yuddhaṁ tena śramaḥ kapolādi-gata-mauktika-sundara-sveda-kaṇādi-karaḥ, tena karśitaḥ kṛśavat pratītaḥ, tena teṣāṁ ca tat-samaṁ vikramatvaṁ sūcitaṁ—gopaiḥ samāna-guṇa-śīla-vayo-vilāsa-veṣaiḥ ity āgamokteś ca śramatvena bhṛtya-sevābhilāṣād uditas tat-sevā rucir eva tasyāḥ aṣṭādaśa-doṣa-viraha-smaraṇāt ||17||

...

|| 10.15.18 ||

pāda-saṁvāhanaṁ cakruḥ kecit tasya mahātmanaḥ |

apare hata-pāpmāno vyajanaiḥ samavījayan ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : vyajanaiḥ pallavādi-nirmitaiḥ ||18||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : pāda-samvāhanaṁ caraṇa-mardanam | tasya hareḥ | mahātmanaḥ brahmādi-pūjyasya | kecit subalādayaḥ | apare śrīdāmādayaḥ | hata-pāpmāno janmāntarārjitānanta-puṇyāḥ | vyajanaiḥ vastra-patrādi-racitaiḥ ||18||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (dig-darṣinī) : [bṛ.bhā. 2.7.124] evaṁ niyuddha-krīḍā-pallava-śayyā-viracanotsaṅgopa-barhaṇādibhiḥ sevā-saubhāgyaṁ pradarśitam adhunā sahaja-parama-snehavantaḥ tat-pariśrama-darśanena vyākulās tad-apanodanāya vaiyagryeṇa bahudhā paricarantaḥ sneha-bharāvirbhāva-vivaśā babhūvur iti parama-prema-sampan-mahimā pratipādyate—pāda-saṁvāhanam [bhā.pu. 10.15.17] iti dvābhyām |

tasya śrī-bhagavataḥ pādayoḥ śrī-caraṇa-padmayoḥ saṁvāhanam kecit katicit mahātmana iti | mahātmāno mahāśayāḥ | yad vā, tasyaiva viśeṣaṇam aparicchinnasyāpi cakruḥ kecid iti bahutvaṁ samaya-parivartena yathā-kramaṁ śrī-caraṇāvayava-vibhāgaśo vā viśva-rūpābhiprāyeṇa sthāne sthāne tri-caturatayā vā anenaiva bālya-līlāyām api paramaiśvarya-prakaṭanābhiprāyeṇāgrya-ślokānte īśa-ceṣṭita [bhā.pu. 10.15.20] iti vakṣyate | yad vā, dustarkyānanta-śakter bhagavataḥ priya-sakha-varga-prītye śrī-nandanandana-rūpātiraskāreṇaiva tat-tad-alakṣya-paramāścarya-nijāparicchinnatā prakaṭanenaikatraiva pāda-saṁvāhana-sevāyāṁ yugapan mahā-yūtham api ghaṭate | ata evātra mahātma-śabda-prayogaḥ |

athavā prema-bharākulānāṁ saṁgharṣeṇa tatraiva bahavo'pi ghaṭer antataś ca tat-tal-līlāyām īśa-ceṣṭitatvaṁ sṛṣṭy-ādi-rūpād īśa-ceṣṭitād api tasyāḥ tasyāḥ jagan-manoharatara-mādhuryādi-māhātmya-viśeṣāpekṣayā jñeyam | hato'pagataḥ pāpmā yebhyas te, yathā hi—dhārmikāṇāṁ yad dharma-viparītaṁ tad eva pāpam | tathā bhaktānām api bhakti-viruddhaṁ sarvam eva pāpam | pāpma-śabdenātra tad eva gṛhītam | athavā śrī-bhagavateva śravaṇa-kīrtanādinā hato nāśito jagatām eva pāpmā yaiḥ, apare katicit-pallava-patra-barhādi-racitair vyajanaiḥ samyak śītala-mandatvādi-guṇa-yuktatayā avījayan ||18||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : kecid iti bahutvam | krameṇa parivṛtyā śrīmat-padābjayor bahubhiḥ saṁvāhanāt | kiṁ vā, bahula-śayyāsu pratyekaṁ tricaturatayā tatra pravṛtter abhiprāyeṇa | kiṁ vā, aiśvarya-viśeṣeṇaikasyāpi tasya bahutaraḥ saṁvāhana-sambhavāt | mahātmana ity ārṣam | mahātmānah parama-saubhāgyavanta ity arthaḥ | yad vā, tasyaiva viśeṣaṇam aparicchinnasyety arthaḥ | svayam ekenaiva śakti-viśeṣataḥ sarvāsv api śayyāsu vyāpakatvena śayanāt | hataḥ, nija-kīrty-ādinā śrī-bhagavataiva nāśitaḥ pāpmā jagatām api yais te tādṛśa-sevā-saubhāgya-brahmāt, vayaṁ tu pāpiṣṭhā eveti bhāvaḥ | evam idaṁ padaṁ pūrveṇa pareṇāpi yojyam | samyak manda-manda-madhura-bhramaṇādi-mudrayā avījayat ||18||

———————————————————————————————————————
