jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tasyāvasarasya yogyāni tasya śrī-bhagavato'pi sadṛśāni vā bhītānīti śeṣaḥ | viśeṣam apy āha—manojñāni cittākarṣaṇāni vicitrādbhuta-svara-tālādi-mayatvāt śanair iti | viśrāmāvasara-yogyatvād uttama-gānam udātvāc ca sneha-klinadhītvasyātraivoktir gāna-svabhāvatas tat-prākaṭya viśeṣābhiprāyeṇa | yad vā, pada-dvayasyāsya sarvānte nirdeśāta pūrva-śloke vākya-dvayenāpi sambandho draṣṭavyaḥ ||18||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : tasya mahā-guṇa-gaṇasyeti hataḥ tādṛśa-tat-sevāntarāya-rūpaḥ pāpmā yair ity ātmānam adhikṣipati teṣāṁ nitya-tādṛśatve’py ayam ātmāpahata-pāpmetivat tat-prayogaḥ ||18||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ), viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : sevām āha—pādeti | hata-pāpmāno daharavan nitya-nivṛtta-doṣāḥ vyajanais tāla-vṛntair bāla-vyajanaiś ca ||18||

...

|| 10.15.19 ||

anye tad-anurūpāṇi manojñāni mahātmanaḥ |

gāyanti sma mahā-rāja sneha-klinna-dhiyaḥ śanaiḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : anye manaḥ-saukhyādayaḥ | tad-anurūpāṇi tayoḥ priyāṇi gītāni | snehena klinnā rāgavatī dhīr yāsāṁ te tathā ||19||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tasya avasarasya śrī-bhagavato’nurūpāṇi yogyāni gītānīti śeṣaḥ | mahātmana iti pūrvavat | yad vā, samudra-koṭi-gabhīrasyākṣobhyatarasyāpi asya manojñāni cittākarṣakāṇi vicitrādbhuta-svara-tālādimattvāc chrī-gopī-guṇādi-mayatvāc ca | he mahārāja ! iti | samrājo’pi bhavādṛśas tādṛśa-krīḍā-sukhaṁ na sidhyatīti bhāvaḥ | yad vā, mahārājo’tyanta-prakāśamānaḥ parama-śreṣṭho yaḥ sneha ity arthaḥ | tena klinna-dhiya ārdra-cittāḥ santaḥ | ata eva śanair bāṣpa-ruddhamāna-kaṇṭhatayā laghu laghu agāyan | sneha-klinna-dhītvasyātraivoktir gāna-svabhāvatvena tat-prākaṭya-viśeṣābhiprāyeṇa | yad vā, pada-dvayasyāsya sarvānte nirdeśāt pūrva-śloke vākya-dvayenāpi sambandho draṣṭavyaḥ ||19||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tasyāvasarasya yogyāni tasya śrī-bhagavato’pi sadṛśāni vā bhītānīti śeṣaḥ | viśeṣam api āha—manojñāni cittākarṣaṇāni vicitrādbhuta-svara-tālādimayatvāt śanair iti viśrāmāvasara-yogyatvād uttama-gānam udātvāc ca sneha-klinna-dhītvasya atraivoktir gāna-svabhāvatas tat-prākaṭya-viśeṣābhiprāyeṇa | yad vā, pada-dvayasyāsya sarvānte nirdeśāt pūrva-śloke vākya-dvayenāpi sambandho draṣṭavyaḥ ||19||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : tad-anurūpāṇi tad-avasarārhāṇi ||19||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ), viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : tad-anurūpāṇi yaśāṁsīti śeṣaḥ ||19||

...

|| 10.15.20 ||

evaṁ nigūḍhātma-gatiḥ sva-māyayā

gopātmajatvaṁ caritair viḍambayan |

reme ramā-lālita-pāda-pallavo

grāmyaiḥ samaṁ grāmyavad īśa-ceṣṭitaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : īśa-ceṣṭita iti | nigūḍha-svabhāvatve’py antarāntarā īśasyaiva ceṣṭitāni dṛśyante’smin saḥ ||20||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : evaṁ gopa-līlayā | nigūḍhātma-gatiḥ pracchann ātma-svarūpaḥ | grāmyair gopaiḥ grāmyo naipuṇya-varjitaḥ iti kośaḥ | īśac eṣṭitaḥ prāduṣkṛta-niścaryaḥ | antarāntarā madhye-madhye daitya-dalanādinā svakīya-svaratām api bodhayatīti bhāvaḥ ||19||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : evaṁ nigūḍhātma-gatir ity-ādi | caritaiś ceṣṭitair eva gopātmajatvaṁ viḍambayann anukurvan, na tu vastu-gatyā ramā-lālita-pāda-pallavo'pi grāmyaiḥ samaṁ grāmyavad īśa-ceṣṭitaḥ san reme | grāmyo jano grāmyaiḥ saha yathā ramate, tathā reme, na tu te'pi grāmyāḥ, svayam api na grāmyaḥ, yata īśa-ceṣṭitaḥ, idam eva īśa-ceṣṭitaṁ yasya ||20||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (dig-darṣinī) : [bṛ.bhā. 2.5.86] gopātmajatvaṁ nija-gopa-kumāra-līlām ity arthaḥ | caritaiḥ sva-ceṣṭitaiḥ | viḍambayan hetau śatṛṅ viḍambayituṁ, ilayor ekatvāt vilambayitum āśrayitum | māyayā nija-śakti-viśeṣeṇa nigūḍhā saṁvṛtā bahir na prakaṭitety arthaḥ | ātma-gatir nijaiśvaryaṁ yena tathā-bhūtaḥ san reme cikrīḍa | aiśvaryāprakāśanena gopātmaja-sadṛśa-līlayā laukikatvam aiśvaryaṁ ca tasyāṁ tasyām eva līlāyām antar-madhurima-viśeṣa-parama-kāṣṭhā-pradarśanād ūhyam | ata eva kaiścid grāmyaiḥ samaṁ yathā kaścid grāmyo ramate, tathaiva mahā-lakṣmī-lālita-pāda-pallavo'pi reme | evaṁ grāmya-dṛṣṭāntena laukikatvaṁ ramā-lālita-pāda-pallavatvena ca svabhāvād aiśvaryam api siddham | tathā īśa-ceṣṭita eva grāmyavad reme ity apy ūhyam | grāmyavat krīḍām api pūrvokta-prakāreṇa aiśvaryasyāpy antar-vṛtter iti dik ||84||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : anuktām apy anyaṁ tasmin dine kṛtāṁ gopa-līlām uddiśan tad-dina-vanya-krīḍām upasaṁharati—evam ity ukta-prakāreṇa | kiṁ vā, īdṛśaiś caritaiś ceṣṭitai ramā-lālita-pāda-pallavo’pi reme ratiṁ prāpeti mahā-lakṣmyā pādābja-lālana-sukhād api gopa-krīḍā-sukhasya māhātmyaṁ sūcitam | yad vā, ramayati sadā tam iti ramā śrī-rādhā-devī, tayā lālita-pāda-pallavaḥ sann iti divāraṇya-paribhramaṇa-śramāpanodanārtha-saṁvāhanādinā rater upakārakatvaṁ darśitam | kiṁ kurvan ? gopātmajatvaṁ viḍambayan prakaṭayann ity arthaḥ | yad vā, ḍalayor ekatvāt sthirī-kurvann ity arthaḥ | caritair ity asyātraiva vānvaya iti śrī-nandād eva jāto’smīti śrī-bhagavad-buddhim api bodhayati |

nanu, prakaṭa-tādṛśaiśvaryasya kathaṁ nāma tad-viḍambanaṁ sidhyet ? tatrāha—svasya māyayā māyākhya-śaktyā kṛpayā vā, nitarāṁ gūḍhā ācchāditā ātmano gatir ātmārāmatva-pūrṇa-kāmatvādi-lakṣaṇam aiśvaryaṁ yena saḥ | yad vā, nitarāṁ gūḍhātirahasyopaniṣadām apy agamyātma-gati-nija-mādhurī-viśeṣo yasya | ata eva kaiścid grāmyair bandhubhiḥ samaṁ kaścid grāmyaḥ samo bandhuḥ prabhur iveti parama-laukikatvam, tena gopa-krīḍā-mādhuryaṁ ca bodhitam |

nanu, tarhi sarvatrocyamānaṁ bhagavattā-prakaṭanaṁ kathaṁ tatra saṅgacchatām ? tatrāha—īśasya ceṣṭitaṁ yasya saḥ, tat-tac-caritānām eva lokātītatvād iti bhāvaḥ | iti laukikālaukikatvena bhagavattā viśeṣa-prakaṭanaṁ pūrvavat siddham eva |

athavā, nigūḍhātmano gatiḥ śrī-vasudeva-dvārā gokule gamanaṁ yena [saḥ] śrī-nandād evātmano janma-jñānāt tad-anurūpa-vyavahārāc ceti gopātmajatva-viḍambanasya mukhya-sādhanam uktam | īśaṁ sarvaiśvarya-yuktaṁ ceṣṭitam api yasya | yad vā, īśa-ceṣṭita aiśvarya-ceṣṭā-yukto’pi grāmyavat rema itīśaceṣṭitād api grāmyavac caritasya māhātmyaṁ sūcitam | anyat samānam ||20||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : anuktām apy anyāṁ gopa-līlām uddiśan tādṛśa-līlāyāś ca tad atipriyatvaṁ pratipādayann upasaṁharati—evam iti | svāvirbhāvāntare ramā-lālita-pāda-pallavo'pi evaṁ vṛndāvana-vihāra-prakāreṇa reme rati prāpa tad evam atra sakhaṁ tādṛśa-sukhasyāpy anādaraḥ sūcitaḥ | kiṁ kurvan caritair nandas tv ātmaja utpanna [bhā.pu. 10.5.1] ity-ādi-rūpair alaukikair laukikaṁ gopātmajatvaṁ viḍambayan hīnopamārūpaṁ kurvan alaukikaṁ gopātmajatvam ātmani darśayann ity arthaḥ |

nanu, śrī-bhagavataḥ katham ātmajatvaṁ tatrāha, sve ye śrī-nanda-yaśodādayaḥ pitrādi-rūpās teṣāṁ māyayā kṛpayā vātsalyavaśatayety arthaḥ | ata eva nitarāṁ gūḍhā sarveṣām apy agamyā ātma-gatir-mahā-praṇayamaya-nija-mādhurī-viśeṣo yasya ata eva kaiścid-grāmyair bandhubhiḥ samaṁ kaścid grāmyo bandhur iveti ātmajavat sakhye'pi tādṛśopamatvam iti bhāvaḥ | nanu, tarhi sarvatrocyamānaṁ śrī-bhagavattā-prakaṭanaṁ tatra kathaṁ saṅgacchatāṁ tatrāha—īśaṁ sarvaiśvarya-yuktaṁ ceṣṭitaṁ yasya tal-līlā-śaktir eva tādṛśī satī śrī-bhagavad anusaṁhitāpi sarva sampādayatīty arthaḥ ||19||

prīti-sandarbhaḥ (151) : tatra śrī-vigraha-ceṣṭā dvividhāḥ | aiśvaryamayyo mādhurya-mayyaś ceti | tatra nija-jana-premamayatvān mādhurya-mayya eva ramaṇādhikye hetavaḥ | yathaiva parama-vismaya-harṣābhyām āha—evaṁ nigūḍhātma-gatiḥ ity-ādi |

śrī-nārāyaṇādi-rūpeṣu svāvirbhāveṣu ramā-lālita-pāda-pallavo’pi sveṣu alaukikeṣv api vraja-vāsiṣu nirīkṣya tad-vapur alam ambare carat [bhā.pu. 10.18.27] ity-ādau haladhara īṣad atra sat iti nyāya-labdhena tal-līlā-mādhurya-viśeṣāveśena laukikavad vyavaharatsu yā māyā kṛpā, sādhavo hṛdayaṁ mahyaṁ [bhā.pu. 9.4.68] ity-ādi-nyāyena tat-kṛtaikya-vyavahāraḥ, tayā nigūḍhātma-gatis tirohita-pāramaiśvarya-sthitiḥ san laukikaṁ yad gopātmajatvaṁ tad eva alaukika-gopātmajamayaiś caritair viḍambayan anukurvan reme svayam api ratim uvāha | atas tādṛśa-ramaṇeṣu yathā tad-icchā, na tathā ramā-lālita-pāda-pallavatve’pīti darśitam |

ramaṇam eva darśayati | yathādhunāpi grāmyair bālakaiḥ samaṁ kaścid grāmādhipa-bālako ramate tadvat | tat-tal-līlā-pradhāna eva ramate, na tv aiśvarya-pradhāna ity arthaḥ | dṛśyate ca tat-tal-līlāveśaḥ--sa jāta-kopa-sphuritāruṇādharaḥ [bhā.pu. 10.9.6] ity-ādau, raho’pi jāta-tādṛśa-bhāvāt | tān vīkṣya kṛṣṇaḥ [bhā.pu. 10.12.27] ity-ādau bālānāṁ sva-karāpacyutatā-jātānutāpād diṣṭa-kṛtatva-mananāc ca | ata eva tasya tat-tal-līlāsu lokānusāri yad yad buddhi-karma-sauṣṭhavaṁ, tat tat suṣṭhu munibhir api sa-camatkāraṁ varṇyate | yathoktaṁ śrī-śukena jarāsandha-yuddhānte—

sthity-udbhavāntaṁ bhuvana-trayasya yaḥ

samīhite’nanta-guṇaḥ sva-līlayā |

na tasya citraṁ para-pakṣa-nigrahas

tathāpi martyānuvidhasya varṇyate || [bhā.pu. 10.50.30] iti |

teṣu cariteṣu yad alaukikam āsīt, tad api tat-tal-līlā-rasa-mātrāsaktasya tasya svabhāva-siddhaiśvaryatvena līlākhyā śaktir eva svayaṁ sampāditavatīty āha—īśaṁ tat-tal-līlocita-sughaṭa-durghaṭa-sarvārtha-sādhakaṁ ceṣṭitaṁ līlaiva yasya sa iti | yathoktam—

athovāca hṛṣīkeśaṁ nāradaḥ prahasann iva |

yoga-māyodayaṁ vīkṣya mānuṣīm īyuṣo gatim || [bhā.pu. 10.69.37]

yathā ca—

yady evaṁ tarhi vyādehīty uktaḥ sa bhagavān hariḥ |

vyādattāvyāhataiśvaryaḥ krīḍā-manuja-bālakaḥ || [bhā.pu. 10.8.36]

sā tatra dadṛśe viśvam [bhā.pu. 10.8.37] iti | atra yadi satya-giras tarhi samakṣaṁ paśya me mukham [bhā.pu. 10.8.35] ity-antā tadīya-sarasa-kṛtaiva līlā pūrvam uktā | avyāhataiśvarya ity-ādikā tu tat-tal-līlā-śakti-kṛtaiva | sā ca śrī-vrajeśvaryā vātsalya-poṣike vismaya-śaṅke puṣṇāti | nāhaṁ bhakṣitavān amba [bhā.pu. 10.8.35] iti sambhrameṇa mithyaiva kṛṣṇa-vākyaṁ ca satyāpayati |

evaṁ śrī-dāmodara-līlāyāṁ yāvat tasya bandhanecchā na jātāsīt tāvad-rajju-paramparābhyas tasmin dvy-aṅgulādhikatva-prakāśaḥ | tad uktaṁ tad-dāmā [bhā.pu. 10.8.15] ity-ādinā | yadā tu mātṛ-śrameṇa tad-icchā jātā, tadā na tat-prakāśaḥ | tad uktaṁ—sva-mātuḥ svinna-gātrāyāḥ [bhā.pu. 10.9.18] ity-ādinā |

evaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa-kṛpā-dṛṣṭi-prabhāveṇaiva viṣamaya-mohāt sakhīnāṁ samuddharaṇaṁ tad-āveśenaiva dāvāgni-pāne cikīrṣita-mātre svayaṁ tan-nāśa ity-ādikaṁ jñeyam | krīḍā-manuja-bālaka iti krīḍayā līlayā manujā-bālaka-sthitiṁ prāpto’pīty arthaḥ | anyatra ca krīḍā-mānuṣa-rūpiṇaḥ [bhā.pu. 10.16.68] iti | evaṁ kārya-mānuṣaḥ [bhā.pu. 10.16.60] ity atrāpi kāryaṁ krīḍaiva | tasmāt sādhu vyākhyātam evaṁ nigūḍhātma-gatiḥ ity-ādi ||20||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : atha śrī-kṛṣṇasya līlā-vaicitryaṁ śrī-śukaḥ prapañcayati—evam ity-ādi | nigūḍhā aprakaṭā ātma-gatir ātmanas tattvaṁ sac-cid-ānandādi-lakṣaṇaḥ svabhāvo yasya sa tathā | ko’rthas tasminn eva vigrahe sann api sac-cid-ānanda-lakṣaṇo dharmo māyayā āvarikākhya-māyayā abhaktān prati na prakāśyate | te tu manujārbhaka evāyam iti jānanti iti gūḍha-śabdasyāyaṁ bhāvaḥ | ata āha—caritair gopārbhakatvaṁ viḍambayann anukurvan, caritair eva, na tu śrī-vigraheṇa, sa tu sac-cid-ānanda-lakṣaṇaḥ | ata āha—ramā ānandinī-śaktiḥ, tayā lālitaḥ pāda-pallavo yasya, gopa-bālakaiḥ saha ity arthaḥ | kaiḥ ka iva ? grāmyaiḥ samaṁ grāmyavat | grāmyair bālakaiḥ samaṁ grāmya-bālaka iva, vastutas tu na te’pi grāmyāḥ | ayaṁ tu naivety evāha—īśa-ceṣṭita īśeṣu īśa-prāyeṣu teṣu tathā-vidhais tair vā ceṣṭitaṁ ceṣṭā yasya | anyathā ramā-lālita-pāda-pallava ity anenaiveśvaratva-siddher īśa-ceṣṭita iti paunaruktyam | caritair gopārbhakatvaṁ viḍambayann ity anena sahāpi virodhaś ca syāt ||20-37||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : sva-yoga-māyayā āvṛtātmaiśvaryaḥ svayaṁ gopātmajo’pi caritair gopātmajatvaṁ bhūpāla-putratvaṁ viḍambayan tiraskurvan so’py evaṁ līlāṁ kartuṁ na jānātīti bhāvaḥ | gopā gopālake goṣṭhādhyakṣe pṛthvī-patāv api iti medinī | aiśvarya-dṛṣṭyā ramā-lālita-pāda-pallavo’pi tad-āvaraṇāt kaiścid grāmyaiḥ bandhubhiḥ saha kaścid grāmyo bandhur iva reme na kevalam āvṛtam eva tad aiśvaryam ity āha—asura-māraṇādi-prastāve īśam aiśvarya-mayaṁ ceṣṭitaṁ yasya saḥ ||20||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : upasaṁharati—evam iti | evaṁ vṛndāṭavī krīḍā-prakāreṇa reme ratiṁ prāpa tena vaikuṇṭha-vihāreṣu nedṛśī ratir iti sūcyate | sveṣu māyayā kṛpayā nitarāṁ gūḍhā mukurādhara-citravan-nara-ceṣṭāntar-avasthāpitā gatir viśuddha-nijaiśvaryaṁ yena saḥ | svebhyo viśuddhaṁ tan na roceteti bhāvāt | svayaṁ gopātmano’pi caritaiḥ pūtanādi-vadha-rūpair anyad gopātmajatvaṁ pṛthvīpati-putratvaṁ vā viḍambayan tiraskurvan tatra tatra tat-tal-leśasyāpy abhāvāt ramayā evaṁ vṛndāvanaṁ śrīmad ity-ādy adhigatayā śrī-gokula-mahā-lakṣmyā lalitā-vīpsitau pāda-pallavau yasya saḥ | yady api nijaiśvaryaṁ tenānigūhyate, tathāpi kvāpy asura-māraṇādau tad vyaktībhaved ity āha—īśam aiśvaryamayaṁ ceṣṭitaṁ yasya saḥ | svaiḥ sārdhaṁ vihāre tu tan nāstīty āha—grāmyair bandhubhiḥ samaṁ grāmyo bandhur yathā iti ||20||

...

|| 10.15.21 ||

śrīdāmā nāma gopālo rāma-keśavayoḥ sakhā |

subala-stoka-kṛṣṇādyā gopāḥ premṇedam abruvan ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : īśa-ceṣṭitatvam eva darśayitum āha—śrīdāmā iti | stoka-kṛṣṇaḥ kvacit ||21||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : śeṣa-kāryam āha—ādya-śabdāt vṛndabandhu-devabhadra-vinodārjuna-kāmakanda-prāṇabhānu-dṛṣṭibhānu-candrabhānu-jayabhānu-kalottāna-vīrasena-kīrtisindhu-kamalākara-sukhasāgara-sañjayādayo jñeyāḥ | śrīdāmnaḥ prāṅ-nirdeśaḥ sakhiṣu mukhyatvāt | stokakṛṣna iti caturakṣaram eva nāma jñeyam | tatra cedam eva lakṣyate bālasyāsya rūpaṁ kṛṣṇam anugacchad eva vartate | tasmān nāma ca tam anugamiṣyat praṇaya-viśeṣāya sampatsya iti vicārya samyag ullasatā tat pitrā tādṛśaṁ nāma prakāśitam iti | premṇeti na tu tāla-phala-lobhena, na tu duṣṭa-vadhārthaṁ veti, kiṁ tu priya-jana-prīti-viśeṣārtham eveti ||21||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ prathama-dina-krīḍayāpy anya-dina-krīḍām api upalakṣyādhunā kadācin nija-priya-jana-prītyai go-pālane kiñcid aiśvaryam api sākṣāt prakaṭitavān iti prasaṅgād āha—śrīdāmety ādinā | subalādyāś ca sakhāyaḥ | śrīdāmnaḥ prāg-nirdeśaḥ sakhyātiśayena śaiṣṭhyāt, tādṛśoktau tasya mukhyatvād vā, stoka-kṛṣṇa-nāmā kaścid-gopaḥ kṛṣṇa-sanāmatvena sakhitvādy artham | yad va, kṛṣṇāt prāg-jātasya tasya kṛṣṇeti nāma-karaṇāt, tathāpi sarvathā kṛṣṇato laghutvena stoka-kṛṣṇa iti khyāteḥ, ādi-śabdenāṁśvarjuna-viśala-vṛṣabhaujasvi-devaprastha-varūthapa-bhadrasenādaya ete mukhyatamā jñeyāḥ | premṇeti, na tu tāla-phala-lobhena, na ca duṣṭa-vadhārthaṁ vā, kintu priyajana-prīti-viśeṣārtham eva kiñcid iṣṭa-dravya-prārthana-lākṣaṇa-prema-svabhāvenaivety arthaḥ | yad vā, duṣṭa-nigrahādi-tat-kīrty anurāgeṇaiveti ||21||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ prathama-dina-krīḍayānya-dina-krīḍām apy upalakṣyādhunā kadācin nija-priya-jana-prītyai gopālane kiñcid aiśvaryam api sākṣāt prakaṭitam iti prasaṅgād āha—śrīdāmety-ādinā | tatra śrī-rāmasya sakheti nirdeśas tad-yuddhe tasyaiva prādhānyāt subalādyāś ca sakhāyaḥ śrīdāmnaḥ prāṅ-nirdeśaḥ sakhiṣu mukhyatvena braje tasminn anyasya kevala-kṛṣṇa-nāmnā pracāraṇāyām anyāyyatvāt stoka-kṛṣṇa iti caturakṣaram eva nāma jñeyam | tatra ced evam eva labhyate bālasyāsya rūpaṁ kṛṣṇam anugacchad eva vartate | tasmān-nāma ca tam anugamiṣyat tat-praṇava-viśeṣāya sampatsyata iti vicārya samyag-ullasatā tat-pitrā tādṛśa-nāma-prakāśitam iti premṇeti na tu tāla-phala-lobhena na ca duṣṭa-vadhārthaṁ kiṁ tu priya-jana-prīti-viśeṣārthaḥ, kiñcid iṣṭa-dravya-prārthana-lakṣaṇa-prema-svabhāvenaiva | yad vā, sva-vyājena śrī-kṛṣṇa-rāgayor eva tādṛśa-bhojana-sampādanecchāmayenaty arthaḥ | atra ca sakhyamaya-premṇeti labhyate sakhyaṁ ca sājātyenaiva bhavatīti mithaḥ prabhāvādi-jñānamayam eva yathoktaṁ taiḥ asmān kim atra grasitā niviṣṭānayaṁ tathā ced vakavad-vinaṅkhyati [bhā.pu. 10.12.14] iti vakṣyate cānantaraṁ rāma-rāmeti tato'rjunena tat-tad-yuddha-sāhāyya-prārthanāvat vīra-rasa-svābhāvika-sakhyamaya-premaivedam iti sthitam ||20||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : evaṁ gocāraṇa-līlā-sāmānya-prasaṅge kādācitkam apy āha—śrīdāmeti | stoka-kṛṣṇa ity eva nāma na tu stoketi viśeṣaṇaṁ tad-agre tan-nāma-karaṇānarhatvāt premṇā priya-janocita-prārthanā-mayena sva-vyājena tayor eva tādṛśa-bhojanecchā-mayena ca ||20.34||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : īśa-ceṣṭitatvam eva darśayitum āha—śrīdāmeti | premṇeti kṛṣṇa-rāmāveva sva-vyājena tāla-phalāni bhojayitum ity arthaḥ ||20||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : īśa-ceṣṭitam āha—śrīdāmeti | ayaṁ śrī-rādhāyāḥ pūrvajaḥ pīṭhamardaḥ sakhyā | premṇeti svavyājena tau tāla-phalānyāsvādayitum iti bhāvaḥ ||21||

...

|| 10.15.22 ||

rāma rāma mahā-bāho kṛṣṇa duṣṭa-nibarhaṇa |

ito’vidūre sumahad vanaṁ tālāli-saṅkulam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tālāli-saṅkulaṁ tāla-paṅktibhir vyāptam ||22-24||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : duṣṭānāṁ nibarhaṇa ! unmūlaka ! avidūre nikaṭe | itaḥ asmāt pradeśāt | he rāma ! iti ramase krīḍasīti tan-nāma-nirukter etām apy aśakyāṁ krīḍāṁ kuru | kiṁ vā, ramayasi krīḍayasi sukhayasi veti tayāsmān ramayeti bhāvaḥ | vīpsādare | mahāsattva iti tava kim apy aśakyaṁ nāstīti bhāvaḥ | he kṛṣṇa ! iti paramānanda-prada-svabhāvatvena sarvākarṣakatvād asmākam api sukhaṁ kartum arhasīti bhāvaḥ | duṣṭa-nibarhaṇa ! iti | vatsāsurādīnāṁ tasmāt sākṣād vadha-dṛṣṭeḥ | ito govardhanād avidūre krośa-catuṣṭayāntare "tārapharā-tālasī" iti-khyāta-pradeśa-gataṁ vanam |

asti tālavanaṁ nāma dhenukāsura-rakṣitam |

mathurā-paścime bhāge hy adūrād eka-yojanam || iti vārāhokteḥ |

paścime paścād-bhave bhāge nairṛta-koṇa iti vyākhyeyaṁ, tatraiva darśanāt | śleṣeṇa tālānām ali-varṇatvenāti-svādu-jātīyatvaṁ dhvanitam | he rāma ! tava mahā-sattva-parīkṣā | he kṛṣṇa tavāpi duṣṭa-nibarhaṇa-parīkṣā | adya-kartavyeti bhāvo'yaṁ tayoḥ sakhya-bhāvena baliṣṭhatva-jñānān na premṇi virudhyate, pratyuta vīra-rasotsāhoddīpanatvena saṁrudhyata eveti jñeyam ||21||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : he rāmeti ramase krīḍasīti niruktyā tāla-vane gatvā tāla-pātanādi-krīḍāṁ kuru | kiṁ vā, ramayasi krīḍayasi sukhayasi veti tayāsmān ramayeti bhāvaḥ—vīpsā ādare, sa ca tāla-phala-pātanādy artha-protsāhanārtham, ata-evādau tasya sambodhanam | he mahāsattveti parama-baliṣṭhasya tava kim apy aśakyaṁ nāstīti bhāvaḥ | kṛṣṇeti paramānanda-ghanatvād asmākam api sukhaṁ kartum arhasīti bhāvaḥ | he duṣṭa-nivarhaṇeti vatsāsurādīnāṁ tasmāt sākṣād-vadha-dṛṣṭeḥ, atas tāla-vana-rodhaka-dhetuka-vadhārtham api tāla-pātanādikaṁ yuktam eveti bhāvaḥ | evaṁ mahā-sattvatayā duṣṭa-nivarhaṇatvaṁ duṣṭa-nivarhaṇatayā ca mahā-sattvatvam iti tat-tad anyo’nyatvayor uktam, tena ca tau prati duṣṭato nija-śaṅkā svayam eva nirastā, itaḥ śrī-govardhanādity arthaḥ, prāyas tatraiva tadā go-cāraṇāt, tathā ca śrī-harivaṁśe tat-prasaṅga eva—

ājagmatus tau sahitau godhanaiḥ saha-gāminau |

giriṁ govardhanaṁ ramyaṁ vasudeva-sutāvubhau ||[vi.pu 13.2] iti |

avidūre’natidūre śrī-govardhana-pūrvataḥ krośa-catuṣṭayāntare vṛtteḥ, tathā ca śrī-vārāhe—

asti govardhanaṁ nāma kṣetraṁ parama-durlabham |

mathurā paścime bhāve adūrād yojana-dvayam ||[varā.pu. 406] iti

tathā—

asti tāla-vanaṁ nāma dhenukāsura-rakṣitam |

mathurā paścime bhāge adūrād eka-yojanam || iti |

sumahat-krośa-dvaya-vyāpitvāt, tad viśeṣaś ca śrī-hari-vaṁśe—

sa tu deśa samaḥ snigdhaḥ sumahān kṛṣṇa-mṛttikaḥ |

darbha-prāyaḥ sthalībhūto loṣṭra-pāṣāṇa-varjitaḥ ||[ha.vaṁ. 2.13.5] iti ||22||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) :

giri govardhanaṁ ramyaṁ vasudeva-sutāvubhau || [ha.vaṁ. 2.13.2] iti |

mathurā paścime bhāge adūrād yojana-dvayam ||[varā.pu. 406] iti,

darbha-prāyaḥ sthalī-bhūto loṣṭra-pāsāṇa-varjitaḥ || [ha.vaṁ. 2.13.5] ||22||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : ito govardhanād avidūre krośa-catuṣṭayāntare tārapharā iti tālasīti khyāta-pradeśa-gataṁ vanam—

asti tāla-vanaṁ nāma dhenukāsura-rakṣitam |

mathurā-paścime bhāge adūrād eka-yojanam || iti vārāhokteḥ |

paścime paścād-bhave bhāge iti nairṛta-koṇe iti vyākhyeyam, tatraiva tad-darśanāt | tālānām ālibhir vyāptaṁ, śleṣeṇa tālānām ali-varṇatvenātisvādu-jātīyatvaṁ dhvanitam | kintu dhenukena avaruddhāni vaśīkṛtānītyata eva he rāma ! tava mahā-sattva-parīkṣā he kṛṣṇa ! tathāpi duṣṭa-nibarhaṇatva-parīkṣādya kartavyeti bhāvo’yaṁ tayoḥ sakhya-bhāvena baliṣṭatva-jñānān na premṇā viruddhyate pratyuta vīra-rasotsāhoddīpanatvena saṁruddhyata evetijñeyam ||22||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : ito govardhanād avidūre yojanāntare—

asti tāla-vanaṁ nāma dhenukāsura-rakṣitam |

mathurā paścime bhāge adūrād eka-yojanam ||

iti vārāha-vākyāt paścime paścād-bhave bhāge nairṛta-koṇe ity arthaḥ | kintu dhenukena avaruddhānīti | he rāma ! tava mahā-satvaṁ he kṛṣṇa ! tava duṣṭa-nibarhaṇatvaṁ cādya parīkṣyam iti bhāvaḥ | na ca bhaya-sthāna-preraṇaṁ prema-kṣatikaraṁ tasya tat-sakhatvena tan-mahā-vikrama-vijñānāt pratyuta vīra-rasoddīpakatvena tad vaddhakam eva ||22.23||

...

|| 10.15.23 ||

phalāni tatra bhūrīṇi patanti patitāni ca |

santi kintv avaruddhāni dhenukena durātmanā ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tatra tālavane | yady aparyeva pākaṁ gatāni tarhi asmākaṁ kim ? iti cet tatrāha—patitānīti | tarhy ānīya bhujyatām iti cet, tatrāha—dhenukena iti | tarhi taṁ pṛṣṭvānīyatām iti ced āha—durātmanā iti | prārthito’pi durātmā na prasīdatīti bhāvaḥ ||22||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : patanti patitāni ceti pātana-prayāso nirastaḥ | tālānāṁ pakvānāṁ sasāṁ svayam eva patanād iti prāyo bhādra-māse krīḍeyam avagamyate, tasminn eva sarveṣāṁ tālānāṁ pākāt | evam iyaṁ līlā śrī-viṣṇu-purāṇādy uktānusāreṇa grīṣme kṛta-kāliya-damanānantaraṁ jñeyā |

nanu, tarhi yuṣmābhir gatvā tānyānīyantām ? tatrāhuḥ—avaruddhāny āvṛtāni rakṣitānīty arthaḥ |

nanu, tarhi prārthya nīyantām ? tatrāhuḥ—durātmaneti | yad va, kathaṁ tarhi parakīyāṇi dravyāṇi tāni grāhyāṇi ? tatrāhuḥ—durātmanā duṣṭa-daityenety arthaḥ | atas taṁ hatvā tad-dravyāṇi grahītuṁ yujyanta eveti bhāvaḥ | yad vā, vanyāni phalāni sāmānyataḥ sarveṣām upabhogyāni, tena kevalaṁ duṣṭa-svabhāvatayaivāvarudhyante, ato grāhyāṇye eveti bhāvaḥ ||23||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tataḥ kim ity āśaṅkya sābhilāṣam anuvadanti—phalānīti | patanti patitāni ceti nirbhara-svayam-pakvatvenātimadhuratvaṁ vṛthā naśvaratvaṁ ca vyañjitaṁ tathā pātana-prayāso'pi nirastaḥ iti | prāyo bhādra-māse krīḍeyaṁ tasminn eva sarveṣāṁ tālānāṁ pākāt evam iyaṁ līlā śrī-viṣṇupurāṇādy uktānusāreṇa grīṣma-kṛtakāliya-damanānantaraṁ jñeyaṁ | tatra viṣṇupurāṇe krama-prāptatvam eva kāraṇaṁ harivaṁśe tu damite sarpa-rāje tu kṛṣṇena yamunā-hrade ity ārabhya sā līlā varṇiteti spaṣṭam eva tad iti |

nanu, tahi yuṣmābhir gatvā tāni vanyatvena sādhāraṇāni | svayam ānīyantāṁ tatrāhuḥ avaruddhāni nanu tasya kiṁ taiḥ prārthyānīyantāṁ tatrāhuḥ—durātmaneti | atas taṁ hatvā tāni grahītuṁ yujyata eveti bhāvaḥ ||23||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : so’tivīryety-ādi tayoḥ parākramottejanam ||23||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : na vyākhyātam.

...

|| 10.15.24 ||

so’tivīryo’suro rāma he kṛṣṇa khara-rūpa-dhṛk |

ātma-tulya-balair anyair jñātibhir bahubhir vṛtaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tatra gatvā prasahya bhujyantām iti ced āha—ativīrya iti | ekākinaṁ taṁ niṣkāsya punaḥ sva-kāryaṁ kriyatām iti ced āha—ātma-tulya-balair iti ||24||

tatra tu gamanam evāsmākaṁ durghaṭam ity āha—tasmād dhenukāt | vanam iti śeṣaḥ | tvam eva tādṛśānāṁ hantety āhuḥ—he amitrahan śatru-nāśaka ||25||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tad bhītyā ca na kaiścid api tat-phalāni bhuktāni santīty uttejayanti-sa iti dvābhyām | ativīrye mahā-bala iti rāmaṁ praty uktir mātsarya-jananāya, gardabha-rūpa-dhṛg iti kṛṣṇaṁ pratyuktiḥ—priya-sakhasya rasika-śiromaṇes tasya hāsāya | yad vā, dvau praty eva dvayaṁ khara-rūpa-dhṛg apy ativīrya ity ato’vajñayānavahitābhyāṁ na bhāvyam iti bhāvaḥ | kiṁ ca, jñātibhir anyaiś ca mitrādibhiḥ, kimvānyair ity ukte prāptam udāsīnatvaṁ nirasyati—jñātibhir iti ||24||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tad bhītyā ca na kaiścid api tat-phalāni bhuktāni santīty uttejayanti-sa iti dvābhyām | ativīrye mahā-bala iti rāmaṁ praty uktir mātsarya-jananāya, gardabha-rūpa-dhṛg iti kṛṣṇaṁ pratyuktiḥ—priya-sakhasya rasika-śiromaṇes tasya hāsāya | yad vā, dvau praty eva dvayaṁ khara-rūpa-dhṛg apy ativīrya ity ato’vajñayānavahitābhyāṁ na bhāvyam iti bhāvaḥ | kiṁ ca, jñātibhir iti teṣām api tatrātyanta-sāhāyyaṁ darśitam ||24||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : āvayor agre tasya tadīyānāṁ cātivīryaṁ kha-puṣpāyamāṇaṁ bhaviṣyatīti cet, tarhi calanaṁ tatratyānnarān nirbhayān tān tāla-bhojinaś ca datta-yuṣmad āśiṣaḥ kurutam ity āhuḥ—tasmād iti |

nanu, kasyāṁ diśi tad vanaṁ tad brūtetyata āhuḥ, eṣa vai gandhaḥ bhādra-māsīya-prācya-samīraṇenānīta iti bhāvaḥ ||24.25||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : so’tivīrya iti tayor vikramottejanam ||24||

...

|| 10.15.25 ||

tasmāt kṛta-narāhārād bhītair nṛbhir amitra-han |

na sevyate paśu-gaṇaiḥ pakṣi-saṅghair vivarjitam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tatra tu gamanam evāsmākaṁ durghaṭam ity āha—tasmād dhenukāt | vanam iti śeṣaḥ | tvam eva tādṛśānāṁ hantety āhuḥ--he amitra-han śatru-nāśaka ||25||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : durātmatām evābhivyañjayanti—kṛta-narāhārād iti, nanu, tarhi para-snigdhā yūyaṁ bata tādṛśe karmaṇi kiṁ māṁ pravartayathety āśaṅkya śrī-kṛṣṇaṁ praty āha—he amitra-hann iti vakāghāsurādayo mahā-daityā bhavatā līlayā hatāḥ | eva gardabhaḥ katamo nāma syāt ? viśeṣataś ca duṣṭāmitra-ghātādinā tava kīrtir eva mahatī bhaviteti bhāvaḥ | ittham api snigdhatarāṇāṁ teṣāṁ yady api tatra tat-preraṇaṁ nopayujyate, tathāpi saparivāraṁ rāvaṇaṁ hatvā śrī-jānakīṁ śrī-raghunāntike netum icchantaṁ śrī-hanumantaṁ prati śrī-raghunāthena svayaṁ rāvaṇe hate sati tasya kīrtirmahatī bhavatīti sundara-kāṇḍe tasyā vākyavat tad vīryānubhavinām eṣām api tat-kīrti-viśeṣārthaṁ tad ghaṭetaiveti dik ||25||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tasmād iti sārddhakam durātmatām evābhivyañjayanti-kṛta-narāhārād iti | atra na sevyata ity raddhaṁ padyam anekatra, kiṁ tv ananvitaṁ cakārāt svāduni ca amukta-pūrvāṇām api saurabhyeṇaiva sākṣād ivāvedayanti, eṣa iti | vai niścaye gandho'vagṛhyate upalabhyata iti prāyo'smin deśe bhādramāse vṛṣṭy-anukūla-paurastyavātāt evaṁ phalānām utkṛṣṭatvaṁ nikaṭavartitvaṁ ca sūcitaṁ tad etat sarvaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa-rāmayor ajñātam iva matvā tair yajjñāpitaṁ tat tu tābhyāṁ narmaṇā tasyājñātasy eva kramaśaḥ pṛṣṭatvād iti jñeyam ||25 |

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : asmāsu kiṁ tasya balam iti cet tarhi gantavyam ity āha—tasmād iti | janopakāraś ca bhāvīti bhāvaḥ ||25||

...

|| 10.15.26 ||

vidyante’bhukta-pūrvāṇi phalāni surabhīṇi ca |

eṣa vai surabhir gandho viṣūcīno’vagṛhyate ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : viṣūcīnaḥ sarvataḥ prasṛtaḥ ||26-28||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : yad vā, kadāpy asmākaṁ lobho nāsīt, navanītādi-lubdhasyāsya sampakārd eva lobhita-cetaso vayaṁ jātā iti rāmaṁ pratyuktir gopānām ity āha—kṛṣṇasya gadhena lābhitaṁ ceto yeṣāṁ teṣāṁ tathā gandho gandhe hiṁsane ca leśa-samparkayor api iti kośāt | adanākāṅkṣā cet tarhi kālāntaraṁ neṣyāmi tatrāhuḥ—vāñcheti ||26||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : ca—kārāt svādūni ca, abhukta-pūrvāṇām api saurabhyaṁ sākṣād vedayanti—eṣa iti | vai niścaye, gandha’vagṛhyate upalabhyata iti prāyo’smin deśe bhādramāse vṛṣṭy anukūla-paurastyavātāt pūrvadig vātāt | evaṁ phalānām utkṛṣṭatvaṁ nikaṭavartitvaṁ ca sūcitam ||26||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī, jīva-gosvāmī krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : no’smabhyaṁ prayaccha yato’smākaṁ vāñchāsti ||26||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : nanu, kiyaddūre tad-vanaṁ tatrāha—eṣa vai iti | viṣūcīnaḥ sarvato visārī ||26||

...

|| 10.15.27 ||

prayaccha tāni naḥ kṛṣṇa gandha-lobhita-cetasām |

vāñchāsti mahatī rāma gamyatāṁ yadi rocate ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : evaṁ gamyatāṁ yadi rocata ity eva-prakāram | prabhū kṛṣṇa-baladevau ||27||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : ataḥ prayaccha, yato gandhena tālānāṁ saurabhyeṇa lobhita-cetasām—caturthy arthe ṣaṣṭhī | he kṛṣṇeti tvaṁ prati viśeṣeṇaivoktiḥ, parama-praiyatama eva tathā prārthanasya yogyatvāt | yad vā, kṛṣṇasya sarva-cittākarṣakasya gandhena sambandha-mātreṇa | lobhita-cetasām itīyaṁ rāmaṁ praty evoktiḥ, durlabhāny api tat samvandha-prabhāveṇa sulabhāni syur iti bhāvaḥ | śleṣeṇāsmākaṁ kadāpi kutrāpi lobho nāsīt, navanītādi-luvdhasyāsya samparkeṇaiva lobhita-cetasām iti narmaṇā nija-gāmi-durlabha prārthanā-doṣo nirastaḥ | ato mahatī vāñchā teṣv asmākaṁ cirād asti | gūḍho’yam arthaḥ—mayā vatsāsurādayaḥ sākṣād vahavo hatāḥ, agrajena tu na ko’pīti tasya kīrty arthaṁ dhenuko’yaṁ tena ghātayitavyaḥ, gardabhāc cāmusmād alpavalāt kācic chaṅkā nāsty eveti śrī-kṛṣṇasyecchā ciraṁ jñātāstīty ato gamyatām, tathāpasmat-preraṇayaiva duṣṭākrānta-sthāne na gantavyam, kintu nija-rucyaiva gamyatām ity āhuḥ—yadīti | anuja-snehākrānta-cittāya tasmai tatra gamanaṁ kadācid evārocetety āśaṅkayā viśeṣatas taṁ prati tathoktiḥ ||27||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : caturthy arthe ṣaṣṭhī kṛṣṇety-ādi muhur ubhaya-sambodhanam ativaiyagryaṁ sūcayati—kṛṣṇa-gandheti | śleṣeṇāsmākaṁ kadāpi lobho nā'sīt navanītādi-lubdhasyāsya samparkeṇaiva lobhita-cetasām iti śrī-rāmaṁ pratyevoktiḥ tena ca narmaṇā nija-durlabha-prārthana-doṣo nirasta eva muhuḥ prārthane'py anaṅgī kāram ivālākṣya sa-praṇaya-roṣam āhuḥ—vāñchāstīty-ādi ||27||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : prahasyety aho gardabho’py evaṁ balīty asambhāvyatvān mṛṣaiva vā brūtheti bhāvaḥ ||27.28||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : no’smabhyaṁ tāni prayaccha teṣu vāñchā asti ||27||

...

|| 10.15.28 ||

evaṁ suhṛd-vacaḥ śrutvā suhṛt-priya-cikīrṣayā |

prahasya jagmatur gopair vṛtau tālavanaṁ prabhū ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : evam gamyatāṁ yadi rocata ity eva-prakāram | prabhū kṛṣṇa-baladevau ||28||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : suhṛdāṁ śrīdāmādīnāṁ priyaṁ nija-kīrti-vistāraṇādi, tasya cikīrṣayā prakaṭam uccair hasitveti teṣāṁ sabhayokti-śravaṇāt | kiṁ vā, teṣāṁ śaṅkā-nivāraṇārtham, ata-evoktaṁ śrī-kapiladevena—hāsaṁ harer avanatakhilalokatīvra-śokāśrusāgara viśeṣaṇam atyudāram [bhā.pu 3.28.32] iti | tathā vṛtau parito veṣṭitau snehākulatayā duṣṭato bhayāt, sārdham iti pāṭhe’pi tathaivārthaḥ | prabhu teṣāṁ praharṣārthaṁ sva-sāmarthyaṁ darśayantāv iti | yad vā, teṣām īśvarāvatas teṣāṁ prīty arthaṁ tad yuktam eveti bhāvaḥ ||28||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : vṛtau sāhāyyāra parito veṣṭitī prabhū teṣāṁ praharṣārtha sva-sāmarthya darśayantau ||28||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ), viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : prahasyeti suhṛt-sukhāya gardabhān api prakṣyāva iti bhāvaḥ ||28.29||

...

|| 10.15.29 ||

balaḥ praviśya bāhubhyāṁ tālān samparikampayan |

phalāni pātayām āsa mataṅgaja ivaujasā ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : mataṅgajo hastī | ojasā avaṣṭaṁ bhena balena vā ojo’vaṣṭambha-balayoḥ iti yādavaḥ ||29||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : baladevasyādau praveśādikam agrajatvenāgrato gamanād anuja-snehād vā | kiṁ vā, tat-kīrty arthaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇasya tatra gauṇyāt | tālān iti bahutvam ekasya kampanenaiva saṅghaṭitānāṁ teṣāṁ bahūnāṁ kampāt | kiṁ vā, bāhubhyāṁ dvābhyām eva bahūnāṁ teṣāṁ yugapad-grahaṇāt | samyak paritaḥ kampayann iti mahā-bala-svabhāvena | kiṁ vā, caturdigvarti-sarva-phalānāṁ sarvadikṣu teṣāṁ pātanārtham ojasā balena vegena vā pātayāmāsa | matto gaja iveti līlayā pātane dṛṣṭāntaḥ ||29||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) baladevasyādau praveśādikam-ādau prārthitatvāt tataḥ śrī-kṛṣṇasyāpi tat-kīrtaye tatra gauṇāyamāṇatvāt tālāniti bahutvam ekasya kampanenaiva saṅghaṭṭitānāṁ teṣāṁ bahūnāṁ kampāt samyak paritaḥ kampayan iti vikīrya dūre patantu na tu śirasīty etad icchyā, kiṁ vā bāhubhyāṁ dvābhyām eva bahūnāṁ teṣāṁ yugapad grahaṇāt samyak paritaḥ kampayann iti mahā-bala-svabhāvena ||29||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ, viśvanātha-cakravartī sārārtha-darśiṇī, baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ vaiṣṇavānandinī) : na vyākhyātam.

...

|| 10.15.30 ||

phalānāṁ patatāṁ śabdaṁ niśamyāsura-rāsabhaḥ |

abhyadhāvat kṣiti-talaṁ sa-nagaṁ parikampayan ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : sa-nagaṁ sa-vṛkṣam ||30||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : asuraś cāsau rāsabhaḥ kharaḥ ||30||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : sa-parvataṁ kṣiti-talaṁ sarvā pṛthvīṁ paritaḥ kampayann iti tasya pūrvoktam ativīryatvaṁ darśitam ||30||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : sa-parvataṁ kṣiti-talaṁ sarvāṁ pṛthvīṁ paritaḥ kampayann iti tasya pūrvoktam ativīryatvaṁ darśitam ||30||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : sa-nagaṁ kula-parvatair api sahitam ||30||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : sa nagaṁ sa-parvatam ||30||

...

|| 10.15.31 ||

sametya tarasā pratyag dvābhyāṁ padbhyāṁ balaṁ balī |

nihatyorasi kā-śabdaṁ muñcan paryasarat khalaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tarasā sametya pratyag dvābhyāṁ padbhyāṁ balaṁ urasi nihatya balī khalaḥ kā-śabdaṁ muñcan ||31||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : ity artha iti | śīghra-gati-dyotanārthaṁ paryadhāvad iti tu chado-bhaṅga-bhayānnoktam iti ||31||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : urasi nitarāṁ hatvā prahṛtā, yato balī | kā-śabdaṁ kutsita-śabdaṁ gardabha-svabhāvatvāt | paryasarat punaḥ padbhyām eva tābhyāṁ hananāya śrī-balabhadrasyāgre paścād bhāgaṁ kṛtvāgrato’dhāvad ity arthaḥ | yataḥ kharo gardhabhasya tādṛśa-svabhāvād ity arthaḥ, khalaḥ iti pāṭhe duṣṭas tādṛśa-duśceṣṭatvāt ||31||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : nitarāṁ hatvā prahṛtya yato balī sādhāraṇa-devatāpekṣayā balavatvena balimānīty arthaḥ | kā-śabdaṁ kutsita-śabdam ārṣaḥ kādeśaḥ, paryāsarat punaḥ padbhayā hanane chidrānveṣaṇāya parito babhrāma yataḥ khalaḥ tādṛśa-duśceṣṭaḥ ||31||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : pratyag dvābhyāṁ paścimābhyāṁ kā-śabdam iti gardabha-śabdānukaraṇaṁ paryasarat parito’dhāvat ||31||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : pratyag dvābhyāṁ paścimābhyāṁ dvābhyām ity arthaḥ | kā śabdam iti rāsabha-śabdānukaraṇaṁ paryasarat pārato’dhāvat ||31||

...

|| 10.15.32 ||

punar āsādya saṁrabdha upakroṣṭā parāk sthitaḥ |

caraṇāv aparau rājan balāya prākṣipad ruṣā ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : upakroṣṭā gardabhaḥ | parāk pratimukhaḥ | aparau paścimau | balāya balaṁ hantum ||32||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : upakroṣṭeti | kroṣṭuvad atyucca-dhvanitvāt | rāsabhe gauṇī lakṣaṇā prāhiṇot cikṣepa ||31||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : upakroṣṭeti nikaṭe kā-śabda-mocanābhiprāyeṇātaḥ parāk vimukhaṁ sthitaḥ san punar etya | he rājann iti premṇāniṣṭa-śaṅkayāntaḥ sīdantaṁ rājānam āśvāsayati | yad va, rājamānakrodheṇāśeṣa-nija-tejaḥ-prakaṭanāt ||32||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : āsādya nikaṭībhūya upakroṣṭā nikaṭe kā-śabdaṁ kurvan parāk vimukha sthitaḥ san punar etya ||32||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : saṁrabdhaḥ ko'pi upakroṣṭā nikaṭa eva kā-śabdaṁ kurvan parāka pṛṣṭhī-kṛtya sthitaḥ ||32||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : saṁrabdhaḥ kruddhaḥ upa samīpe kroṣṭā kā-śabdaṁ muñcan parāk pṛṣṭhī-kṛtya sthitaḥ, aparau paścimau ||32||

...

|| 10.15.33 ||

sa taṁ gṛhītvā prapador bhrāmayitvaika-pāṇinā |

cikṣepa tṛṇa-rājāgre bhrāmaṇa-tyakta-jīvitam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tṛṇa-rājas tālaḥ ||33||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : sa balaḥ | tam daityam | prapadoḥ pādāgrayoḥ pādāgraṁ prapadam ity amaraḥ ||32||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : atha prakṣepa-kāla evety artha iti pāda-prahārasya vañcanaṁ jñeyam | pūrvaṁ tu nihatyeti tasyāvalatvaṁ svasya ca mahā-sāmarthyaṁ darśayitum avañcanāt hananārthaṁ prakṣiptayoḥ pādayor eva grahaṇena prahārāsiddheḥ | tṛṇa-rājety atisthūlātyuccatvābhiprāyeṇa, mahā-tāla iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | tad agre kṣepaṇaṁ gopānāṁ praharṣārtham, tasya laghutām ātmanaś ca mahāvaliṣṭhatāṁ darśayituṁ tathā līlayā tāla-pātanādy arthaṁ ca ||33||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : ekenaiva pāṇinā padayor gṛhītvā bhrāmayitvā ca prapador iti pāṭhas tv ārṣaḥ, pādayor agra-bhāga ity arthaḥ | pūrva tu tat prahārāṅgīkāraḥ svāna-vadhān aprakāśanena svasya tenākṣo'bhyatvaṁ prakhyāpayitum ||33||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : taṁ dhenukaṁ prapadoḥ padayor agra-bhāge ity arthaḥ | tṛṇa-rāja-stālaḥ || 32 ||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : sa balaḥ prapadoḥ pādayor agra-bhāge taṁ kharaṁ gṛhītvā tṛṇa-rājasya tālataror agre cikṣepa ||33.34||

...

|| 10.15.34 ||

tenāhato mahā-tālo vepamāno bṛhac-chirāḥ |

pārśva-sthaṁ kampayan bhagnaḥ sa cānyaṁ so’pi cāparam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tena kharāsura-dehena | bṛhacchirāḥ atituṅgaḥ | bhagnaḥ truṭyan | pārśvastham tālam ||34||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tena khara-dehena, sa pārśva-sthaś cānyaṁ sva-pārśvasthaṁ kampayan bhagna ity arthaḥ | evam agre’pi, ca-kārād anyo’py aparam ity evaṁ bahavo bahūn kampayanto bhagnā iti jñeyam ||34||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : ca-kārād aparo'pi param ity evaṁ bahavo bahūn kampayanto bhagnā iti jñeyam ||34||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ, viśvanātha-cakravartī sārārtha-darśiṇī, baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ vaiṣṇavānandinī) : na vyākhyātam.

...

|| 10.15.35 ||

balasya līlayotsṛṣṭa- khara-deha-hatāhatāḥ |

tālāś cakampire sarve mahā-vāteritā iva ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : utsṛṣṭena khara-dehena hatair āhatāḥ | cakampire akampanta ||35-37||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : hatair bhagnaiḥ | āhatā bhagnāḥ ||35||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ sannikṛṣṭā bhagnā dūrasthās tu kampitā ity āha—balasyeti mahā-sattveti [bhā.pu. 10.15.22] pūrvam uddiṣṭaḥ śrī-baladevasya balātiśayo darśitaḥ ||35||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ sannikṛṣṭā bhagnā dūrasthās tu kampitā ity āha—balasyeti ||35||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, jīva-gosvāmī bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : utsṛṣṭena khara-dehena hatais tālair āhatāḥ prāptāghātāḥ ||35.36||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : utsṛṣṭena kṣiptena khara-dehena itais tālair āhatā labdhāghātāḥ ||35||

...

|| 10.15.36 ||

naitac citraṁ bhagavati hy anante jagad-īśvare |

ota-protam idaṁ yasmiṁs tantuṣv aṅga yathā paṭaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : yasminn idaṁ viśvam otam ūrdhva-tantuṣu paṭa iva grathitam, protaṁ tiryak tantuṣu paṭavad eva saṅgrathitam | sarvato’syūtaṁ vartata ity arthaḥ ||36||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : etat tālādi-pātanam | yasmin bala | ity artha iti | sarvādhāratvena tasya tan nādbhutam iti bhāvaḥ | na tasya citraṁ parapakṣya nigrahas tathāpi martyānuvidhasya varṇyate [bhā.pu. 10.15.30] ity evaṁ vakṣyamāṇa-rītyā pratiyodhādy anukaraṇa-mātra-śakti-prakāriṇyā nara-lilayaivadaṁ kṛtam ity āścaryatvena varṇyate, na tv aiśvarya-līlayety āha—naitad iti | acitratve hetuḥ—bhagavati śaktyā samagraiśvaryādi-yukte’nante svarūpato’py aparichinne tathopādhi-saṁbandhenāpi jagad īśvare otaṁ protam ity-ādi-lakṣaṇe ca dṛṣṭānte’pi tantūnāṁ kāraṇatvena paṭād anyatvam | atra tādṛśa-bhagavattvādikaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇāṁśeṣu mukhyatvād uktaneveti bhāva iti toṣiṇī ||36||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : idaṁ ca tasya loke’dbhutam api tattva-dṛṣṭyā na citram ity āha—neti | etat līlayā dhenuka-māraṇa-tad dehotkṣepaṇādi-karma-sāmarthyaṁ vā, citram adbhutaṁ na bhavati | kutaḥ ? bhagavati sarvaiśvarya-yukte’to’nante’paricchinna-śaktyāvata-eva jagad-īśvare | bhagavattvādikam eva darśayati—otaḥ protam iti | idaṁ ca sarvaṁ śrī-bhagavad avatāratvena tad abhedād uktam | dṛṣṭānta sarvato’nusyūtatva-mātre, na cabheda-vivakṣayā | anyat tair vyākhyātam |

yad vā, bhagavatīty agrajatayā gaurava-viśeṣeṇa sarvajñatvābhiprāyeṇa vā, anante śeṣa-rūpe’to jagad-īśvare, śirasi pṛthvīdhāraṇena jagat-pālanāt tad evābhivyañjayati—idaṁ bhū-talam otaṁ protaṁ kāraṇa-kaṛyātmakam ity arthaḥ | yad vā, parvatādibhir dairghye vistāre ca sarvato niviḍī-bhūtaṁ yasmin yan mastakoparyasti | dṛṣṭānto naiviḍya-mātre ||36||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : idaṁ ca na tasya citraṁ para-pakṣa-nigrahas tathāpi martānuvidhasya varṇyate [bhā.pu. 10.50.29] ity evaṁ vakṣyamāṇa-rītyā prati-yoddhādy anurūpa-mātra-śakti-prakāśa-dhāriṇyā nara-līlayaiva kṛtam ity āścaryatvena varṇyate na tv aiśvarya-līlayety āha—naitad iti | acitratve hetuḥ bhagavati bhaktyā samagraiśvaryādi-yuktaṁ anante svarūpeṇāpy aparicchinne tathopādhi-sambandhenāpi jagad-īśvare otaṁ protam ity-ādi-lakṣaṇe ca dṛṣṭāntepi tantūnāṁ kāraṇatvena kāryāt paṭād anyatvam atra tādṛśa-bhagavattvādikaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇāṁśeṣu mukhyatvāt yuktam eveti bhāvaḥ ||36||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ : kiṁ ca, idaṁ ca na tasya citraṁ para-pakṣa-nigrahas tathāpi martyānuvidhasya varṇyate [bhā.pu. 10.50.29] iti vakṣyamāṇa-rītyā pratiyodhādy-anurūpa-mātra-śakti-prakāśa-kāryāt dhāriṇyā nara-līlayaiva kṛtam ity āśrayatvena varṇyate, naitad iti, dṛṣṭānte ’pi tantūnāṁ kāraṇatvena kāryāt paṭād anyatvam ||35.37||

———————————————————————————————————————

bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : viśvam otaṁ agra-tantuṣu paṭa iva grathitaṁ protaṁ tiryak tantuṣu paṭavad eva grathitaṁ sarvato'nusyūtaṁ vartata ity arthaḥ || 35-36 ||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : baladevasyedaṁ śauryann āścaryam ity āha—naitad iti | idaṁ viśvaṁ yasmin tat amūrdha-tantupu paṭavad grathitaṁ prātaṁ tiyak tantuṣu tad vad grathitaṁ sarvato’nusyūtam astīty arthaḥ ||36.37||

...

|| 10.15.37 ||

tataḥ kṛṣṇaṁ ca rāmaṁ ca jñātayo dhenukasya ye |

kroṣṭāro’bhyadravan sarve saṁrabdhā hata-bāndhavāḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : kroṣṭāraḥ svajāti-svanaṁ kurvāṇāḥ | tataḥ dhenuka-patanānantaram saṁrabdhāḥ kupitā saṁrabhaḥ kopavegayoḥ iti viśvaḥ | kṛṣṇām ity ādāv uktiḥ śrī-baladevasya parākrama-dṛṣṭyā bhayāt tat-tyāgena tad abhidravaṇāt | yad vā, agraja-premṇā svayam evāgre gamanāt rāmañceti paścād anuja-snehena tasyāpi tat-pārśve gamanāt | abhidravaṇe tu dvayor api prādhānyāc cakārau | kroṣṭāraḥ mahā-krośanaṁ kurvāṇāḥ hata-bāndhavā iti | śokenāpy atikrodhāc chakty atiśayaṁ kurvanta iti bhāvaḥ ||36||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : kṛṣṇam ity-ādāv uktiḥ śrī-baladevasya parākrama-dṛṣṭyā bhayāt tat-tyāgena prāk kṛṣṇaṁ pratyabhidravaṇāt, kimvāgraja-vātsalyena svayam agrato bhavanāt [sthitatvāt] rāmañceti paścād anuja-snehena rāmasyāpi tat-pārśve gamanāt | abhidravaṇe tu dvayor api prādhānyāc cakārau | kroṣṭāra iti mahā-krośanaṁ kurvāṇā hata-bāndhavā iti ca | śokenātikrodhān nija-śakty atiśayaṁ darśayanta iti bhāvaḥ ||37||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : kroṣṭāraḥ sva-jāti-svanaṃ kurvāṇāḥ | tataḥ dhenuka-patanānantaram saṁrabdhāḥ kupitā “saṃrabhaḥ kopa-vegayoḥ” iti viśvaḥ | kṛṣṇām ity-ādāv uktiḥ śrī-baladevasya parākrama-dṛṣṭyā bhayāt tat yāgena tad-abhidravaṇāt | yad vā, agraja-premṇā svayam evāgre gamanāta rāmañceti paścād anaja-snehena tasyāpi tat pārśe gamanāt | abhidravaṇe tu dvayor api prādhānyāc cakārau | kroṣṭāraḥ mahā-krośanaṃ kurvāṇāḥ hata-bāndhavā iti | śokenāpy atikrodhāc chakty atiśayaṃ kurvanta iti bhāvaḥ ||37||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ, viśvanātha-cakravartī sārārtha-darśiṇī, baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ vaiṣṇavānandinī) : na vyākhyātam.

...

|| 10.15.38 ||

tāṁs tān āpatataḥ kṛṣṇo rāmaś ca nṛpa līlayā |

gṛhīta-paścāc-caraṇān prāhiṇot tṛṇa-rājasu ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tās tān dhenuka-vāndhavān | he nṛpeti | nṛpā yathā nara-pālanārthaṁ duṣṭān hantīty evaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇenāpi tat-kṛtam iti bhāvaḥ | yd vā, nṛpasyeva līlayā rājāno’pi mṛgayā-krīḍā-kautukena mṛgān ghantīty anāyāsa eva tātparyāt ||37||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : he nṛpeti praharṣodayāt | yad vā, nṛpasyeva līlayā rājāno hi mṛgayayā krīḍā-kaukukena mṛgān ghnantīty anāyāse eva tātpryam | tṛṇa-rājasv ity ārṣam—tṛṇa-rājeṣu ||38||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : he nṛpeti praharṣodayāt | yad vā, nṛpasy eva līlayā rājāno hi mṛgayayā krīḍā-kautukena mṛgān ghnantīti anāyāsa eva tātparyam | tṛṇa-rājasv iti ārṣam—samāsānta-vidher anityatvāt ||38||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : daitya-dehair upalakṣitā bhūḥ phala-prakara-saṅkīrṇaṁ yathā syāt tathā rarāja nabhas talaṁ nabhaḥ-svarūpam ||38.39||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : he nr̥pa ||38||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : he nṛpa ||38||

...

|| 10.15.39 ||

phala-prakara-saṅkīrṇaṁ daitya-dehair gatāsubhiḥ |

rarāja bhūḥ sa-tālāgrair ghanair iva nabhas-talam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : ghanair nabha iva bhuvas talaṁ rarāja | tatra phala-prakara-saṅkīrṇam iti daitya-dehaiś ca sa-tālāgraiḥ saṅkīrṇam ity aruṇaś ceta-nīla-ghana-sādṛśya-saṁpādanam ||39.40||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : bhūr iti | talam ity anena vyavahite nāpi saṁbandhyate | ṣaṣṭy arthe prathamā bodhyety āśayena bhuvas talam iti svāmī | phalānāṁ prakareṇa samūhena saṅkīrṇaṁ militm | tatra bhūtale | daitya-dehair ity anena saṅkīrṇam ity asya samāsāntargatasyāpi bhīṣma-droṇa-pramukhataḥ sarveṣāṁ ca mahīkṣitām iti-vatsaṁbandhaḥ | yad vā, daitya-dehaiḥ saha phala-prakara-saṅkīrṇam iti saṁbadhaḥ | pūrṇopameyam upamānopameya-sadṛśya-dharmopamā-vācaka-padānāṁ caturṇām evopādānāt | yad va, nabhas talaṁ nabhaḥ-svarūpam talaṁ svarūpādhārayoḥ iti viśvaḥ ||38||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : phala-prakara-saṅkīrṇam iti | phala-prakara-saṅkīrṇaṁ yathā syāt tathā | bhūḥ rarāja | kaiḥ ? gatāsubhir dehaiḥ | kaiḥ kim iva ? ghanair nabhas talam iva ||38||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : gatāsubhir iti dehānām aspandanaṁ bodhayati, ata eva rarāja, bhūr bhūmi-rūpam, talaṁ tālānām adhodeśaḥ | kiṁ vā, bhūr ity avyayam—bhūr lokādivat ||39||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : gatāsubhir iti dehānām aspandanaṁ bodhayati, ata eva rarāja śrī-kṛṣṇa-pakṣīyāṇām ānanda-janakatvāt bhūḥ bhūmi-rūpaṁ talaṁ tālānām adho deśaḥ | kiṁ vā, bhūr ity avyayaṁ bhūr lokādivat | yad vā, supāṁsu-lug ity-ādinā ṅasaḥ subhāvaḥ | athavā satālāgrair daitya-dehe-rupa-lakṣitā bhūḥ phala-prakara-saṅkīṇaṁ yathā

syāt tayā rarāja nabhas talaṁ nabhaḥ-svarūpaṁ talaṁ svarūpādharayoḥ iti viśvaḥ ||39||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : atha tāla-vana-krīḍām āha—phala-prakarety-ādi | phala-prakara-saṅkīrṇaṁ yathā syāt tathā bhū rarāja | kaiḥ ? gatāsubhir daitya-dehaiḥ | kaiḥ kim iva ? ghanair nabhaḥ-sthalam iva ||39.52||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : phala-prakara-saṅkīrṇaṁ yathā syāt tathā bhū rarāja kaiḥ daitya-dehaiḥ nirbhinna-tālāgra-sahitaiḥ teṣāṁ svataḥ śyāmatvāt rudhirokṣitatvāc ca ghanaiḥ śyāma-raktair nabhas talam iva tala svarūpādhārayoḥ iti viśvaḥ ||38.39||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : phala-prakaraiḥ saṅkīrṇaṁ yathā syāt-tathā bhūḥ rarāja kair ity āha—daitya-dehair bhagna-tālāgra-sahitaiḥ teṣāṁ śyāmatvād-rudhira-niṣiktatvāc ca ghanaiḥ śyāma-raktair nabhas talam ākāśam iva tala svarūpādhārayoḥ iti viśvaḥ ||39.40||

...

|| 10.15.40 ||

tayos tat su-mahat karma niśāmya vibudhādayaḥ |

mumucuḥ puṣpa-varṣāṇi cakrur vādyāni tuṣṭuvuḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tayoḥ rāma-kṛsṇayoḥ | tad daitya-hanana-tāla-pātana-rūpam | nara-kṛtyāyogyatvān mahat | ādinā gndharvarṣayo grāhyāḥ ||39||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : gopānām eva prīty artham api tatra tat tad anyeṣāṁ jātam ity āha—tayor iti dvābhyām | sumahad iti devādi –bhayaṅkarasya mahā-baliṣṭhasya saparivārasya tasyāvahelayāśumāraṇāt | ādi-śabdād-vidyādharādayo maharyādayaś ca, krameṇa teṣāṁ tat-tat-karma jñeyam | vādyair gīta-nṛtyāny api jñeyāni, prāyo’nyo’nyaṁ teṣāṁ saṅgatvāt ||40||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : gopānām eva prīty artham api tatra tat tad anyeṣāṁ jātam ity āha—tayor iti dvābhyām | sumahad iti devādibhayaṅkarasya mahābaliṣṭhasya saparivārasya tasyāvahelayāśu-māraṇāt | ādi-śabdād-vidyā-dharādayo maharṣyād ayaś ca, krameṇa teṣāṁ tat tat karma jñeyam | vādyair jīta-nṛtyāny api jñeyāni, prāyo'nyo'nyaṁ teṣāṁ saṅgatatvāt ||40||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : yad vā, sādhāraṇa-manuṣyā evādan, na tu gopāḥ mṛta-gardabha-prasaṅge ghṛṇāṁ vidhāyeti bhāvaḥ ||40||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : manuṣyās tatratyāḥ pulindādaya eva na tu gopālāḥ ādan gardabha-raktokṣitatvena phaleṣu ghṛṇotpatteḥ ||40||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : na vyākhyātam.

...

|| 10.15.41 ||

atha tāla-phalāny ādan manuṣyā gata-sādhvasāḥ |

tṛṇaṁ ca paśavaś cerur hata-dhenuka-kānane ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : ādann abhakṣayan ||41||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : hata-dhenukasya kānane ceruḥ babhakṣuḥ | manuṣyās tatratyāḥ pulindādaya eva na tu gopālā ādan, gardabha-raktokṣitatvena phaleṣu ghṛṇotpatteḥ ||41||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : atha tad-vadhānantaraṁ manuṣyā gopā eva, gataṁ sādhvasaṁ rāma-kṛṣṇa-viṣayakāniṣṭa-śaṅkā yeṣām | ādan tatraivābhakṣayan | yad vā, tataḥ-prabhṛti gopādayaḥ sarva eva manuṣyā dhenukotpāta-gata-bhayā abhūkta-pūrvāṇi [bhṛpatitāni] tāny abhakṣayann ity arthaḥ | kiṁ vā, gopās tāla-phalāny ādan, manuṣyā anye’pi sarve gata-sādhvasā babhūvur iti | hata-dhenuketi manuṣyāṇām agamyatayā ghana-tṛṇādi-sañcaye tatra nirbhayatvena sarvatra sukhena tṛṇa-caraṇādikam abhipretam ||41||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : gopālā ity anuktvā manuṣyā ity uktes te tu mṛta-gardabha-prasaṅgena dhṛṇāṁ vidhāya nādan kiṁ tv anya eva manuṣyā ity arthaḥ | hata-dhenuka-kānana iti tṛṇa-bāhulyam api sūcitam ||41||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : manuṣyās tatratyāḥ pulindādaya eva na tu gopālāḥ ādana gardabha-rattokṣitatvena phaleṣu ghṛṇotpatteḥ ||41||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : mānuṣyās tatratyāḥ śavaśadayaḥ evādan na tu gopāḥ teṣāṁ rāsabha-rudhirā-niṣiktatvena tad-dine tad-asvīkārāt ||41|

...

|| 10.15.42 ||

kṛṣṇaḥ kamala-patrākṣaḥ puṇya-śravaṇa-kīrtanaḥ |

stuyamāno’nugair gopaiḥ sāgrajo vrajam āvrajat ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : kṛṣṇa iti vraja-sthānāṁ cittākarṣaṇaṁ, kamala-patrākṣa iti netra-nāsayor akarṣaṇam | puṇye dhanye karṇau yatas tādṛk kīrtanaṁ veṇu-gānaṁ yasyeti śrotrasyāpy ākarṣaṇaṁ sūcitam | anugaiḥ deva-rūpair ity arthaḥ ||42||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ prasaṅgād āpatitāṁ tālīvana-krīḍāṁ nirūpya pūrvaṁ prastutāṁ go-pālana-prathama-dina-kṛīḍām eva vadan vana-vihāraṁ samāpya vrajāntar vilāsam āha—kṛṣṇa iti ṣaḍbhiḥ | athavā, tasminn eva dine tālīvana-krīḍāpi sā draṣṭavya, eka-detyādy anuktyā krameṇa tad dina-krīḍām eva tat-kathanāt | tataś ca kāle’pi kārtika-śeṣe pakva-tālānāṁ samṛddhiḥ sarvartvāśraya-śrī-vṛnḍāvana-sambandhāt | puro bāto’pi śarad-vṛṣṭy artham etad deśa-svabhāvādghaṭata eveti dik | sāgrajaḥ kṛṣṇa iti prādhānyena tasya nirdeśaḥ prāyaḥ sarvatra tasyaiva mukhyatvāt, viśeṣataś ca vrajāgamane tasyātyantotsāhāt, vraja-janānanda-viśeṣotpādanāc ca, tathāgrato’bhigamiṣyantībhiḥ śrī-gopikābhiḥ saha saṅgamotsavārtham agre bhavanāc ca | kṛṣṇa ity abhipreta-sarva-cittākarṣakatvaṁ darśayan viśinaṣṭi—kamalety ādinā | kamala-patrākṣa iti saundaryam, puṇye śravaṇa-kīrtane yasyeti sarva-sad-guṇa-karmādi-māhātmyam | anena vraja-sthānaṁ tac chravaṇād eva virahārtyupaśamaḥ, stāvakānāṁ ca cittollāsaḥ sūcitaḥ | evaṁ vrajāgamane vraja-sthānāṁ praharṣārthaṁ viśeṣato rūpa-guṇādi-prakaṭanam abhipretam ato nijābhīṣṭa-siddheś ca, gopaiḥ stūyamāno rūpa-guṇādi-pradarśanena viśeṣataś ca tad dina-kṛta-dhenuka-vadhādi-varṇanenānugaiḥ paścād vartibhiḥ sadbhiḥ, śrī-kṛṣṇasya gopīnāṁ cānyo’nyaṁ samyag darśana-siddhy artham | yad vā, anugaiḥ sevakair gopaiś ca sahacarair iti premastavanaṁ bodhayati | yad vā, anugaiḥ śrīdāmādibhiḥ sakhya-svabhāvata eva gopaiś ca vrajasthair agrato’bhigatair anyair bāndhavais tac chravaṇāt | stūyamāna āvrajad iti gaṅgā-tīre niviśya kathayato’pi śrīvādarāyaṇer bhāva-viśeṣeṇātmanaḥ prāyo vrajasthatva sphurtteḥ ||42||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ prasaṅgena dināntarasya dhenuka-vadha-līlām api samāhatya prathama-gocāraṇadina-sandhyā-līlāpi yathāyuktam itthaṁ jñeyeti | tad dina-sandhyā-līlām āha ṣaḍbhiḥ—tatra sāmānya-vraja-jana-dṛśyamānatvena varṇayati—kṛṣṇa iti | kṛṣṇa ity abhipretaṁ sarva-cittākarṣatvaṁ darśayan viśinaṣṭi-kamalety-ādinā | kamala-patrākṣa iti saundaryaṁ śoṇac chavikoṇatayā vistīrṇatā-kīrṇatayā ca kaiśorāṁśa-vyaktir api puṇye śravaṇa-kīrtane yasya sa iti sarva-sad-guṇa-karmādi māhātmyam anena vraja-nātha-santac chravaṇād eva virahārty upaśamastāvakānāṁ ca cittollāsa sūcitaḥ evaṁ svarūpa-śobhā darśayitvā āvaraṇa-śobhām āha—stuyamāna ity-ādinā ||42||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : tatra sāmānya-vraja-jana-dṛśyamānatvena varṇayati—kṛṣṇa iti ||42||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : vanād-goṣṭha-praveśa-līlām āha tribhiḥ—kṛṣṇa iti | vraja-sthānāṁ cittasyākarṣaṇaṁ kamala-patrākṣa iti netra-nāsayoḥ puṇye dhanye śravaṇe karṇau yatas tathā-bhūtaṁ kīrtanaṁ veṇu-nānaṁ yasya sa iti śrotrasyāpy ākarṣaṇa dhvanitam ||42||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : vanād-vraja-praveśa-līlām āha—kṛṣṇa iti tribhiḥ ||42||

...

|| 10.15.43 ||

taṁ go-rajaś-churita-kuntala-baddha-barha-

vanya-prasūna-rucirekṣaṇa-cāru-hāsam |

veṇum kvaṇantam anugair upagīta-kīrtiṁ

gopyo didṛkṣita-dṛśo’bhyagaman sametāḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : go-rajobhiś churiteṣu kuntaleṣu baddhaṁ barhaṁ vanyāni prasūnāni ca yasya | ruciram īkṣaṇaṁ cāru-hāsaś ca yasya, taṁ ca taṁ ca | didṛkṣita-dṛśo didṛkṣitā darśanotkaṇṭhā-yuktā dṛśo yāsāṁ tāḥ ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : taṁ śrī-kṛṣṇam | go-rajobhiḥ churiteṣu vyāpteṣu kuntaleṣu keśa-pāśeṣu baddhaṁ barhaṁ mayūra-pakṣo yasya sa ity uttareṇa karma-dhārayaḥ | churitaṁ vyāpti-bhedayoḥ iti dharaṇiḥ | didṛkṣā jātā yāsāṁ tā didṛkṣitā dṛśaḥ svayam eva kartṛtvaṁ bhejuḥ karaṇatvaṁ tyaktveti dhvaniḥ | tāḥ dṛśo yāsāṁ iti ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tataś sāyantana-vanya-veṣa-vibhūṣitasya śrī-bhagavato gopīnāṁ cānyonya-sandarśana-sammānanānandam āha—tam iti dvābhyām | kuntalāḥ keśā alakā vā | īkṣaṇam avalokanam | evam upari madhye’dhaś ca sarvataḥ śrī-mukhasya saundaryam uktam | anyālaṅkārādi-sad-bhāve’pi tat-tan-mātrasyaiva varṇanaṁ sāyaṁ vanya-veśe tasya tasyaiva mukhyatvāt, tena tena śobhā-viśeṣodayāc ca | veṇu-kvaṇanaṁ svabhāvata eva, viśeṣataś ca nijāgamana-vijñāpanena tāsām ākarṣaṇārthaṁ praharṣaṇārthaṁ cā | ata eva upa sāmīpyena veṇu-kvaṇanaṁ copagīyate, tena vā gītā kīrtir dhenuka-ghātanādi-lakṣaṇā yasya tam | ayam api didṛkṣitatve hetur eko draṣṭavyaḥ | abhigamanārthaṁ ca tasya vrajāgamana-jñāna-nirdhāre—go-raja iti, veṇuṁ kvaṇantam iti, anugair upagīta-kīrtim iti ca hetu-trayam | gopya ullaṅghita-bālyās tat-priyāḥ, ata eva śrī-baladevo’pi dṛḍhaṁ vyāvartitas tābhiḥ sahānujasyāsaṅkoca-saṅgama-sukhārthaṁ kenāpi chalena tasya dūra-gamanāt |

abhigamane hetu-viśeṣaḥ—didṛkṣita-dṛśaḥ | astu tāvad ātmā mano vā, draṣṭuṁ paramotsukā dṛśo’pi yāsāṁ prathama-go-cāraṇena dūrāgamanato vividha-śaṅkotpattes tathā pūrvaṁ vatsa-pālanato’dhunā go-pālane kāla-vilambenāgamanāc ca sametā anyo’nyaṁ militāḥ satya aikamatyena bhaya-lajjādi-hāneḥ, anyo’nyaṁ sakhya-svabhāvād vā | yad vā, sva-sva-gṛhato’tivegena sarvāsām eva dhāvanād-yaugapadyena pathi | kiṁ vā, pūrvam eva virahārtyā sarvāsām ekatra saṅgataḥ ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : atha tad ullaṅghita-bālyaiḥ śrī-gopa-kumārī-viśeṣair api dṛśyamānatvena taṁ varṇayas teṣām anurāgotpattiṁ spaṣṭayati—tam iti dvābhyām | īkṣaṇam avalokanam | go-raja ity-ādinā | upari madhye tale ca śrī-mukha-śobhā darśitā anya-veṣa-sadbhāve'pi tat tan mātrasyaiva varṇanaṁ sāyaṁ vanād āgatatvena vaiśiṣṭyāt | venu-kvaṇanaṁ svabhāvata eva viśeṣataś ca tāsāṁ praharṣaṇārtham ākarṣaṇārthaṁ ca upetir āgamātra-gānamaya-veṇu-kvaṇanopagānatvena gītā kīrtir yasya tam atra sāgrajatānuktis tasya tatrānupayukta-prāyatvāt ata eva chalena vyavahitatvāt guru-jana-saṅgatau hi tasyāgrajatābhāva eva prabalate tasya vrajāgamananir dhāre gorajaś churiteti sūcita-go-raja uddhṛtir veṇu-kvaṇanam anugopa-gīta-kīrtitvam iti hetutr ayaṁ jñeyam abhigamane hetuḥ, didṛkṣitāḥ sajāta-didṛkṣā dṛśo yāsām iti, atra prathamato go-caraṇena dūra-gamanato vividha-śaṅkotpattiḥ tathā pūrvatodhunā gopālate kāla-vilaṁ vanenāgamanaṁ cotkaṇṭhā-vaiśiṣṭaye hetuḥ sametāḥ anyonyaṁ militāḥ aikamatyena sakhyāt tata eva bhaya-lajjādi-hāneś ca tac ca sva-sva-gṛhataḥ sarvāsām eva yugapad-dhāvanāc chrī-kṛṣṇādhvani vā pūrvam evāgatānām ucca-sthāna-viśeṣe vā jñeyam ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : ekena atha parama-tejasvitvena jhaṭiti tadvad ullaṅghita-bālyaiḥ śrī-gopa-kumārī-viśeṣair api dṛśyamānatvena varṇayaṁs teṣām anurāgotpattiṁ spaṣṭayati—tam iti dvābhyām ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : vraja-bālānāṁ viśeṣata ākarṣaṇam āha—taṁ gopyo’bhyagaman go-rajobhiś churiteṣu vyāpteṣu kuntaleṣu baddha-barhaṁ vanya-prasūnāni ca yasya ruciram īkṣaṇaṁ cāru-hāsaś ca yasya īkṣaṇayor hāso vā yasya taṁ didṛkṣitāḥ sañjāta-darśanecchā dṛśo yāsāṁ tā iti gopī-kartṛkaṁ lajjā bhava-dhenukaṁ varjanam amānayantyo dṛśaspadākaraṇatvaṁ parityajya svatantra-kartṛtvaṁ prāptā iti dhvaniḥ | tena ca prativeśināṁ śrotra-ghrāṇendriyāṇāṁ veṇu-sausvaryāṅga-saurabhya-sambal-lābham ālakṣya mātsaryeṇaiva sveṣāṁ raṅkatvam asahamānāḥ svāśraya-bhūtā gopīḥ parityajyeva sapatnī-bhavitum iva cāpalyāt svayam eva kṛṣṇa-pārśvaṁ calitā ity utprekṣā dhvanyate | sametā iti sarvā eva kula-vadhvaḥ sva-sva-gṛhān vihāya calanti "mām eva kiṁ tvaṁ vārayantī vadhiṣyasi" iti sva-sva-śvaśrūḥ pratyuttarayantya iti bhāvaḥ ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : kiśorīṇāṁ viśeṣataḥ samākarṣaṇam āha—tam iti | gopyas tam abhyagaman tad-darśanāya tad-āgama-pathastha-prāsāda-śikharāṇy ārohann ity arthaḥ | kīdṛśyaḥ ? didṛkṣitāḥ sañjātā didṛkṣā dṛśo yāsāṁ tā iti karaṇānām api dṛśāṁ didṛkṣāyāṁ kartṛtvaṁ nirdeśāt lajjā bhaya-hetukaṁ tan-nivāraṇaṁ na menire iti | tad-dṛśas tad-avaśyā iti bhāvaḥ | śrotra-ghrāṇayos tad-veṇu-nāda-tad-aṅga-saugandhya-lābhān mātsaryād iva dṛśāṁ tad-rūpa-lābha-pravṛttir iti sūcyate | sametā mitho militā iti śvaśrūṇāṁ na kṛteti vyajyate taṁ kīdṛśaṁ ? go-rajobhiḥ churiteṣu vyāpteṣu kuntaleṣu baddhaṁ barhaṁ vanyāni prasūnāni ca yasya rucirayor īkṣaṇayoś cārur hāso yasya, taṁ ca taṁ ca tam ||43||

...

|| 10.15.44 ||

pītvā mukunda-mukha-sāragham akṣi-bhṛṅgais

tāpaṁ jahur viraha-jaṁ vraja-yoṣito’hni |

tat-satkṛtiṁ samadhigamya viveśa goṣṭhaṁ

savrīḍa-hāsa-vinayaṁ yad apāṅga-mokṣam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : mukundasya mukha-padma-gataṁ sāraghaṁ madhu | ahni yo virahaḥ, tasmāj jātaṁ tāpam | sa ca tāsāṁ satkṛtiṁ pūjām | kāṁ sat-kṛtiṁ ? kathaṁ vā kṛtāṁ ? tām āha—sa-vrīḍena sa-lajjena hāsena vinayo yathā bhavati tathā yad etat | kiṁ tat ? apāṅga-mokṣaṁ kaṭākṣa-darśanam | tathā tāṁ sat-kṛtiṁ prāpyeti ||44.51||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : saraghābhiḥ sañcitaṁ sāragham, saraghā madhu-makṣikā iti halāyudhaḥ | madhu makarandaṁ, mukhaṁ lakṣaṇayā padmam āha | sāragha-padam api lakṣita-lakṣaṇayā makarandam āha ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tataś ca tasya sandarśanāmṛtena tāsāṁ viraha-jvaro nirasta ity āha—pītvā iti | muktir api kutsitā yasmād ānandāt, taṁ dadātīti mukunda iti tan-mukhasyāpi tādṛśatvam abhipretam | yad va, kukhe kundāni danta-rūpāṇi yasya, evam aruṇādhara-śyāma-sundara-śrī-mukhasya śobhā-viśeṣaḥ sūcitaḥ | tasya sāragham akṣi-bhṛṅgaiḥ pītvā iti tat-saundaryaṁ paramāsaktyā bhṛśaṁ sākṣād dṛṣṭvā ity arthaḥ | yady api bhṛṅgāḥ svayam eva madhu pivanti, tathāpi teṣāṁ karaṇatvam akṣṇāṁ cāñcalyādinā saundarya-viśeṣas tathā śrī-mukhāvayaveṣu tatas tato muhur dṛṣṭiś ca dhvanitā | virahajam iti tad-dine viśeṣato virahārtiḥ | tac ca didṛkṣita-dṛśaḥ ity atroktam eva | vrajasthā yoṣita iti tatraiva hetu-viśeṣaḥ | ahni iti rātrau viraha-tāpābhāvaḥ sūcitaḥ, tasyāṁ śrī-nanda-gṛhādau tat-sandarśanādi-siddheḥ | kiṁ vā, asya pareṇānvayaḥ | kiñcid dina-śeṣe goṣṭhāntaḥ praviveśa, rātrau samyag-darśanādya-sampatteḥ | tām abhigamana-śrī-mukha-prema-sandarśana-lakṣaṇāṁ tāsāṁ vā kiñcid upayanārpaṇa-pūrvāṁ gīti-rañjanādi lakṣaṇāṁ satkṛtim samyag adhikaṁ ca prāpya | śrī-bhagavatāpi tāḥ premṇā sammānitā ity āha—savrīḍa- iti | vinayo’tra cāpalyābhāvaḥ, na ca vinayato mukha-namratādinā tāsu sa-bhāva-dṛṣṭi-viśeṣa-hānir ity āha—yad iti | apāṅga-mokṣaṁ ca yathā syāt tathā | yad yasmād viveśa ||44||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tataś ca yad vṛttaṁ tad āha—pītveti | tair vyākhyātam | sva-mādhurī-pariveṣaṇena viyoga-duḥkhān muktiṁ dadātīti mukhe kundāni danta-rūpāṇi yasyeti vā | mukundaḥ tasya mukhābja-sāragham akṣi-bhṛṅga-svarūpaiḥ pītveti tat-saudarya-paramāsastvā netramātrātmikāsatyo bhṛśaṁ vīkṣyety arthaḥ | prakṛtyābhirūpa ity-ādi-vattṛtīyā | kiñ ca, bhṛṅga-rūpakeṇa śrī-mukhāvayaveṣu tatas tato muhur dṛṣṭiś ca yad-dhavanitā vraja-yoṣita iti samutkaṇṭhā-viśeṣaḥ | ahnīti rātrau vihara-tāpābhāvaḥ sūcitaḥ | tasyāṁ vrajendra-grahādau tat-sandarśanādi-siddheḥ sat-kṛtim āha—savīḍo hāsopatādṛśo'pi vinayaḥ rahasya-priya-janocita-vyavahāropatratādṛśaṁ yathā syāt tathā tad-rūpām ity arthaḥ | yad ity etat proktā tāṁ kiṁ rūpāṁ apāṅga-mokṣa-kaṭākṣa-darśana-rūpām ity arthaḥ | yad vā, yady āsāṁ apāṅga-mokṣa eva satkāropakaraṇatvena vidyate | yatra tādṛśaṁ yathā syāt tathā satkṛti samadhigamyeti | yad vā, yat sad-vṛtty-adhigamanaṁ savrīḍety-ādi-rūpaṁ apāṅgasya mokṣo yatra tad-rūpaṁ ca klībatvam ārṣaṁ vā kaṁsāt kṛtiṁ yaḥ apāṅga-mokṣa ity arthaḥ | tad-viśeṣaṇaṁ savīḍa-hāseti yad vā, vrajyoṣitaḥ pūrvoktās tad-viśeṣāḥ mukundasya sarva-duḥkha-mocakatvena tādṛśa-nāmnaḥ śrī-kṛṣṇasya mukha-sāraghaṁ mukha-kamalasya saundarya-rūpaṁ makarandam akṣi-bhṛṅgair akṣibhir eva bhṛṅgāraiḥ pāna-pātraḥ pītvā samāsvādya ahni yo virahas tena yas tāpas tad aprātijā tṛṣṇā tāṁ jahū rātri-javira-hatāpaṁ tu prāta-darśanena jahur eveti bhāvaḥ | yat yatraiva savīḍo hāsa-vinayau yatra tādṛśam apāṅga-mokṣaṁ kaṭākṣa-niḥkṣepa-rūpāṁ tat satkṛti tābhiḥ kṛtaṁ sammānaṁ samadhigamya matvā goṣṭhaṁ goṣṭhāntar-nija-gṛhaṁ viveśeti ||44||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : akṣīṇy eva bhṛṅgās tat-pāne svataḥ pravṛttatvāt taiḥ dvāra-bhūtaiḥ svayam api pītvā satkṛtim āha—savrīḍo hāso yatra tādṛśo vinaya-rahasya-priya-janocita-vyavahāro yatra etādṛśaṁ yathā syāt tayā yat syāt tad-rūpām ity arthaḥ | yad ity etat proktāṁ tāṁ kiṁ-rūpām apāṅga-mokṣāṁ kaṭākṣa-darśana-rūpām ity arthaḥ ||44||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : abhigamya kiṁ cakruḥ ? ity ata āha—pītvā iti | mukundasya mukhe sāraghaṁ smita-rūpaṁ madhu akṣi-bhṛṅgaiḥ pītvā na tv apāṅga-bhṛṅgaiḥ | pītvety anena kṛṣṇasyādṛṣṭa-gopīkasyānya-manaskasyaiva yat sāhajikaṁ smitaṁ tat tābhir niḥśaṅkatayā sampūrṇa-netrair eva pītam iti gamyate | tataś ca dvitīya-kṣaṇe kṛṣṇasya tatrāvadhāne sati harṣottho hāsas tāyāṁ yad evājani, tadaivodbhūtayā [vrīḍayā] lajjayā sa sampūrṇāvaloko hāsaś cāvṛtaḥ | vāma-kara-kṛtam avaguṇṭhanaṁ ca kiñcit saṁvṛttaṁ tat-tad-āvaraṇa-vyañjito vinayaś cābhūd ity etat sarva-mādhuryam eva kṛṣṇo’nubabhūvety āha—tat-satkṛtiṁ tādṛśāvalokana-rūpāṁ satkṛtiṁ tābhiḥ kṛtaṁ kiñcid upāyana-pradāna-rūpaṁ sammānanam ity arthaḥ | samadhigamya vidagdha-śiromaṇitvāt sa-rasāsvāda svīkṛtya goṣṭhaṁ viveśa | atra satkāra-samadhigama-kriyayoḥ krameṇa sa-vrīḍety-ādi-viśeṣaṇa-dvayaṁ | tena ca vrīḍayā sahito hāso vinayaś ca yatra, tat yathā syāt, tathā tāsāṁ sat-kṛtiṁ yataḥ prāpnuvataḥ apāṅgasya mokṣo yatra, tad yathā syāt, tathā samadhigamya goṣṭhaṁ viveśa ity arthaḥ | tābhiḥ kṛtā sa-vrīḍa-hāsa-vinayā tādṛśāvaloka-rūpā satkṛtiḥ, tasyādhigamaḥ kṛṣṇena tat prāpnuvad apāṅga-mokṣa-sahitaḥ kṛta iti phalitam | atra sampūrṇa-netrābhyāṁ darśane tāsāṁ lajjayā vimukhī-bhāvaḥ syāt, atas tat-kaṭākṣa-prāpty-artham eva kṛṣṇenāpāṅga-mokṣa iti jñeyam | arthaitad-vivaraṇaṁ tābhiḥ pratyekaṁ sva-nayanāñjanāv autsukyaṁ sañcāriṇā sva-parijanena ānīyāvalokana-kusumam arpitaṁ tathaiva svādhara-pallavāñjalau harṣa-sañcāriṇo'py ādīyārpitaṁ hāsa-kusumaṁ ca gṛhītvā | etad vastu-dvayam evāsmad-gṛhe tatra bhavate devam asti tat kṛpayā gṛhyatām iti yadaiva darśitaṁ, tadaiva tad upāyana-dvayam ānetuṁ kṛṣṇena sva-preṣyo’pāṅgo'ny ayujyata sa mahā-capalaḥ pūrvam eva tad advayaṁ tāsām antar-gṛha-gatam api corayitum udyato’taḥ kṛṣnena baddhvaiva sthāpita āsīt, tābhis tasminn upāyana-dvaye prakaṭīkṛtya ditsite sati, sa eva bandhān mocitaḥ san śara iva śīghraṁ gatvā tad yaiva grahītum ārabhata tat-kṣaṇa eva tāsāṁ koṣādhikāriṇyā sakhyā vrīḍayā prādurbhūya tad upagāyana-dvayam āvarītuṁ pravavṛte tataś ca tayor vigrahe pravṛtte sandhyarthyaṁ vinaye ca tāsāṁ parijane samāyāte sa ca balavān kṛṣṇa-preṣyo’pāṅgo vrīḍāvinayābhyāṁ sahitam eva sahāsāvalokanam upāyanam ākṛṣṇānīya kṛṣṇāya prādāt sa ca tatrikam atidurlabha-mahā-ratnam iva prāpya svahṛdaya-mandirābhyāntara eva sthāpayāmāseti kathā satkāra-vyāñjitopalabdhā yad vā vraja-yoṣitohnito’hni tāpaṁ jahuḥ, kāstā vraja-yoṣitaḥ ? yāsām apāṅga-mokṣaṁ tatāṁ prasiddhāṁ satkṛtiṁ samadhigamya goṣṭhaṁ viveśa kīdṛśaṁ savrīḍa-hāsa-vinayam atra yat padasyottara-vākya-gata-svān na tat padāpekṣā ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : abhigamya yac cakruḥ, tad āha—pītvā iti | mukundasya viraha-duḥkha-mukti-dātur mukhe aravinde smita-rūpaṁ yat sāraghaṁ madhu tad akṣi-rūpeṇa tu kaṭākṣa-rūpair bhṛṅgaiḥ pītvā iti svakarmakaṁ tad-avalokāt prākṛta-svābhāva-siddhaṁ tat smitaṁ tābhir niḥśaṅkaṁ nipītam iti bhāvaḥ | ahni yo virahaḥ, tena yas tāpaḥ tad-aprāptijā tṛṣṇā, taṁ jahur iti rātri-virahajaṁ tāpaṁ tu prātar darśanena jahur eveti bhāvaḥ | tatas tat-satkṛtiṁ kṛṣṇaḥ samādhigamya goṣṭhaṁ sva-bhavanaṁ viveśa | atra sa-vrīḍa- iti satkṛti-kriyāyā viśeṣaṇaṁ yad apāṅga- iti tu samadhigama-kriyāyā bodhyaṁ, tena ca sa-vrīḍa-hāso vinayaś ca yatra, tad yathā syāt, tathā tābhiḥ kṛtāṁ satkṛtiṁ yataḥ prāpnuvato’pāṅgasya mokṣo yatra tad yathā syāt, tathā samādhigamya goṣṭhaṁ viveśa ity arthaḥ | tābhiḥ kṛṣṇa-mukha-candre samavalokyamāne tatra kṛṣṇāvadhāne sati harṣeṇa hāsas tāsām abhudaita tadodbhūtayā lajjayā tat-samavaloka-hāsā va vṛtau mukha-paṅkajāni cāvaguṇṭhitānīti sauśīlyarūpo vinayaś cābhūt netrodharāñjali-dhṛtasya avaloka-smita-kusuma-gucchasya vinaya-saurabhyādig-rūpasyārpaṇaṁ kṛṣṇasya satkāraḥ | taṁ ca yad apāṅga-mokṣaṁ samadhigamya iti netrābhyāṁ vilokane lajjayā antas tirodadhyur iti netrās tenaiva tad-avalokanaṁ tat-kaṭākṣa-lābhārthakaṁ | tataś ca tāṁ satkṛtiṁ mahā-ratna-sampadam iva svahat-koṣe nidadhāno gṛhaṁ prāpa iti bhāvaḥ |

yad vā, vraja-yoṣito’hni tāpaṁ jahuḥ | tāḥ kāḥ ? tatrāha—yāsām apāṅga-mokṣaṁ prasiddhāṁ satkṛtiṁ samadhigamya goṣṭhaṁ viveśa | kīdṛśaṁ ? tatrāha—sa vrīḍa- iti | atra yac-chabdasya uttara-vākya-gatatvāt pūrvatra tac-chabdāpekṣā nāsti ||44||

...

|| 10.15.45 ||

tayor yaśodā-rohiṇyau putrayoḥ putra-vatsale |

yathā-kāmaṁ yathā-kālaṁ vyadhattāṁ paramāśiṣaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tayo rāma-kṛṣṇayoḥ | yathā-kāmaṁ tad icchānusāreṇety arthaḥ | paramāśiṣas tad vāñchitāni vyadhattām ||44||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ tāsām ānandam uktvā dvābhyāṁ mātror dvayor āha—tayor iti | putrayor iti dvayor api, dvau praty eva sva-putra-bhāvena sneha-bharaṁ bodhayati | tataś ca kācit kañcit prati kiñcid ity evaṁ dve eva te kramaśo yugapac ca dvayos tayor vyadhattām iti jñeyam | tathāpi parama-snigdhatvādinā vrajeśvaryā eva tatra mukhyatvam | śrī-rohiṇyāś ca sāhāyyena sāhitya-mātram iti | yathā-kālam iti vikāle sāyaṁ-pradoṣādau ca samaye vidheyānusāreṇety arthaḥ | yad vā, ādau nīrājanam, tato vanya-bhūṣaṇottāraṇam, tato’ṅgato go-dhuli-mārjanādikam ity evaṁ krameṇeti | yathā-kāmaṁ putrayoḥ svayor vecchānusāreṇa | paramotkṛṣṭā āśiṣa upabhogādīn sampāditavatyau | yad vā, paraṁ kevalaṁ tayor āśiṣa eva vyadhat tām—na tad-gṛha-kṛtyaṁ kim api cakratur ity arthaḥ, yataḥ putra-vatsale—anena prāg aṅkārohaṇāliṅgana-cumbanādikaṁ kuśala-praśna-sarvāṅga-nirīkṣaṇādikaṁ ca tathā stanya-sravārdra-vastratvādikam api sūcitam eva ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) evaṁ tāsām ānandaṁ dvābhyām uktvā mātror dvayor āha—tayor iti | putrayor iti | dvayor api dvau praty eva sva-putra bhādena lālana-bharaṁ bodhayati—yathā-kālam iti | śarad-ādau sāyaṁ pradoṣādau ca samaye vidheyānusāreṇety arthaḥ | yathā kāmaṁ putrayoḥ svayor vā icchānusāreṇa yathetyādikayor viparyayeṇa pāṭhaḥ | kvacit paramā utkṛṣṭā āśiṣaḥ upabhogān sampāditavatyau yataḥ putra-vatsale anena prāg aṅkāropaṇāliṅgana-cumbanādikaṁ kuśala-praśna-sarvāṅga-nirīkṣaṇādikañ ca sūcitam ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : paramāśiṣaḥ utkṛṣṭopabhogān tatra goṣṭha-madhye nijāvarodha-prāsāde ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : yathā-kāmaṁ putrayor vāñchitaṁ bhakṣyādikam anatikramya yathā-kālaṁ pradoṣādikaṁ bhojana-kālam anatikramya paramāśiṣo bhakṣya-paridheyādi-bhogāt ||44||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : yathā-kāmaṁ putra-vāñchām anatikramya yathā-kālaṁ pradoṣa-kālam anatikramya paramāśiṣo bhakṣya-paridheyādīn uttama-bhogān ||45||

...

|| 10.15.46 ||

gatādhvāna-śramau tatra majjanonmardanādibhiḥ |

nīvīṁ vasitvā rucirāṁ divya-srag-gandha-maṇḍitau ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————
