vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : adhvano’yam ādhvanaḥ sa cāsau śramaś cety ādhvana-śramaḥ | gata ādhvana-śramo yayoḥ, tau tathā ||46|| [atra viśvanāthaḥ]

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : āśīr-vidhānam eva prapañcayati—gata iti dvābhyām | tatra goṣṭha-madhye gṛhe vā gata ādhvānaḥ adhva-sambandhī vana-paribhramaṇa-bhavaḥ śramo yayoḥ | sa-visarga-pāṭhe’luk-samāsa ārṣaḥ, "gatādhvāhaḥ-śramau" iti puṇyāraṇya-śrīpāda-dhṛtaṁ pāṭhāntaraṁ cintyam | ādi-śabdena keśa-prasādhana-gātra-jala-mārjanādīni, sneha-bhara-sambhrameṇa kramollaṅghanān majjanasyādāv uktiḥ | yad vā, yathā-kālam ity-ādau kramasya sūcitatvād atrānirdiṣṭo’py asau boddhavya eva | yad vā, unmardanaṁ jala-mārjanam | ādi-śabdān nirmañchanādayaḥ | nīvīm iti lakṣaṇayā paridhāna-vastram uttarīyasya brahmacaryāt prāg-anapekṣatvāt, anulepādi-śobhā-vyavadhāyakatayā tasyāgrahaṇād vā | yad vā, ekatvenāpi nitya-saṁyuktatayā vastra-dvayam eva tad-upalakṣyam, bhūṣaṇānām anuktir vastrādīnām iva mlānatvābhāvena teṣām aparivartanāt | kiṁ vā, divya-srag-gandhābhyāṁ gṛhe tāny api divyāny upalakṣyante ||46||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : āśīr vidhānam eva prapañcayati—gateti yugmakena | gatādhvāneti na śramo aśramaḥ sa ceśvaratvāt śrīman-nara-līlāṅgīkāreṇa tasyābhāvas tv anaśramaḥ śrama evety arthaḥ | samprati kṣaṇaṁ viśrāma-līlāyāṁ vigatādhva-śramāv ity arthaḥ | ādi-śabdena keśa-prasādhana-jala-mārjanādīni snehabhara-saṁbhrameṇa kramollaṅghanān majjanasyādāv uktiḥ | yad vā, jalena dhūlim apasārya paścāt sugandhidravyeṇa tat nīvīm iti ajahal lakṣaṇayā paridhāna-vastraṁ ca uttarīyasya yajñopavītāt prāg anapakṣatvād anulepanādi-śobhā-vyavadhāyakatayā tasyāgrahaṇāc ca bhūṣaṇānām anuktiḥ vastrādīnām iva mlānatvābhāvena teṣāmaparivartanāt prātareva tadādhikyaucityācca jananībhyā supahṛtaṁ pariviṣṭaṁ prāśya prakarṣeṇa sukhenāśitvā upalālitau tāmbūlādi-mukha-vāsārpaṇa-sukha-goṣṭhī-śiroghrāṇādibhiḥ prati-lālitau tatra vraje tan-madhye avarodhānta-mahā-prāsāde tathā ca pādmottarakhaṇḍe vaṇitam—

tasmiśṁ ca bhavane śreṣṭhe ramye dīpair virājite |

ślakṣṇe vicitra-paryaṅke nānā-puṣpa-vivāsite ||

tasmin śete hariḥ kṛṣṇaḥ śeṣe nārāyaṇo yathā | iti |

vara-śayyāyāṁ divya-paryaṅkopari saṁviśya śrī-gātraṁ prasārya anena krīḍārthaṁ nidrāyāṁ kṣaṇaṁ vilambo bodhyate tad eva sūcayati, sukhaṁ yathā syād iti sakhi-dāsādi-kṛtopaskṛta-tāmbūla-samarpaṇa-cāmarāndolana-padābja-samvāhana-narma-goṣṭhī-gīta-gānādi-sukha-prakāreṇety arthaḥ ||45-46||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : gataḥ ādhvanaḥ adhva-sambandhi śramo yayoḥ, ṭer alopa ārṣaḥ | sa-visarga-pāṭhe gatādhva-śramāv ity arthaḥ | ṣaṣṭhy-aluk chāndasaḥ | samprati kṣaṇaṁ viśrama-līlāyā vigata-śramāv ity arthaḥ | evaṁ go-pālana-bhṛṅgādy-anukaraṇokta-prakāreṇa ||46-47||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : na śramo’śramaḥ sa ceśvaratvāt nara-līlayā tasyābhāvas tv aśramaḥ gato’dhvano’naśramaḥ eva yayos tau | nīvīṁ paridha-vastram ||46||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : gata- iti yugmakam | gato’dhvano mārga-hetukaḥ śramo yayos tau śramaḥ pūrvavat | nīvīṁ paridhāna-vastraṁ ||46||

...

|| 10.15.47 ||

janany-upahṛtaṁ prāśya svādv annam upalālitau |

saṁviśya vara-śayyāyāṁ sukhaṁ suṣupatur vraje ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : saṁviśya patitvā | yady api saṁ-pūrva-viśateḥ śayane vṛttir dṛśyate—svapnaḥ saṁveśa ity api ity amarokteḥ, tathāpy upasargāṇām anekārthatvād atra pattane’rthe | vara-śayyāyām mṛdu-vastrācchādita-paryaṅke ||46||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : jananībhyām upahṛtaṁ pariveṣitaṁ prāśya prāśana-rūpeṇa kiñcit kiñcid-bhuktvā | yad vā, prakarṣeṇāśitvā yatas tābhyām upahṛtamataḥ svādu | upalālitau tāmbulādi-mukha-vāsārpaṇa-sukha-goṣṭhī-mukha-cumbanādibhiḥ | vara-śayyāyāṁ divya-paryaṅkopari sambiśya śayitvā—anena krīḍārthaṁ nidrāyāṁ lakṣaṁ vilambo bodhyate | tad eva sūcayati—sukhaṁ yathā syād iti śrī-gopikābhir upaskṛta-tāmbula-samarpaṇa-cāmarāndolana-pādāvja-sambāhana-narma-goṣṭhī-gīta-gānādi-sukha-prakāreṇety arthaḥ | tat tad viśeṣaḥ śrī-bhāgavatāmṛtottara-khaṇḍe śrī-goloka-krīḍā-prasaṅgato jñeyaḥ |

vraje gavāṁ vāsa-madhye viviktatvāt priyatvāc ca, na ca prāsādādau dāsa-dāsyādi-bahula-loka-saṅkīrṇatayā svācchandyābhāvāt | yad va, vraja iti muhuḥ pūrvoktābhiprāyam eva, tataś ca mahā-prasādāntar iti jñeyam | vara-śayyāyām ity ukteḥ, anyathā pitroḥ sukhānutpatteś ca | suyupatur nidrā-līlāṁ bhejatuḥ | evam etat prathama-dina-krīḍānusāreṇa tālī-vana-krīḍāṁ vinā prāyo’nya-dina-krīḍāpy anumeyā | sā cāvaśiṣṭa-jīvanāvadhyalpatara-kālasya śrotuḥ śrī-parīkṣitas tat tad aśeṣa-kathanena tāt-kāla-gate satyanyasyāvaśyakathāsya vṛttasyāvakāśāsthiti-śaṅkayā na kila śrī-vādarāyaṇinā vistāritā | itthaṁ nāma sāyaṁ vrajāgamanādi-krīḍaiva viśeṣato varṇitā, na tu prātar vrajād-vana-gamana-vārtā, tadānīṁ śrī-yaśodādīnāṁ sarveṣāṁ vrajastha-janānāṁ śrī-bhagavad vicchedena śoka-vaikalyādi-vṛttasya parama-duḥkhāpādakatvād iti | tad viśeṣo’pekṣyaścet so’pi tata eva vijñeyaḥ ||47||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī)

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ, viśvanātha-cakravartī sārārtha-darśiṇī) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : vara-śayyāyāṁ dugdha-phena-mṛdulāyāṁ ratna-paryaṅkās tṛtāyāṁ suṣupatuḥ vicitra-līlā-vijñiṣṭaṁ svarūpānanda-yoga-nidrayā tu babhūvatur ity arthaḥ ||47||

...

|| 10.15.48 ||

evaṁ sa bhagavān kṛṣṇo vṛndāvana-caraḥ kvacit |

yayau rāmam ṛte rājan kālindīṁ sakhibhir vṛtaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : kvacit kadācit | rāmam ṛte balabhadraṁ vinā | eva kurvann iti śeṣaḥ ||47||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : itthaṁ nigūḍhaiśvaryāṁ laukikīṁ līlām uktvā tālī-vana-krīḍā-prasaṅgena prakaṭaiśvaryāntāṁ vakṣyan kāliya-damanaṁ vaktum ārabhate—evam ity-ādinā | evaṁ pūrvokta-prakāreṇa gopālana-bhṛṅgādi-vividhānukaraṇādi-vicitra-paugaṇḍa-līlayety arthaḥ | sa vraja-janaika-jīvana-bhūto bhagavān nijaiśvaryaṁ sākṣāt prakaṭayann ity arthaḥ | ataḥ kṛṣṇo jagac-cittākarṣakaḥ | yad vā, bhagavān sarvajño yataḥ kṛṣṇaḥ sarva-loka-hitārthaṁ svayam avatīrṇaḥ parameśvaraḥ kāliya-duṣṭatvādikaṁ jñātvā tasmād vṛndāvana-jīva-rakṣādy-arthaṁ tan-nigrahānugrahārthaṁ ca yayāv iti bhāvaḥ | vṛndāvane caran san kvacit kadācit ṣaṣṭha-varṣa-vayo’nte grīṣma-kāla ity arthaḥ | rāmaṁ vinety anyathā kāliya-hrada-praveśādināśeṣa-vraja-janānām anarthāpatteḥ | etac cāgre vyaktaṁ bhāvi |

evaṁ vraja-jana-ramaṇābhiprāyeṇa rāmam ity uktam | yad vā, parama-snigdhena tena svābhipreta-kāliya-hradāntar-nipātasya nivāraṇā-śaṅkayā dhairyārthaṁ sambodhayati | yad vā, rājamānaḥ san kiṁ vā, tad-dine kāliya-hrada-jala-vihārādy-artham upayuktālpa-bhūṣaṇa-svīkāreṇa sarvāṅga-śobhābhivyakteḥ | kāliya-damanārthaṁ tejo-viśeṣa-prakaṭanād vā | vṛto veṣṭitaḥ śrī-mukha-sandarśanādy-arthaṁ sarveṣām eva prema-sandarśanādy-arthaṁ sarveṣām eva prema-spardhayā parito’ntikāgamanād viśeṣataḥ snehena vrajeśvaryā anuśāsanāc ca | kaliṁ kali-kāla-doṣam anyo’nya-kalahaṁ ca dyaty avakhaṇḍayatīti kalindas tasya putrīm iti tasyā api tādṛśatvam abhipretam | ataḥ kāliya-niḥsāraṇārthaṁ tasyāṁ yānaṁ yuktam eveti bhāvaḥ ||48||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : atrādhyāya-samāptya-karaṇaṁ krama-prātām api pūrvatra duḥkhamayatayā tyaktāṁ kāliya-damana-līlāmanusmṛtya vaicityāt anusmṛtāyāñ ca | tasyām āveśād eva tāṁ prathama-bhāgato vaktum ārabdhām api tan mātram uktvādhyāyaḥ samāpayiṣyate | punaś ca viharaty api tatra svayaṁ bhagavati yamunāyāḥ sa doṣo nāpagata iti śrotṛṇām aparitoṣamāśaya vilokya dūṣitāṁ kṛṣṇām ity ekenaiva padyena sā līlā sūcayiṣyate | rāja-praśna-samuddīpyamānāveśād eva tu vistārayiṣyate atha tathaivopakrame te evam ity-ādinā | evaṁ pūrvokta-prakāreṇa gopālana-bhṛṅgādy anukaraṇādinety arthaḥ | sa vraja-janaka-jīvana-bhūto vṛndāvana-caro bhagavān kṛṣṇa iti bhagavattāyām api sārdra-cittas tad-viśeṣa smaranti, kvacit kadācit gocāraṇārambha-varṣasya nidāghe rāmaṁ vineti anyathā tena tādṛśa-sāhasa-niṣedhamaya-mātṛ-śikṣayā kāliya-hrada-praveśo nivāryeteti sambhāvya tasmin dina eva tatra gata iti bhāvaḥ | vṛto veṣṭitaḥ śrī-mukha-sandarśanādy artha | sarveṣām eva premasparddhayā parito 'ntikāgamanāt viśeṣataḥ snehena vrajeśvaryā anuśāsanāc ca ||48||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : evaṁ kārtika-gopāṣṭamī-dina-līlāṁ samāpya tad varṣīya-nidāgha-gatasya kasyacid dinasya līlām āha—evam iti | rāmam ṛte iti | janmarkṣa-śāntika-snānārthaṁ mātṛbhyāṁ tasya tad dine gṛha evopaveśitatvāt ||48||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : evaṁ kārttika-gopāṣṭamī-dina-līlāṁ samāpya tad-varṣīya-grīṣmartur gatasya kasyacid vāsarasya līlāmāhaivam ity-ādibhiḥ rāmam r̥te iti tasmin vāsare taj-janmarkṣa-yogāt tan-mātrā grha eva sthāpanāt ||48||

...

|| 10.15.49 ||

atha gāvaś ca gopāś ca nidāghātapa-pīḍitāḥ |

duṣṭaṁ jalaṁ papus tasyās tṛṣṇārtā viṣa-dūṣitam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : nidāgho grīṣmartus tat-saṁbadhyātapo ravikarabhavoṣṇatva-viśeṣas tena pīḍitā vyākulā ity arthaḥ atha kārtsnyena | atheti maṅgalānantarārambha-praśna-kārtsnyādhikāra-pratijñā-samuccayeṣu | duṣṭam vikṛti-janakam | tatra hetuḥ | viṣa-dūṣitam iti | tasyāḥ kālindyāḥ ||48||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : atha yān ānantaram eveti vicārādikaṁ nirastam | yad vā, bhinnopakrame’tha-śabdaḥ | mahārti-vārtopasatteḥ | ca-kārābhyām ubhayeṣām api prādhānyaṁ bodhyate | sarveṣām ekadaiva saha-pānāt | anyathā prāk-kṛta-pānānāṁ keṣāñcid anarthāpattyā paścād anyeṣāṁ tatra pravṛtty-asambhavāt | nanu, kṛṣṇaṁ vinā te kuto jalaṁ papuḥ ? tatrāha—nidāgheti | ata eva tṛṣārtā iti | duṣṭatve kāraṇam āha—viṣa-dūṣitam iti ||49||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : gāvaś ca gopāś ca athānantaram eva papur iti | gāvas tāvad anyatra cālitā api nidāghātapa-pīḍitāra satyas taj jalam ūrdhvāt yamunātīrādṛṣṭvā paśutayā tad-doṣājñānād eva druta-gatyā praviśya papuḥ | gopāś ca tat pīḍitā eva kiṁ tu prasiddha-tad-doṣa jānan tāsāṁ mṛtiṁ dṛṣṭvā śarīra-jihāsayā papur iti jñeyam | ata eva gopāś ceti tad-anantaram uktaṁ tāś ca te cāgragāminaḥ katicid eva jñeyāra śrī-kṛṣṇasya-kākitvena paścāt tyaktuṁ tair aśakyatvāt saṅkhyātīta-go-cāraṇāya samantāt bhāgaśa evaṁ gantuṁ teṣāṁ yogyatvāt paścād gāminā śrī-kṛṣṇena dṛśyamānānāṁ gavām api tat-pānāsambhavāt duṣṭatve kāraṇamāha—viṣa-dūṣitām iti ||49||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : duṣṭatva-kāraṇam āha—viṣa-dūṣitam iti ||49||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : gāva iti paścāt śanair āgacchantaṁ kṛṣṇam anapekṣya tṛṣārtatvāt druta-gāmiṇyaḥ tad anudrutā kecana gopāś ca ||49.50||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : gāva iti paścāt śanair āgacchantaṁ kṛṣṇam anapekṣya tṛṣṇārtatvāt druta-gāminyaḥ tad-anudrutāḥ kecana gopāś ca ||49||

...

|| 10.15.50 ||

viṣāmbhas tad upaspṛśya daivopahata-cetasaḥ |

nipetur vyasavaḥ sarve salilānte kurūdvaha ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tat duṣṭam | viśeṇa yutambho viṣāmbho madhya-pada-lopī samāptaḥ | daivopahata-cetasaḥ diṣṭan āśita-jñānāḥ | salilānte jala-nikaṭe | kṛṣṇa-kṛpayā kuru-vaṁśe’pi duryodhana-datta-viśeṇa bhīmo jalānte patitvā yathotthitas tatheme’py utthāsyantīti | bodhayituṁ sambodhayati mahā-muniḥ—kurūdvaheti ||50||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : etac ca śrī-kṛṣṇaṁ vinā jala-pānaṁ teṣāṁ nidāghātapa-pīḍitatvādikaṁ ca sarvaṁ kāliya-niḥsāraṇārthaṁ śrī-bhagavad-icchayaivety agre vyaktaṁ bhāvi | ata evāha—daivena devasya bhagavato mano-bhāvena kāliyasya daivena vā, upahataṁ ceto jñānaṁ yeṣāṁ te iti | ata evopaspṛśya kiñcid ācamya kiṁ vā, samīpe spṛṣṭvaiva | evaṁ tad-viśeṣa-vivakṣayokta-poṣa-nyāyena viṣāmbha ity ādeḥ punar-uktiḥ | kiṁ vā, nipātādau tad-eka-hetutā-vivakṣayā sarve go-gopāś ca, salilasya tasyānte samīpa eva | yad vā, svarūpe’nta-śabdaḥ salila evety arthaḥ | durviṣa-jala-pānena sadyas tatraiva nipatanād anyatra gantum aśakter vyasava eva | etad anyac ca vakṣyamāṇam aśeṣaṁ śrī-bhagavato’dbhuta-ceṣṭitatvam ity ūhyam | kṛṣṇenādbhuta-karmaṇe [bhā.pu. 10.16.35] ity ante vakṣyamāṇatvāt | tac ca tatraiva vistāryam | kurūdvaha he kuru-kula-nandaneti viṣīdantaṁ rājānaṁ sāntvayati ||50||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : etac ca sarva śrī-bhagavato bhāvi-līlā-viśeṣādhiṣṭhātṛ-śakti-vaibhavam evety āha—devo bhagavān tasyedaṁ devaṁ līlā-śakti-vaibhavaṁ | tena upahataṁ jñānaṁ yeṣāṁ ta iti tad uktaṁ īśac eṣṭita iti vakṣyate ca | kṛṣṇenādbhuta-karmaṇe [bhā.pu. 10.16.35]ity upaspṛśya kiñcit ācamya viṣāmbha iti punar uktis tad viśeṣa-vivakṣayā vyasava ivātra ca tādṛśa-daivam eva kāraṇam ||50||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : sarvānupapatti-samādhānārtham āha—daiveti | devo bhagavān tasyedaṁ bhāvi-līlā-śaktiḥ vaibhavaṁ daivaṁ vyasana iva atra ca tādṛśa-daivam eva kāraṇaṁ jñeyam ||50.51||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : devo bhagavāṁs tasyedaṁ daivaṁ līlā-śakti-vaibhavaṁ tenopahata-buddhayaḥ vyasava iti līlā-sauṣṭhavārthaṁ yoga-māyayaiva nityānām api teṣām asad ācchādya tathā darśanāt ||49.50||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : devasya kṛṣṇasya idaṁ daivaṁ tal līlā-śakti-vaibhavam | vāsava iti tad vaibhavenaiva tal līlā-siddhaye tad asūnāṁ mūrchita-tad-vidhānāt balabhadra-dhīmohavat ||50||

...

|| 10.15.51 ||

vīkṣya tān vai tathā-bhūtān kṛṣṇo yogeśvareśvaraḥ |

īkṣayāmṛta-varṣiṇyā sva-nāthān samajīvayat ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tān go-gopān | tathā-bhūtān gata-prāṇān | sva ātmaiva nāyo yeṣāṁ tān | mṛta-sañjīvanaṁ tu yogino’pi, sa tu yogeśvara iti nātrādbhutam iti bhāvaḥ | īkṣayā dṛṣṭyā ||50||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tān sarvān tathā-bhūtān tādṛg-avasthā-gatān | spaṣṭān uktir amaṅgalasya punar ativyañjanāyogyatvāt sneha-bharodayād vā | vai eva | vīkṣyaivettham avilambaṁ bodhayati | yataḥ sva-nāthān ananya-gatīn ata evāmṛta-varṣiṇyā dṛṣṭyā parama-snehāvalokanenety arthaḥ | anyatheccha-mātreṇāpi tat-siddheḥ | samyak glāni-śokādi-nirāsena yugapad evājīvayat sva-sthān akarot | yataḥ kṛṣṇo vraja-janaika-bandhuḥ para-duḥkha-kātaro vā | etac cādbhutaṁ na syād ity āha—yogeśvarāṇām apīśvara iti tadīya-śakty-aṁśa-gandhenaiva yogeśvarāṇām api tat-tac-chakter ity arthaḥ | yad vā, yogeśvarān api paripālayati kim uta tān vraja-janān iti bhāvaḥ ||51||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : vai eva īkṣaṇety avilābaṁ bodhayati, yataḥ sva-nāthān ananya-gatīn ata evāmṛta-varṣiṇyā prākṛtānāṁ prākṛtam amṛtam iva teṣāṁ tadīyānām ekaṁ jīvana-hetuṁ kāruṇyaṁ varṣituṁ śīlaṁ yasyāḥ | yad vā, amṛtaṁ tādṛśaṁ kāruṇyājalaṁ tad varṣiṇyā yathoktaṁ dvitīye yad vai vraje vraja-paśūn viṣa-toya-pītān pālāna-jīvayad anugraha-dṛṣṭi-vṛṣṭyā iti samyaka glāni-śokādi-nirāsena yugapad evājīvayat sva-sthān akarot tādṛśī ca śaktir na kṛtrimā kintu svābhāvikyety āha—yogeśvareśvara iti | yad upāsanā-viśeṣeṇaiva yogeśvarāṇām api tat-tac-chaktir ity arthaḥ ||51||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ, viśvanātha-cakravartī sārārtha-darśiṇī) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : sama-jivayat sutha-prāṇān vyadhāt ||51||

...

|| 10.15.52 ||

te sampratīta-smṛtayaḥ samutthāya jalāntikāt |

āsan su-vismitāḥ sarve vīkṣamāṇāḥ parasparam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) :

samprati sadya eva itā prāptā smṛtir yas te sampratīta-smṛtayaḥ |

yad vā samyak pratītā pratiprāptā smṛtir yas te tatheti ||52.53||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : ādya-samāse kliṣṭa-kalānāṁ manvānaḥ samāsāntaram āha—yad veti | smṛtir atra anena sarva-durgāṇi yūyam añjas tariṣyatha iti gargācārya-vacana-smaraṇam | kenāpīdam uktaṁ vacanaṁ tena sarvarjñātaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇenaiva punar jīvitā vayam iti ||52||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : suvismitāḥ suṣupter iva sarveṣām ekadaiva sadyaḥ samutthānāt, parasparaṁ vīkṣamāṇā ity atyanta-vismaya-svabhāvāt, sarveṣām aikam aty ārthaṁ vā ||52||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : suvismitāḥ suṣupter iva sarveṣām ekadaiva sadyaḥ samutthānāt, parasparaṁ vīkṣamāṇā ity atyanta-vismaya-svabhāvāt ||52||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : te jalāntikāt samutthāya suvismitā iti mṛtā eva vayaṁ kena jīvitāḥ kenāpy auṣadhena viṣaharam antreṇa vā parasparam iti sakhe ! kintv ametad rahasyaṁ jānāsīti pratyekaṁ praśnāt evaṁ mahā-sandehe pravartamāne bho vayasyā āṁ mayaivaitat kāraṇam anena sarva-durgāṇi yūyam añjas tariṣyatha iti gargācārya-vacana-smaraṇāt samyag avagatam iti kenāpy ukte sati sarve eva samyak prakāreṇa pratītā pratīta-viṣayīkṛtā smṛtis tadīyā yais tathā-bhūtā āsann ity anvaḥ ||52||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : te sarve susthāḥ kenāsmākaṁ svāsthyaṁ kṛtam iti parasparaṁ vīkṣamāṇāḥ sampratītāḥ samyak pratīti-viṣayī-kṛtāghāhivarya-smṛtir yais tādṛśāḥ santaḥ suvismitā āsan ||52||

...

|| 10.15.53 ||

anvamaṁsata tad rājan govindānugrahekṣitam |

pītvā viṣaṁ paretasya punar utthānam ātmanaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : viṣaṁ sa-viṣaṁ jalaṁ | viṣaṁ vidyate’treti matv-arthīyoc-pratyayaḥ | paretasya mṛtasyātmanaḥ yat-punar utthānaṁ tad-govindānugrahekṣitam | anvamaṁsata jñātavantaḥ | he rājann iti bhavād dṛśām etad uktam iti bhāvaḥ | yathā tvam ātmanośvatthāmāstra-hatasya punar ujjīvanaṁ kṛṣṇānugrahaṁ manuṣe tathā te’pīti bhāvaḥ ||53||

atra dhenuka-vadha-līlāyāṁ śrutim āha—samidra gardabhaṁ samṛṇa iti | arthaḥ—he indraṁ gardabhaṁ tad rūpaṁ dhenukaṁ samṛṇa mārayety arthaḥ ||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : govindasya gokulendrasyānugraheṇekṣitaṁ svīkṛtam | kiṁ vā, īkṣekṣaṇaṁ tat-kṛtam ity arthaḥ | yad vā, tad-eka-kāraṇatva-vivakṣayābhedopacāraḥ—īkṣitam īkṣaṇam evotthānam amanyata | he rājann iti bhavādṛśānāṁ bhāgavatottamānāṁ śrī-bhagavad-anugrahaṁ vinātma-maṅgale kāraṇāntarā-mananād yathā bhavat-prāṇane śrī-yudhiṣṭhirādīnām iti bhāvaḥ ||53||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : punar utthānaṁ govindānugrahekṣitaṁ tad-dhenukam anvamaṁsata ||53||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : anu anantaram aikamatyena vrajeṣṭa-deva-śrī-nārāyaṇenāviṣṭaya govindasya anugrahekṣitam eva kāraṇam amaṁsata yasyāt pītvā viṣam ity-ādi ||53||

iti sārārtha-darśinyāṁ harṣiṇyāṁ bhakta-cetasām |

daśame’smin pañca-daśaḥ saṅgataḥ saṅgata satām ||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : anu anantaram govindānugrahekṣitam tad amaṁsata kiṁ tad ity āhuḥ pītveti paretasya mūrcchita-prāṇasya ||53||

...

iti śrīmad-bhāgavate mahā-purāṇe brahma-sūtra-bhāṣye pāramahaṁsyaṁ saṁhitāyāṁ vaiyāsikyāṁ daśama-skandhe dhenuka-vadho nāma pañcadaśo’dhyāyaḥ |
